7/16/2026, 8:01:56 AM

Saya lanjut ke **Seri 3/14**. Fokusnya sekarang bukan syntax dulu, tapi memilih partition key yang benar dari query nyata, growth pattern, retention policy, dan risiko skew.

# Seri 3/14 - Partition Key Design: Time-based, Tenant-based, Composite Key, dan Skew Analysis

## 1. Tujuan Seri Ini

Seri ini menjawab pertanyaan:

> **Kolom apa yang seharusnya dipakai sebagai partition key?**

Ini keputusan paling mahal dalam PostgreSQL partitioning. Salah memilih key bisa membuat partitioning tidak berguna, bahkan memperburuk performa karena query tetap membaca banyak partition, planning time naik, index bertambah banyak, dan migrasi menjadi sulit.

PostgreSQL mendukung declarative partitioning dengan **range**, **list**, dan **hash**; manfaat performa utamanya muncul ketika planner/executor bisa melakukan **partition pruning**, yaitu mengabaikan partition yang tidak relevan dengan predicate query. Karena itu, partition key ideal adalah kolom yang sering muncul di `WHERE` clause query besar. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/ddl-partitioning.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 2. Mental Model

```text
Partition key bukan sekadar kolom teknis.
Partition key adalah boundary fisik untuk:
│
├─ Query pruning
│  └─ Partition mana yang bisa di-skip?
│
├─ Data lifecycle
│  └─ Data mana yang bisa di-archive/drop?
│
├─ Write distribution
│  └─ Apakah semua write menumpuk di satu partition?
│
├─ Constraint design
│  └─ Apakah primary key / unique key tetap valid?
│
├─ Operational maintenance
│  └─ Partition mana yang perlu vacuum/analyze/index/reindex?
│
└─ API contract
   └─ Apakah REST endpoint bisa mewajibkan filter partition key?
```

Kalau partition key tidak muncul di query aplikasi, PostgreSQL sulit melakukan pruning. Akibatnya query bisa tetap membaca banyak/semua partition.

---

## 3. Golden Rule Partition Key

```text
Pilih partition key dari kolom yang:
1. Sering muncul di WHERE clause query mahal
2. Selaras dengan lifecycle data
3. Stabil / jarang berubah
4. Distribusinya cukup sehat
5. Kompatibel dengan primary key, unique key, dan foreign key
6. Bisa dipaksa di API/service contract
```

PostgreSQL memiliki batasan penting: untuk membuat `PRIMARY KEY` atau `UNIQUE` constraint pada partitioned table, kolom constraint harus mencakup seluruh partition key. Alasannya, index unik pada tiap partition hanya bisa menjamin uniqueness di partition masing-masing; struktur partition harus ikut menjamin tidak ada duplicate lintas partition. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/ddl-partitioning.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 4. Kategori Partition Key

## 4.1 Time-based Partition Key

Contoh kolom:

| Kolom | Umum dipakai untuk |
|---|---|
| `created_at` | audit, event, outbox, inbox, history |
| `event_time` | event log, domain event, telemetry |
| `business_date` | settlement, transaction, finance, reporting |
| `completed_at` | workflow SLA, task history |
| `effective_date` | policy, rule, entitlement, regulatory state |

### Cocok jika

| Kondisi | Penilaian |
|---|---|
| Query sering pakai date range | Sangat cocok |
| Data append-heavy | Sangat cocok |
| Retention/archive by date | Sangat cocok |
| Query dominan by ID saja | Kurang cocok |
| Kolom waktu sering berubah | Tidak ideal |
| Banyak late-arriving data | Masih bisa, tapi perlu default/staging strategy |

### Contoh desain

```sql
CREATE TABLE audit_event (
    event_id    uuid        NOT NULL,
    tenant_id   uuid        NOT NULL,
    case_id     uuid        NOT NULL,
    event_type  text        NOT NULL,
    event_time  timestamptz NOT NULL,
    payload     jsonb       NOT NULL,
    created_at  timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),

    PRIMARY KEY (event_id, event_time)
) PARTITION BY RANGE (event_time);
```

Monthly partitions:

```sql
CREATE TABLE audit_event_p2026_01
PARTITION OF audit_event
FOR VALUES FROM ('2026-01-01 00:00:00+00')
             TO ('2026-02-01 00:00:00+00');

CREATE TABLE audit_event_p2026_02
PARTITION OF audit_event
FOR VALUES FROM ('2026-02-01 00:00:00+00')
             TO ('2026-03-01 00:00:00+00');
```

### Query yang ideal

```sql
SELECT
    event_id,
    tenant_id,
    case_id,
    event_type,
    event_time,
    payload
FROM audit_event
WHERE event_time >= :from_time
  AND event_time <  :to_time
  AND tenant_id = :tenant_id
ORDER BY event_time DESC
LIMIT :limit;
```

### Query yang buruk untuk partition by time

```sql
SELECT *
FROM audit_event
WHERE case_id = :case_id;
```

Karena tidak ada predicate `event_time`, query ini bisa membaca banyak partition.

---

## 4.2 Tenant-based Partition Key

Contoh kolom:

| Kolom | Use case |
|---|---|
| `tenant_id` | SaaS / multi-tenant platform |
| `organization_id` | enterprise org boundary |
| `customer_id` | customer-level isolation |
| `institution_id` | bank/regulator/institution data |
| `agency_id` | government/regulatory agency |

Tenant-based partitioning biasanya memakai **LIST** atau **HASH**.

### Pilihan model

| Model | Cocok jika |
|---|---|
| `LIST (tenant_id)` | Tenant sedikit, besar, dan stabil |
| `HASH (tenant_id)` | Tenant banyak dan ingin distribusi lebih merata |
| `RANGE(time) + HASH(tenant_id)` | Data sangat besar, query by time + tenant |

### Contoh hash by tenant

```sql
CREATE TABLE tenant_event (
    event_id    uuid        NOT NULL,
    tenant_id   uuid        NOT NULL,
    event_time  timestamptz NOT NULL,
    event_type  text        NOT NULL,
    payload     jsonb       NOT NULL,

    PRIMARY KEY (event_id, tenant_id)
) PARTITION BY HASH (tenant_id);
```

```sql
CREATE TABLE tenant_event_p00
PARTITION OF tenant_event
FOR VALUES WITH (MODULUS 8, REMAINDER 0);

CREATE TABLE tenant_event_p01
PARTITION OF tenant_event
FOR VALUES WITH (MODULUS 8, REMAINDER 1);

CREATE TABLE tenant_event_p02
PARTITION OF tenant_event
FOR VALUES WITH (MODULUS 8, REMAINDER 2);

CREATE TABLE tenant_event_p03
PARTITION OF tenant_event
FOR VALUES WITH (MODULUS 8, REMAINDER 3);

CREATE TABLE tenant_event_p04
PARTITION OF tenant_event
FOR VALUES WITH (MODULUS 8, REMAINDER 4);

CREATE TABLE tenant_event_p05
PARTITION OF tenant_event
FOR VALUES WITH (MODULUS 8, REMAINDER 5);

CREATE TABLE tenant_event_p06
PARTITION OF tenant_event
FOR VALUES WITH (MODULUS 8, REMAINDER 6);

CREATE TABLE tenant_event_p07
PARTITION OF tenant_event
FOR VALUES WITH (MODULUS 8, REMAINDER 7);
```

### Query yang ideal

```sql
SELECT *
FROM tenant_event
WHERE tenant_id = :tenant_id
ORDER BY event_time DESC
LIMIT :limit;
```

### Risiko tenant-based partitioning

| Risiko | Penjelasan |
|---|---|
| Tenant skew | Satu tenant jauh lebih besar dari yang lain |
| Tenant churn | Tenant baru terus masuk |
| Tenant merge/split | Perubahan organisasi bisa mahal |
| Retention by date sulit | Hash/list tenant tidak memudahkan drop data lama |
| Query reporting by date | Bisa menyentuh banyak tenant partitions |

---

## 4.3 Case-based / Entity-based Partition Key

Contoh:

| Kolom | Use case |
|---|---|
| `case_id` | case activity, case comments, case timeline |
| `process_instance_id` | workflow runtime/history |
| `account_id` | account transaction/activity |
| `customer_id` | customer activity |
| `order_id` | order line/event history |

Biasanya memakai **HASH**.

### Cocok jika

```text
Query dominan selalu:
WHERE case_id = ?
atau
WHERE process_instance_id = ?
atau
WHERE account_id = ?
```

### Contoh

```sql
CREATE TABLE case_activity (
    activity_id   uuid        NOT NULL,
    case_id       uuid        NOT NULL,
    tenant_id     uuid        NOT NULL,
    activity_type text        NOT NULL,
    created_at    timestamptz NOT NULL,
    payload       jsonb       NOT NULL,

    PRIMARY KEY (activity_id, case_id)
) PARTITION BY HASH (case_id);
```

### Query ideal

```sql
SELECT *
FROM case_activity
WHERE case_id = :case_id
ORDER BY created_at DESC
LIMIT 100;
```

### Anti-pattern

```sql
SELECT *
FROM case_activity
WHERE created_at >= :from_time
  AND created_at <  :to_time;
```

Kalau partition key adalah `case_id`, query berbasis waktu saja tidak banyak terbantu.

---

## 4.4 Domain/List-based Partition Key

Contoh:

| Kolom | Use case |
|---|---|
| `jurisdiction_code` | regulatory system |
| `region_code` | geographic operation |
| `case_type` | complaint, investigation, enforcement |
| `product_code` | product-specific processing |
| `business_unit` | organizational boundary |

Biasanya memakai **LIST**.

### Contoh

```sql
CREATE TABLE regulatory_case_event (
    event_id           uuid        NOT NULL,
    jurisdiction_code  text        NOT NULL,
    case_id            uuid        NOT NULL,
    event_time         timestamptz NOT NULL,
    event_type         text        NOT NULL,
    payload            jsonb       NOT NULL,

    PRIMARY KEY (event_id, jurisdiction_code)
) PARTITION BY LIST (jurisdiction_code);
```

```sql
CREATE TABLE regulatory_case_event_id
PARTITION OF regulatory_case_event
FOR VALUES IN ('ID');

CREATE TABLE regulatory_case_event_sg
PARTITION OF regulatory_case_event
FOR VALUES IN ('SG');

CREATE TABLE regulatory_case_event_my
PARTITION OF regulatory_case_event
FOR VALUES IN ('MY');

CREATE TABLE regulatory_case_event_other
PARTITION OF regulatory_case_event
DEFAULT;
```

### Cocok jika

| Kondisi | Cocok? |
|---|---:|
| Domain value kecil dan stabil | Ya |
| Query sering filter by domain | Ya |
| Ada operational ownership per domain | Ya |
| Value sering berubah | Tidak |
| Value jumlahnya ribuan | Tidak ideal |
| Domain value sering bertambah | Hati-hati |

---

# 5. Composite Partition Key

Composite strategy dipakai ketika satu dimension saja tidak cukup.

## 5.1 RANGE time + HASH tenant

Pola ini paling umum untuk tabel event besar.

```text
Parent: audit_event
│
├─ RANGE(event_time) per bulan
│  ├─ 2026-01
│  │  ├─ HASH(tenant_id) p0
│  │  ├─ HASH(tenant_id) p1
│  │  ├─ HASH(tenant_id) p2
│  │  └─ HASH(tenant_id) p3
│  │
│  └─ 2026-02
│     ├─ HASH(tenant_id) p0
│     ├─ HASH(tenant_id) p1
│     ├─ HASH(tenant_id) p2
│     └─ HASH(tenant_id) p3
```

### Parent

```sql
CREATE TABLE audit_event (
    event_id    uuid        NOT NULL,
    tenant_id   uuid        NOT NULL,
    case_id     uuid        NOT NULL,
    event_time  timestamptz NOT NULL,
    event_type  text        NOT NULL,
    payload     jsonb       NOT NULL,

    PRIMARY KEY (event_id, event_time, tenant_id)
) PARTITION BY RANGE (event_time);
```

### Monthly partition yang di-subpartition by hash

```sql
CREATE TABLE audit_event_p2026_01
PARTITION OF audit_event
FOR VALUES FROM ('2026-01-01 00:00:00+00')
             TO ('2026-02-01 00:00:00+00')
PARTITION BY HASH (tenant_id);
```

```sql
CREATE TABLE audit_event_p2026_01_h00
PARTITION OF audit_event_p2026_01
FOR VALUES WITH (MODULUS 4, REMAINDER 0);

CREATE TABLE audit_event_p2026_01_h01
PARTITION OF audit_event_p2026_01
FOR VALUES WITH (MODULUS 4, REMAINDER 1);

CREATE TABLE audit_event_p2026_01_h02
PARTITION OF audit_event_p2026_01
FOR VALUES WITH (MODULUS 4, REMAINDER 2);

CREATE TABLE audit_event_p2026_01_h03
PARTITION OF audit_event_p2026_01
FOR VALUES WITH (MODULUS 4, REMAINDER 3);
```

### Query ideal

```sql
SELECT *
FROM audit_event
WHERE event_time >= :from_time
  AND event_time <  :to_time
  AND tenant_id = :tenant_id
ORDER BY event_time DESC
LIMIT :limit;
```

### Kapan layak

| Kondisi | Layak? |
|---|---:|
| Data per bulan masih sangat besar | Ya |
| Query selalu punya time range | Ya |
| Query juga sering filter tenant | Ya |
| Tenant skew signifikan | Ya |
| Tim belum punya automation partition lifecycle | Jangan dulu |
| Total partition akan ribuan | Hati-hati |

PostgreSQL documentation memperingatkan bahwa jumlah partition yang besar bisa meningkatkan planning time dan memory consumption, sehingga composite/sub-partitioning perlu dikendalikan. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/ddl-partitioning.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 5.2 RANGE time + LIST jurisdiction

Cocok untuk regulatory system.

```text
RANGE(event_time) monthly
└─ LIST(jurisdiction_code)
```

Cocok jika:

```text
Query umum:
WHERE event_time >= ?
  AND event_time < ?
  AND jurisdiction_code = ?
```

Contoh:

```sql
CREATE TABLE regulatory_event (
    event_id           uuid        NOT NULL,
    jurisdiction_code  text        NOT NULL,
    event_time         timestamptz NOT NULL,
    case_id            uuid        NOT NULL,
    event_type         text        NOT NULL,
    payload            jsonb       NOT NULL,

    PRIMARY KEY (event_id, event_time, jurisdiction_code)
) PARTITION BY RANGE (event_time);
```

---

# 6. Skew Analysis

## 6.1 Apa itu skew?

Skew adalah kondisi ketika distribusi data tidak merata.

Contoh:

```text
Tenant A = 70% data
Tenant B = 10%
Tenant C = 5%
Tenant lain = 15%
```

Kalau partition by tenant/list, satu partition bisa menjadi terlalu besar. Kalau hash by tenant, tenant besar tetap bisa mendominasi satu hash bucket karena semua row tenant tersebut masuk bucket yang sama.

---

## 6.2 Cek distribusi tenant

```sql
SELECT
    tenant_id,
    COUNT(*) AS row_count,
    ROUND(
        COUNT(*) * 100.0 / SUM(COUNT(*)) OVER (),
        2
    ) AS percentage
FROM audit_event
GROUP BY tenant_id
ORDER BY row_count DESC
LIMIT 20;
```

Interpretasi:

| Temuan | Implikasi |
|---|---|
| Top tenant < 5% | Distribusi relatif sehat |
| Top tenant 10-20% | Perlu hati-hati |
| Top tenant > 30% | Skew besar |
| Top tenant > 50% | Jangan naïf partition by tenant saja |

---

## 6.3 Cek distribusi per bulan

```sql
SELECT
    date_trunc('month', event_time) AS month_bucket,
    COUNT(*) AS row_count
FROM audit_event
GROUP BY date_trunc('month', event_time)
ORDER BY month_bucket;
```

Interpretasi:

| Temuan | Implikasi |
|---|---|
| Per bulan relatif stabil | Monthly range cocok |
| Per bulan sangat besar | Pertimbangkan daily atau subpartition |
| Growth tajam | Future capacity harus dihitung |
| Banyak late-arriving data | Perlu default/staging partition handling |

---

## 6.4 Cek distribusi tenant per bulan

```sql
SELECT
    date_trunc('month', event_time) AS month_bucket,
    tenant_id,
    COUNT(*) AS row_count
FROM audit_event
GROUP BY
    date_trunc('month', event_time),
    tenant_id
ORDER BY
    month_bucket,
    row_count DESC;
```

Query ini penting sebelum memutuskan:

```text
RANGE(event_time)
vs
RANGE(event_time) + HASH(tenant_id)
vs
HASH(tenant_id)
```

---

## 6.5 Simulasi bucket hash

Untuk memperkirakan distribusi hash bucket:

```sql
SELECT
    mod(abs(hashtext(tenant_id::text)), 8) AS simulated_bucket,
    COUNT(*) AS row_count
FROM audit_event
GROUP BY simulated_bucket
ORDER BY simulated_bucket;
```

Catatan: ini hanya simulasi kasar untuk melihat potensi distribusi. Untuk keputusan final, tetap lakukan PoC dengan partitioned table dan workload nyata.

---

# 7. Query Pattern Analysis

Partition key harus dipilih berdasarkan query nyata, bukan feeling.

## 7.1 Ambil query mahal dari `pg_stat_statements`

```sql
SELECT
    calls,
    total_exec_time,
    mean_exec_time,
    rows,
    query
FROM pg_stat_statements
WHERE query ILIKE '%audit_event%'
ORDER BY total_exec_time DESC
LIMIT 20;
```

Cari predicate yang sering muncul:

```text
event_time >= ?
event_time < ?
tenant_id = ?
case_id = ?
jurisdiction_code = ?
status = ?
```

## 7.2 Buat frequency matrix manual

| Predicate | Muncul di query mahal? | Selective? | Stabil? | Kandidat partition key? |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| `event_time` | Tinggi | Sedang/tinggi | Ya | Sangat kuat |
| `tenant_id` | Tinggi | Tergantung skew | Ya | Kuat |
| `case_id` | Sedang | Tinggi | Ya | Kuat untuk hash |
| `status` | Tinggi | Rendah/sedang | Tidak stabil | Lemah |
| `event_type` | Sedang | Sedang | Ya | Biasanya index, bukan partition |
| `payload->>'x'` | Rendah | Tergantung | Tidak ideal | Jangan |

---

# 8. Partition Key Decision Matrix

| Candidate key | Pruning benefit | Lifecycle benefit | Write distribution | Constraint risk | Recommendation |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---|
| `event_time` | Tinggi jika query by range | Tinggi | Current partition hot | PK harus include time | Default untuk event/history |
| `created_at` | Tinggi jika query by created date | Tinggi | Current partition hot | PK include created_at | Baik untuk append-only |
| `business_date` | Tinggi untuk finance/reporting | Tinggi | Tergantung | PK include business_date | Baik untuk transaction/report |
| `tenant_id` | Tinggi jika query by tenant | Rendah | Bisa skew | PK include tenant_id | Baik untuk multi-tenant |
| `case_id` | Tinggi untuk case lookup | Rendah | Biasanya baik | PK include case_id | Baik untuk activity table |
| `jurisdiction_code` | Tinggi untuk regulatory boundary | Sedang | Bisa skew | PK include jurisdiction | Baik jika domain stabil |
| `status` | Sedang | Rendah | Row movement tinggi | Risk tinggi | Hindari |
| `updated_at` | Tidak stabil | Rendah | Row movement tinggi | Risk tinggi | Hindari |

---

# 9. Practical Pattern per Enterprise Table

## 9.1 Audit Event

```text
Recommended key:
RANGE(event_time)
```

Alasan:

| Faktor | Penilaian |
|---|---|
| Append-heavy | Ya |
| Query by time | Ya |
| Retention by date | Ya |
| Update partition key | Hampir tidak |
| Risk | Current partition hot |

Index tambahan:

```sql
CREATE INDEX audit_event_p2026_01_tenant_time_idx
ON audit_event_p2026_01 (tenant_id, event_time DESC);

CREATE INDEX audit_event_p2026_01_case_time_idx
ON audit_event_p2026_01 (case_id, event_time DESC);
```

---

## 9.2 Outbox Event

```text
Recommended key:
RANGE(created_at)
```

Contoh:

```sql
CREATE TABLE outbox_event (
    outbox_id       uuid        NOT NULL,
    aggregate_id    uuid        NOT NULL,
    event_type      text        NOT NULL,
    status          text        NOT NULL,
    created_at      timestamptz NOT NULL,
    processed_at    timestamptz,
    payload         jsonb       NOT NULL,

    PRIMARY KEY (outbox_id, created_at)
) PARTITION BY RANGE (created_at);
```

Catatan penting: jangan partition by `status` walaupun worker sering query `status = 'PENDING'`, karena status berubah dari `PENDING` ke `PROCESSED`/`FAILED`.

Lebih baik partition by `created_at`, lalu index status di tiap partition:

```sql
CREATE INDEX outbox_event_p2026_01_status_created_idx
ON outbox_event_p2026_01 (status, created_at);
```

---

## 9.3 Workflow Task History

Pilihan key tergantung query:

| Query dominan | Partition key |
|---|---|
| SLA report by completed date | `completed_at` |
| Task created history | `started_at` |
| Lookup by process instance | `HASH(process_instance_id)` |
| Long-term retention by time | `RANGE(completed_at)` atau `RANGE(started_at)` |

Contoh:

```sql
CREATE TABLE workflow_task_history (
    task_history_id      uuid        NOT NULL,
    process_instance_id  uuid        NOT NULL,
    case_id              uuid        NOT NULL,
    task_name            text        NOT NULL,
    started_at           timestamptz NOT NULL,
    completed_at         timestamptz,

    PRIMARY KEY (task_history_id, started_at)
) PARTITION BY RANGE (started_at);
```

---

## 9.4 Transaction History

Untuk finance/regulatory transaction, `business_date` sering lebih baik daripada `created_at`.

```text
created_at     = kapan row masuk sistem
business_date  = tanggal bisnis transaksi
```

Kalau reporting, settlement, dan retention memakai business date, pilih:

```sql
PARTITION BY RANGE (business_date)
```

Contoh:

```sql
CREATE TABLE transaction_history (
    transaction_id  uuid NOT NULL,
    account_id      uuid NOT NULL,
    business_date   date NOT NULL,
    created_at      timestamptz NOT NULL,
    amount          numeric(19, 4) NOT NULL,
    status          text NOT NULL,

    PRIMARY KEY (transaction_id, business_date)
) PARTITION BY RANGE (business_date);
```

---

# 10. Anti-pattern Partition Key

## 10.1 Partition by mutable status

```sql
PARTITION BY LIST (status)
```

Masalah:

| Problem | Dampak |
|---|---|
| Status sering berubah | Row movement |
| Query lifecycle tidak by status | Retention sulit |
| Status value bertambah | DDL bertambah |
| Hot status seperti `PENDING` | Hot partition |

Untuk outbox/job queue, lebih baik:

```text
PARTITION BY RANGE(created_at)
INDEX(status, created_at)
```

---

## 10.2 Partition by expression tanpa alasan kuat

Contoh:

```sql
PARTITION BY RANGE (date_trunc('month', event_time))
```

Hindari jika tidak perlu. Gunakan raw column `event_time`, lalu boundary bulanan. Selain lebih jelas, primary/unique constraint pada partitioned table juga memiliki batasan terkait partition key dan expressions. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/ddl-partitioning.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Lebih baik:

```sql
PARTITION BY RANGE (event_time)
```

---

## 10.3 Partition by low-cardinality noisy column

Contoh:

```sql
PARTITION BY LIST (priority)
```

Masalah:

```text
LOW, MEDIUM, HIGH, CRITICAL
```

Biasanya ini lebih cocok sebagai index/filter biasa, bukan partition boundary.

---

## 10.4 Partition by updated_at

```sql
PARTITION BY RANGE (updated_at)
```

Berisiko karena `updated_at` berubah setiap update. Kalau row berpindah partition, write path menjadi lebih mahal.

PostgreSQL dapat memindahkan row ke partition lain ketika update pada partition key membuat row tidak lagi memenuhi bound partition asal. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/ddl-partitioning.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 11. Java/Jersey/MyBatis Design Impact

## 11.1 REST API harus enforce partition key

Kalau table dipartisi by `event_time`, endpoint harus mewajibkan range.

Buruk:

```http
GET /audit-events?caseId=...
```

Baik:

```http
GET /audit-events?caseId=...&from=2026-01-01T00:00:00Z&to=2026-02-01T00:00:00Z
```

---

## 11.2 DTO request

```java
public record AuditEventSearchRequest(
    UUID tenantId,
    UUID caseId,
    OffsetDateTime fromTime,
    OffsetDateTime toTime,
    Integer limit
) {}
```

Validation:

```java
public void validate(AuditEventSearchRequest request) {
    if (request.fromTime() == null || request.toTime() == null) {
        throw new BadRequestException("fromTime and toTime are required");
    }

    if (!request.toTime().isAfter(request.fromTime())) {
        throw new BadRequestException("toTime must be after fromTime");
    }

    long days = Duration.between(request.fromTime(), request.toTime()).toDays();
    if (days > 31) {
        throw new BadRequestException("Date range must not exceed 31 days");
    }
}
```

---

## 11.3 MyBatis mapper discipline

```xml
<select id="searchAuditEvents" resultMap="AuditEventResultMap">
    SELECT
        event_id,
        tenant_id,
        case_id,
        event_type,
        event_time,
        payload
    FROM audit_event
    WHERE tenant_id = #{tenantId}
      AND case_id = #{caseId}
      AND event_time &gt;= #{fromTime}
      AND event_time &lt;  #{toTime}
    ORDER BY event_time DESC
    LIMIT #{limit}
</select>
```

Jangan buat mapper generic seperti ini untuk tabel partitioned besar:

```xml
<select id="searchAuditEventsUnsafe" resultMap="AuditEventResultMap">
    SELECT *
    FROM audit_event
    WHERE 1 = 1
    <if test="tenantId != null">
        AND tenant_id = #{tenantId}
    </if>
    <if test="caseId != null">
        AND case_id = #{caseId}
    </if>
    <if test="fromTime != null">
        AND event_time &gt;= #{fromTime}
    </if>
    <if test="toTime != null">
        AND event_time &lt; #{toTime}
    </if>
</select>
```

Masalahnya: filter partition key menjadi opsional. Untuk table besar, ini berbahaya.

---

# 12. Boundary Semantics: Timestamp vs Date

## 12.1 Gunakan half-open interval

Selalu gunakan:

```sql
WHERE event_time >= :from_time
  AND event_time <  :to_time
```

Bukan:

```sql
WHERE event_time BETWEEN :from_time AND :to_time
```

Alasan praktis:

| Pattern | Risiko |
|---|---|
| `BETWEEN` | Inclusive upper bound, rawan overlap |
| `>=` dan `<` | Boundary bersih, cocok dengan partition range |
| `date(event_time) = ?` | Bisa mengganggu pruning/index karena function di kolom |

---

## 12.2 Hindari function di partition key pada WHERE

Buruk:

```sql
WHERE date(event_time) = DATE '2026-01-15'
```

Baik:

```sql
WHERE event_time >= TIMESTAMPTZ '2026-01-15 00:00:00+00'
  AND event_time <  TIMESTAMPTZ '2026-01-16 00:00:00+00'
```

---

# 13. Decision Workflow Praktis

Gunakan urutan ini sebelum memilih key.

```text
1. Ambil top query dari pg_stat_statements
2. Kelompokkan predicate query mahal
3. Hitung data distribution untuk candidate key
4. Cek retention/archive policy
5. Cek constraint/PK/unique/FK compatibility
6. Simulasikan jumlah partition 3-5 tahun
7. Buat PoC DDL
8. Jalankan EXPLAIN/EXPLAIN ANALYZE
9. Jalankan benchmark insert/query/maintenance
10. Putuskan key final
```

---

# 14. SQL Toolkit untuk Memilih Partition Key

## 14.1 Kandidat query predicate dari `pg_stat_statements`

```sql
SELECT
    calls,
    ROUND(total_exec_time::numeric, 2) AS total_exec_time_ms,
    ROUND(mean_exec_time::numeric, 2) AS mean_exec_time_ms,
    rows,
    query
FROM pg_stat_statements
WHERE query ILIKE '%audit_event%'
ORDER BY total_exec_time DESC
LIMIT 30;
```

---

## 14.2 Size growth by month

```sql
SELECT
    date_trunc('month', event_time) AS month_bucket,
    COUNT(*) AS row_count
FROM audit_event
GROUP BY date_trunc('month', event_time)
ORDER BY month_bucket;
```

---

## 14.3 Tenant skew

```sql
SELECT
    tenant_id,
    COUNT(*) AS row_count,
    ROUND(
        COUNT(*) * 100.0 / SUM(COUNT(*)) OVER (),
        2
    ) AS pct_total
FROM audit_event
GROUP BY tenant_id
ORDER BY row_count DESC
LIMIT 20;
```

---

## 14.4 Case/event distribution

```sql
SELECT
    case_id,
    COUNT(*) AS row_count
FROM audit_event
GROUP BY case_id
ORDER BY row_count DESC
LIMIT 20;
```

---

## 14.5 Distinct cardinality check

```sql
SELECT
    COUNT(DISTINCT tenant_id) AS tenant_count,
    COUNT(DISTINCT case_id) AS case_count,
    COUNT(DISTINCT event_type) AS event_type_count
FROM audit_event;
```

Interpretasi:

| Cardinality | Cocok untuk |
|---|---|
| Sangat rendah | Biasanya bukan partition key |
| Sedang dan stabil | LIST mungkin cocok |
| Tinggi dan query equality | HASH mungkin cocok |
| Time/range natural | RANGE cocok |

---

# 15. Partition Key Review Template

Gunakan format ini saat design review.

```text
Table:
Current size:
Growth rate:
Retention policy:
Top query patterns:
Candidate partition key:
Partition model:
Expected partition count after 3 years:
Expected largest partition size:
PK/unique impact:
FK impact:
API contract impact:
Migration approach:
Failure modes:
Benchmark result:
Decision:
```

Contoh:

```text
Table:
audit_event

Current size:
1.5B rows / 900GB

Growth rate:
40M rows/month

Retention policy:
Hot 6 months, warm 36 months, archive >36 months

Top query patterns:
- tenant + event_time range
- case + event_time range
- monthly compliance report

Candidate partition key:
event_time

Partition model:
RANGE monthly

Expected partition count after 3 years:
36 active/warm partitions

PK/unique impact:
PRIMARY KEY must become (event_id, event_time)

FK impact:
Avoid FK referencing audit_event by event_id only

API contract impact:
fromTime/toTime required

Decision:
Proceed to PoC with RANGE(event_time)
```

---

# 16. Practical Recommendation Matrix

| Table type | Recommended partition key | Model | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| Audit event | `event_time` | RANGE | Monthly default |
| Domain event | `event_time` or `created_at` | RANGE | Append-heavy |
| Outbox event | `created_at` | RANGE | Index `status, created_at` |
| Inbox event | `received_at` | RANGE | Useful for retention |
| Transaction history | `business_date` | RANGE | Better than created_at for reporting |
| Workflow task history | `started_at` / `completed_at` | RANGE | Pick based on SLA/report query |
| Case activity | `case_id` | HASH | If query by case dominates |
| Tenant activity | `tenant_id` | HASH/LIST | Watch skew |
| Regulatory event | `jurisdiction_code` or `event_time` | LIST/RANGE | Pick based on query/reporting |
| Reporting fact | `business_date` | RANGE | Common OLAP pattern |

---

# 17. Common Design Mistakes

| Mistake | Consequence | Better approach |
|---|---|---|
| Partition by column not used in query | No pruning benefit | Use query predicate analysis |
| Partition by mutable status | Row movement overhead | Index status instead |
| Partition by tenant without skew analysis | Hot/large partition | Analyze distribution first |
| Partition by `created_at` when reporting uses `business_date` | Poor pruning for reports | Use business date |
| Use optional date filter in API | Unsafe full partition scans | Make range mandatory |
| Use too many subpartitions | Planning/ops overhead | Start simple, benchmark |
| Ignore PK/unique limitation | DDL failure/redesign late | Include partition key in PK |
| Use `BETWEEN` for timestamp range | Boundary bugs | Use `>=` and `<` |
| Apply function to partition key in WHERE | Pruning/index risk | Use raw range predicate |

---

# 18. Mini PoC: Compare Candidate Keys

Misal kandidat:

```text
A. RANGE(event_time)
B. HASH(tenant_id)
C. RANGE(event_time) + HASH(tenant_id)
```

Benchmark minimal:

```sql
EXPLAIN (ANALYZE, BUFFERS)
SELECT *
FROM audit_event
WHERE event_time >= '2026-01-01'
  AND event_time <  '2026-02-01'
  AND tenant_id = '00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000001'
ORDER BY event_time DESC
LIMIT 100;
```

```sql
EXPLAIN (ANALYZE, BUFFERS)
SELECT *
FROM audit_event
WHERE tenant_id = '00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000001'
ORDER BY event_time DESC
LIMIT 100;
```

```sql
EXPLAIN (ANALYZE, BUFFERS)
SELECT COUNT(*)
FROM audit_event
WHERE event_time >= '2026-01-01'
  AND event_time <  '2026-02-01';
```

Yang dibandingkan:

| Metric | Harus dilihat |
|---|---|
| Planning time | Terutama jika banyak partition |
| Execution time | Query utama |
| Buffers read/hit | I/O impact |
| Partition scanned | Apakah pruning bekerja |
| Rows removed by filter | Apakah index/predicate efektif |
| Insert latency | Write path |
| Maintenance time | Vacuum/analyze/index |

---

# Kesimpulan Seri 3

Partition key terbaik adalah kolom yang mempertemukan **query pattern**, **data lifecycle**, **constraint model**, dan **operational maintainability**.

Untuk kebanyakan sistem enterprise Java/Jersey/MyBatis:

```text
Audit/event/history/outbox/reporting → RANGE by time
Case/entity lookup heavy             → HASH by entity key
Jurisdiction/region boundary         → LIST by domain code
Very large mixed workload            → RANGE(time) + HASH/LIST secondary key
```

Jangan memilih partition key dari struktur tabel saja. Pilih dari **query nyata, growth nyata, dan lifecycle nyata**.

**Seri 3/14 selesai.**

Rekomendasi materi berikutnya: **Seri 4 - DDL Implementation: `CREATE TABLE ... PARTITION BY`, `PARTITION OF`, naming convention, schema convention, index inheritance behavior, dan production-style migration script.**

---

**Sources:**

- [Documentation: 18: 5.12. Table Partitioning](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/ddl-partitioning.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com)

## Response:
7/16/2026, 8:03:36 AM

# Seri 4/14 - DDL Implementation: `PARTITION BY`, `PARTITION OF`, Naming Convention, Schema Convention, dan Production-style Migration Script

## 1. Tujuan Seri Ini

Seri ini fokus ke implementasi DDL PostgreSQL partitioning secara praktis:

```text id="4yrbb7"
1. Membuat parent partitioned table
2. Membuat child partitions
3. Membuat default partition
4. Membuat index dan constraint dengan aman
5. Menyiapkan naming convention
6. Menyiapkan migration script production-style
7. Menyiapkan smoke test setelah deploy
```

Di PostgreSQL declarative partitioning, parent partitioned table adalah struktur logis/virtual, sedangkan data aktual disimpan di partitions. PostgreSQL mendukung partitioning berbasis **RANGE**, **LIST**, dan **HASH**. Partition dibuat dengan `CREATE TABLE ... PARTITION OF`, dan setiap partition harus memiliki kolom yang sama dengan parent table. ([postgresql.org](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/ddl-partitioning.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 2. DDL Mental Model

```text id="2ic2k7"
Partitioned Table DDL
│
├─ Parent table
│  ├─ Logical table name used by application
│  ├─ Defines columns
│  ├─ Defines partition key
│  ├─ Defines common constraints
│  └─ Does not store rows directly
│
├─ Child partitions
│  ├─ Store actual rows
│  ├─ Have partition bounds
│  ├─ Can have local indexes
│  └─ Can be attached/detached
│
├─ Default partition
│  ├─ Optional fallback
│  ├─ Prevents insert failure for out-of-bound rows
│  └─ Must be monitored
│
└─ Indexes / constraints
   ├─ Parent-level logical/partitioned index
   ├─ Partition-level physical indexes
   ├─ PK/UNIQUE must include partition key
   └─ Concurrent index build must be planned carefully
```

Saat membuat partition dengan `CREATE TABLE ... PARTITION OF`, PostgreSQL akan meng-clone index, constraint, dan user-defined row-level trigger yang ada di parent table ke partition baru. ([postgresql.org](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-createtable.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 3. Production Naming Convention

Gunakan naming yang konsisten agar automation, monitoring, dan troubleshooting mudah.

## 3.1 Parent Table

```text id="5c0aoq"
audit_event
transaction_history
workflow_task_history
outbox_event
case_activity
```

Parent table memakai nama domain normal karena ini yang dipakai aplikasi.

---

## 3.2 RANGE Monthly Partition

```text id="5jnvkd"
<table>_pYYYY_MM
```

Contoh:

```text id="bmvly5"
audit_event_p2026_01
audit_event_p2026_02
audit_event_p2026_03
```

---

## 3.3 RANGE Daily Partition

```text id="7tvz4v"
<table>_pYYYY_MM_DD
```

Contoh:

```text id="79esbk"
outbox_event_p2026_07_16
outbox_event_p2026_07_17
```

---

## 3.4 HASH Partition

```text id="dzim6l"
<table>_h00
<table>_h01
<table>_h02
```

Contoh:

```text id="t0cn21"
case_activity_h00
case_activity_h01
case_activity_h02
case_activity_h03
```

---

## 3.5 Composite Partition

```text id="69vixb"
<table>_pYYYY_MM_h00
<table>_pYYYY_MM_h01
<table>_pYYYY_MM_h02
```

Contoh:

```text id="xgwwpr"
audit_event_p2026_01_h00
audit_event_p2026_01_h01
audit_event_p2026_01_h02
audit_event_p2026_01_h03
```

---

## 3.6 Index Naming

```text id="kumbo8"
<partition_name>_<column_group>_idx
```

Contoh:

```text id="t2ec9d"
audit_event_p2026_01_tenant_time_idx
audit_event_p2026_01_case_time_idx
audit_event_p2026_01_event_type_time_idx
```

Untuk parent-level logical index:

```text id="tw3a73"
audit_event_tenant_time_idx
audit_event_case_time_idx
```

---

# 4. Schema Convention

Untuk kebanyakan aplikasi, mulai dari schema sederhana:

```text id="s2j63w"
app.audit_event
app.audit_event_p2026_01
app.audit_event_p2026_02
```

Alternatif jika partition sangat banyak:

```text id="ywub1o"
app.audit_event                 -- parent
app_part.audit_event_p2026_01   -- child partitions
app_part.audit_event_p2026_02
```

Namun, schema terpisah bisa membuat permission, search path, backup filter, dan migration script lebih kompleks. Untuk awal, rekomendasi praktis:

```text id="ub3pqn"
Gunakan schema yang sama dulu,
kecuali ada alasan operasional kuat untuk memisahkan partitions.
```

---

# 5. DDL Pattern 1 - RANGE Partitioning Monthly

## 5.1 Parent Table

Contoh untuk audit/event history:

```sql id="xlomqx"
CREATE TABLE app.audit_event (
    event_id      uuid        NOT NULL,
    tenant_id     uuid        NOT NULL,
    case_id       uuid        NOT NULL,
    event_type    text        NOT NULL,
    event_time    timestamptz NOT NULL,
    actor_id      uuid,
    payload       jsonb       NOT NULL,
    created_at    timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),

    CONSTRAINT audit_event_pk
        PRIMARY KEY (event_id, event_time),

    CONSTRAINT audit_event_event_type_not_blank_chk
        CHECK (length(trim(event_type)) > 0)
) PARTITION BY RANGE (event_time);
```

Catatan penting: `PRIMARY KEY` pada partitioned table harus mencakup partition key, yaitu `event_time`. Jika hanya `PRIMARY KEY (event_id)`, PostgreSQL tidak bisa menjamin uniqueness lintas partition. ([postgresql.org](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/ddl-partitioning.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 5.2 Monthly Partitions

```sql id="rddr9z"
CREATE TABLE app.audit_event_p2026_01
PARTITION OF app.audit_event
FOR VALUES FROM ('2026-01-01 00:00:00+00')
             TO ('2026-02-01 00:00:00+00');

CREATE TABLE app.audit_event_p2026_02
PARTITION OF app.audit_event
FOR VALUES FROM ('2026-02-01 00:00:00+00')
             TO ('2026-03-01 00:00:00+00');

CREATE TABLE app.audit_event_p2026_03
PARTITION OF app.audit_event
FOR VALUES FROM ('2026-03-01 00:00:00+00')
             TO ('2026-04-01 00:00:00+00');
```

Boundary RANGE di PostgreSQL memakai lower bound inclusive dan upper bound exclusive. Artinya `2026-02-01 00:00:00+00` masuk ke partition Februari, bukan Januari. ([postgresql.org](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/ddl-partitioning.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 5.3 Default Partition

```sql id="vw1tm6"
CREATE TABLE app.audit_event_default
PARTITION OF app.audit_event
DEFAULT;
```

Default partition berguna sebagai fallback, tetapi jangan dibiarkan tumbuh diam-diam. Default partition harus masuk monitoring.

---

## 5.4 Local Indexes per Partition

```sql id="dh8pgg"
CREATE INDEX audit_event_p2026_01_tenant_time_idx
ON app.audit_event_p2026_01 (tenant_id, event_time DESC);

CREATE INDEX audit_event_p2026_01_case_time_idx
ON app.audit_event_p2026_01 (case_id, event_time DESC);

CREATE INDEX audit_event_p2026_01_event_type_time_idx
ON app.audit_event_p2026_01 (event_type, event_time DESC);
```

Untuk partition Februari:

```sql id="cs8df4"
CREATE INDEX audit_event_p2026_02_tenant_time_idx
ON app.audit_event_p2026_02 (tenant_id, event_time DESC);

CREATE INDEX audit_event_p2026_02_case_time_idx
ON app.audit_event_p2026_02 (case_id, event_time DESC);

CREATE INDEX audit_event_p2026_02_event_type_time_idx
ON app.audit_event_p2026_02 (event_type, event_time DESC);
```

---

# 6. DDL Pattern 2 - LIST Partitioning

Contoh regulatory event by jurisdiction.

## 6.1 Parent Table

```sql id="hnj0ei"
CREATE TABLE app.regulatory_event (
    event_id           uuid        NOT NULL,
    jurisdiction_code  text        NOT NULL,
    case_id            uuid        NOT NULL,
    event_type         text        NOT NULL,
    event_time         timestamptz NOT NULL,
    payload            jsonb       NOT NULL,

    CONSTRAINT regulatory_event_pk
        PRIMARY KEY (event_id, jurisdiction_code),

    CONSTRAINT regulatory_event_jurisdiction_chk
        CHECK (jurisdiction_code = upper(jurisdiction_code))
) PARTITION BY LIST (jurisdiction_code);
```

---

## 6.2 List Partitions

```sql id="2wxlxa"
CREATE TABLE app.regulatory_event_id
PARTITION OF app.regulatory_event
FOR VALUES IN ('ID');

CREATE TABLE app.regulatory_event_sg
PARTITION OF app.regulatory_event
FOR VALUES IN ('SG');

CREATE TABLE app.regulatory_event_my
PARTITION OF app.regulatory_event
FOR VALUES IN ('MY');

CREATE TABLE app.regulatory_event_default
PARTITION OF app.regulatory_event
DEFAULT;
```

---

## 6.3 Indexes

```sql id="nxivg2"
CREATE INDEX regulatory_event_id_case_time_idx
ON app.regulatory_event_id (case_id, event_time DESC);

CREATE INDEX regulatory_event_sg_case_time_idx
ON app.regulatory_event_sg (case_id, event_time DESC);

CREATE INDEX regulatory_event_my_case_time_idx
ON app.regulatory_event_my (case_id, event_time DESC);
```

---

# 7. DDL Pattern 3 - HASH Partitioning

Contoh case activity by `case_id`.

## 7.1 Parent Table

```sql id="3j5vvw"
CREATE TABLE app.case_activity (
    activity_id    uuid        NOT NULL,
    case_id        uuid        NOT NULL,
    tenant_id      uuid        NOT NULL,
    activity_type  text        NOT NULL,
    created_at     timestamptz NOT NULL,
    payload        jsonb       NOT NULL,

    CONSTRAINT case_activity_pk
        PRIMARY KEY (activity_id, case_id)
) PARTITION BY HASH (case_id);
```

---

## 7.2 Hash Partitions

```sql id="5l3007"
CREATE TABLE app.case_activity_h00
PARTITION OF app.case_activity
FOR VALUES WITH (MODULUS 8, REMAINDER 0);

CREATE TABLE app.case_activity_h01
PARTITION OF app.case_activity
FOR VALUES WITH (MODULUS 8, REMAINDER 1);

CREATE TABLE app.case_activity_h02
PARTITION OF app.case_activity
FOR VALUES WITH (MODULUS 8, REMAINDER 2);

CREATE TABLE app.case_activity_h03
PARTITION OF app.case_activity
FOR VALUES WITH (MODULUS 8, REMAINDER 3);

CREATE TABLE app.case_activity_h04
PARTITION OF app.case_activity
FOR VALUES WITH (MODULUS 8, REMAINDER 4);

CREATE TABLE app.case_activity_h05
PARTITION OF app.case_activity
FOR VALUES WITH (MODULUS 8, REMAINDER 5);

CREATE TABLE app.case_activity_h06
PARTITION OF app.case_activity
FOR VALUES WITH (MODULUS 8, REMAINDER 6);

CREATE TABLE app.case_activity_h07
PARTITION OF app.case_activity
FOR VALUES WITH (MODULUS 8, REMAINDER 7);
```

---

## 7.3 Indexes

```sql id="77g4sa"
CREATE INDEX case_activity_h00_case_created_idx
ON app.case_activity_h00 (case_id, created_at DESC);

CREATE INDEX case_activity_h01_case_created_idx
ON app.case_activity_h01 (case_id, created_at DESC);

CREATE INDEX case_activity_h02_case_created_idx
ON app.case_activity_h02 (case_id, created_at DESC);

CREATE INDEX case_activity_h03_case_created_idx
ON app.case_activity_h03 (case_id, created_at DESC);

CREATE INDEX case_activity_h04_case_created_idx
ON app.case_activity_h04 (case_id, created_at DESC);

CREATE INDEX case_activity_h05_case_created_idx
ON app.case_activity_h05 (case_id, created_at DESC);

CREATE INDEX case_activity_h06_case_created_idx
ON app.case_activity_h06 (case_id, created_at DESC);

CREATE INDEX case_activity_h07_case_created_idx
ON app.case_activity_h07 (case_id, created_at DESC);
```

---

# 8. DDL Pattern 4 - Composite Partitioning: RANGE + HASH

Gunakan hanya jika satu partition waktu masih terlalu besar atau ada tenant/case skew.

## 8.1 Parent Table

```sql id="1qr8z8"
CREATE TABLE app.audit_event (
    event_id    uuid        NOT NULL,
    tenant_id   uuid        NOT NULL,
    case_id     uuid        NOT NULL,
    event_time  timestamptz NOT NULL,
    event_type  text        NOT NULL,
    payload     jsonb       NOT NULL,

    CONSTRAINT audit_event_pk
        PRIMARY KEY (event_id, event_time, tenant_id)
) PARTITION BY RANGE (event_time);
```

---

## 8.2 Monthly Partition sebagai Partitioned Table

```sql id="29lwgv"
CREATE TABLE app.audit_event_p2026_01
PARTITION OF app.audit_event
FOR VALUES FROM ('2026-01-01 00:00:00+00')
             TO ('2026-02-01 00:00:00+00')
PARTITION BY HASH (tenant_id);
```

---

## 8.3 Hash Sub-partitions

```sql id="2nlu2z"
CREATE TABLE app.audit_event_p2026_01_h00
PARTITION OF app.audit_event_p2026_01
FOR VALUES WITH (MODULUS 4, REMAINDER 0);

CREATE TABLE app.audit_event_p2026_01_h01
PARTITION OF app.audit_event_p2026_01
FOR VALUES WITH (MODULUS 4, REMAINDER 1);

CREATE TABLE app.audit_event_p2026_01_h02
PARTITION OF app.audit_event_p2026_01
FOR VALUES WITH (MODULUS 4, REMAINDER 2);

CREATE TABLE app.audit_event_p2026_01_h03
PARTITION OF app.audit_event_p2026_01
FOR VALUES WITH (MODULUS 4, REMAINDER 3);
```

---

## 8.4 Warning

Composite partitioning cepat menghasilkan banyak object.

```text id="yh7hw7"
60 monthly partitions
x 8 hash partitions
= 480 physical partitions

480 partitions
x 4 indexes
= 1.920 index objects
```

Jangan gunakan composite partitioning tanpa automation, monitoring, dan benchmark.

---

# 9. Parent-level Index vs Partition-level Index

## 9.1 Membuat Index di Parent

```sql id="6lft3s"
CREATE INDEX audit_event_tenant_time_idx
ON app.audit_event (tenant_id, event_time DESC);

CREATE INDEX audit_event_case_time_idx
ON app.audit_event (case_id, event_time DESC);
```

Ini membuat partitioned index secara logical pada parent, dan PostgreSQL akan membuat/mengelola index pada partitions yang relevan. Namun untuk tabel production besar, strategi index harus memperhatikan locking dan deployment.

---

## 9.2 Batasan `CREATE INDEX CONCURRENTLY`

Dokumentasi PostgreSQL menyebut concurrent build untuk index pada partitioned table parent saat ini belum didukung. Workaround yang direkomendasikan adalah build index secara concurrent pada tiap partition, lalu membuat partitioned index di parent secara non-concurrent; tahap parent itu menjadi metadata-only jika index partition sudah cocok. ([postgresql.org](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-createindex.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Praktisnya:

```sql id="7xemf8"
CREATE INDEX CONCURRENTLY audit_event_p2026_01_tenant_time_idx
ON app.audit_event_p2026_01 (tenant_id, event_time DESC);

CREATE INDEX CONCURRENTLY audit_event_p2026_02_tenant_time_idx
ON app.audit_event_p2026_02 (tenant_id, event_time DESC);

CREATE INDEX CONCURRENTLY audit_event_p2026_03_tenant_time_idx
ON app.audit_event_p2026_03 (tenant_id, event_time DESC);
```

Lalu buat parent-level index:

```sql id="jwzv41"
CREATE INDEX audit_event_tenant_time_idx
ON app.audit_event (tenant_id, event_time DESC);
```

Catatan: untuk migration tools, `CREATE INDEX CONCURRENTLY` tidak boleh dijalankan di dalam transaction block. Jadi migration runner harus mendukung non-transactional migration untuk statement tersebut.

---

# 10. Constraint Implementation Rules

## 10.1 Primary Key Harus Include Partition Key

Valid:

```sql id="q51tbj"
PRIMARY KEY (event_id, event_time)
```

Tidak valid untuk `PARTITION BY RANGE(event_time)`:

```sql id="hffbjo"
PRIMARY KEY (event_id)
```

Karena unique/primary key partitioned table harus mencakup semua partition key. ([postgresql.org](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/ddl-partitioning.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 10.2 Unique Constraint

Valid:

```sql id="25kgkr"
CONSTRAINT audit_event_request_unique
UNIQUE (tenant_id, external_request_id, event_time)
```

Tidak valid:

```sql id="v8gm2h"
CONSTRAINT audit_event_request_unique
UNIQUE (tenant_id, external_request_id)
```

Jika partition key adalah `event_time`, constraint unique harus memasukkan `event_time`.

---

## 10.3 Check Constraint

Common check di parent:

```sql id="u4c6db"
CONSTRAINT audit_event_payload_is_object_chk
CHECK (jsonb_typeof(payload) = 'object')
```

Check seperti ini akan berlaku ke partitions karena partition memiliki struktur kolom dan constraint yang sesuai dengan parent.

---

# 11. Production Migration Script - Fresh Table

Contoh script `V20260716_001_create_audit_event_partitioned.sql`.

```sql id="wv9y4d"
BEGIN;

CREATE SCHEMA IF NOT EXISTS app;

CREATE TABLE app.audit_event (
    event_id      uuid        NOT NULL,
    tenant_id     uuid        NOT NULL,
    case_id       uuid        NOT NULL,
    event_type    text        NOT NULL,
    event_time    timestamptz NOT NULL,
    actor_id      uuid,
    payload       jsonb       NOT NULL,
    created_at    timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),

    CONSTRAINT audit_event_pk
        PRIMARY KEY (event_id, event_time),

    CONSTRAINT audit_event_event_type_not_blank_chk
        CHECK (length(trim(event_type)) > 0),

    CONSTRAINT audit_event_payload_object_chk
        CHECK (jsonb_typeof(payload) = 'object')
) PARTITION BY RANGE (event_time);

CREATE TABLE app.audit_event_p2026_07
PARTITION OF app.audit_event
FOR VALUES FROM ('2026-07-01 00:00:00+00')
             TO ('2026-08-01 00:00:00+00');

CREATE TABLE app.audit_event_p2026_08
PARTITION OF app.audit_event
FOR VALUES FROM ('2026-08-01 00:00:00+00')
             TO ('2026-09-01 00:00:00+00');

CREATE TABLE app.audit_event_p2026_09
PARTITION OF app.audit_event
FOR VALUES FROM ('2026-09-01 00:00:00+00')
             TO ('2026-10-01 00:00:00+00');

CREATE TABLE app.audit_event_default
PARTITION OF app.audit_event
DEFAULT;

CREATE INDEX audit_event_p2026_07_tenant_time_idx
ON app.audit_event_p2026_07 (tenant_id, event_time DESC);

CREATE INDEX audit_event_p2026_07_case_time_idx
ON app.audit_event_p2026_07 (case_id, event_time DESC);

CREATE INDEX audit_event_p2026_08_tenant_time_idx
ON app.audit_event_p2026_08 (tenant_id, event_time DESC);

CREATE INDEX audit_event_p2026_08_case_time_idx
ON app.audit_event_p2026_08 (case_id, event_time DESC);

CREATE INDEX audit_event_p2026_09_tenant_time_idx
ON app.audit_event_p2026_09 (tenant_id, event_time DESC);

CREATE INDEX audit_event_p2026_09_case_time_idx
ON app.audit_event_p2026_09 (case_id, event_time DESC);

COMMIT;
```

Catatan: script ini transactional karena tidak memakai `CREATE INDEX CONCURRENTLY`. Cocok untuk fresh table atau saat belum ada traffic besar.

---

# 12. Production Migration Script - Adding Future Partition

Contoh script rutin bulanan:

```sql id="odyrz1"
BEGIN;

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS app.audit_event_p2026_10
PARTITION OF app.audit_event
FOR VALUES FROM ('2026-10-01 00:00:00+00')
             TO ('2026-11-01 00:00:00+00');

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS audit_event_p2026_10_tenant_time_idx
ON app.audit_event_p2026_10 (tenant_id, event_time DESC);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS audit_event_p2026_10_case_time_idx
ON app.audit_event_p2026_10 (case_id, event_time DESC);

ANALYZE app.audit_event_p2026_10;

COMMIT;
```

Untuk partition kosong/future, index creation biasanya cepat. Untuk partition yang sudah besar, gunakan strategy concurrent index per partition.

---

# 13. Production Migration Script - Attach Existing Table as Partition

Ini penting untuk backfill/migration.

## 13.1 Buat staging table

```sql id="55zeea"
CREATE TABLE app.audit_event_stage_2026_06 (
    LIKE app.audit_event INCLUDING DEFAULTS INCLUDING CONSTRAINTS
);
```

## 13.2 Load data

```sql id="9781wq"
INSERT INTO app.audit_event_stage_2026_06 (
    event_id,
    tenant_id,
    case_id,
    event_type,
    event_time,
    actor_id,
    payload,
    created_at
)
SELECT
    event_id,
    tenant_id,
    case_id,
    event_type,
    event_time,
    actor_id,
    payload,
    created_at
FROM app.audit_event_old
WHERE event_time >= '2026-06-01 00:00:00+00'
  AND event_time <  '2026-07-01 00:00:00+00';
```

## 13.3 Tambahkan CHECK constraint sesuai bound

```sql id="14ti12"
ALTER TABLE app.audit_event_stage_2026_06
ADD CONSTRAINT audit_event_stage_2026_06_bound_chk
CHECK (
    event_time >= TIMESTAMPTZ '2026-06-01 00:00:00+00'
    AND event_time < TIMESTAMPTZ '2026-07-01 00:00:00+00'
);
```

## 13.4 Validate data

```sql id="k5o426"
SELECT COUNT(*) AS invalid_rows
FROM app.audit_event_stage_2026_06
WHERE NOT (
    event_time >= TIMESTAMPTZ '2026-06-01 00:00:00+00'
    AND event_time < TIMESTAMPTZ '2026-07-01 00:00:00+00'
);
```

Expected:

```text id="j4zi7n"
invalid_rows = 0
```

## 13.5 Create indexes on staging table

```sql id="7t24a7"
CREATE INDEX audit_event_stage_2026_06_tenant_time_idx
ON app.audit_event_stage_2026_06 (tenant_id, event_time DESC);

CREATE INDEX audit_event_stage_2026_06_case_time_idx
ON app.audit_event_stage_2026_06 (case_id, event_time DESC);
```

## 13.6 Attach sebagai partition

```sql id="hsiv5c"
ALTER TABLE app.audit_event
ATTACH PARTITION app.audit_event_stage_2026_06
FOR VALUES FROM ('2026-06-01 00:00:00+00')
             TO ('2026-07-01 00:00:00+00');
```

Dokumentasi PostgreSQL menjelaskan bahwa saat attach partition, scan validasi bisa dihindari jika table yang akan di-attach sudah memiliki `CHECK` constraint yang membuktikan data cocok dengan partition constraint. Ini penting untuk tabel besar. ([postgresql.org](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/ddl-partitioning.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

## 13.7 Rename setelah attach

Opsional, agar naming konsisten:

```sql id="vm9jwn"
ALTER TABLE app.audit_event_stage_2026_06
RENAME TO audit_event_p2026_06;
```

---

# 14. Smoke Test Setelah DDL

## 14.1 Cek partition list

```sql id="5irk1q"
SELECT
    parent.relname AS parent_table,
    child.relname  AS partition_table
FROM pg_inherits i
JOIN pg_class parent ON parent.oid = i.inhparent
JOIN pg_class child  ON child.oid = i.inhrelid
WHERE parent.relname = 'audit_event'
ORDER BY child.relname;
```

`pg_inherits` menyimpan informasi inheritance hierarchy, termasuk declarative partitioning; kolom `inhdetachpending` menunjukkan partition yang sedang dalam proses detach. ([postgresql.org](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/catalog-pg-inherits.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 14.2 Cek partitioned table metadata

```sql id="rb8c7n"
SELECT
    c.oid::regclass AS table_name,
    c.relkind,
    pt.partstrat,
    pg_get_partkeydef(c.oid) AS partition_key
FROM pg_class c
JOIN pg_partitioned_table pt ON pt.partrelid = c.oid
WHERE c.oid = 'app.audit_event'::regclass;
```

Interpretasi `partstrat`:

```text id="dqf087"
r = range
l = list
h = hash
```

---

## 14.3 Insert routing test

```sql id="4n23h3"
INSERT INTO app.audit_event (
    event_id,
    tenant_id,
    case_id,
    event_type,
    event_time,
    payload
)
VALUES (
    gen_random_uuid(),
    gen_random_uuid(),
    gen_random_uuid(),
    'TEST_EVENT',
    '2026-07-16 10:00:00+00',
    '{"test": true}'
);
```

Cek actual partition:

```sql id="tvzmb4"
SELECT
    tableoid::regclass AS actual_partition,
    event_id,
    event_time
FROM app.audit_event
WHERE event_time >= '2026-07-16 00:00:00+00'
  AND event_time <  '2026-07-17 00:00:00+00'
ORDER BY event_time DESC
LIMIT 5;
```

Expected:

```text id="0r4vjq"
actual_partition = app.audit_event_p2026_07
```

---

## 14.4 Pruning smoke test

```sql id="nkil8e"
EXPLAIN
SELECT *
FROM app.audit_event
WHERE event_time >= '2026-07-01 00:00:00+00'
  AND event_time <  '2026-08-01 00:00:00+00';
```

Yang diharapkan:

```text id="zqjrum"
Plan hanya menyentuh audit_event_p2026_07,
bukan semua monthly partitions.
```

# 15. Java/Jersey/MyBatis Usage Impact

## 15.1 Aplikasi tetap insert ke parent

Jangan insert ke child partition dari aplikasi.

```xml id="ta9czn"
<insert id="insertAuditEvent">
    INSERT INTO app.audit_event (
        event_id,
        tenant_id,
        case_id,
        event_type,
        event_time,
        actor_id,
        payload
    )
    VALUES (
        #{eventId},
        #{tenantId},
        #{caseId},
        #{eventType},
        #{eventTime},
        #{actorId},
        CAST(#{payloadJson} AS jsonb)
    )
</insert>
```

Aplikasi tidak perlu tahu nama `audit_event_p2026_07`.

---

## 15.2 Query harus membawa partition predicate

```xml id="0r1u59"
<select id="searchAuditEvents" resultMap="AuditEventResultMap">
    SELECT
        event_id,
        tenant_id,
        case_id,
        event_type,
        event_time,
        actor_id,
        payload,
        created_at
    FROM app.audit_event
    WHERE tenant_id = #{tenantId}
      AND event_time &gt;= #{fromTime}
      AND event_time &lt;  #{toTime}
    ORDER BY event_time DESC
    LIMIT #{limit}
</select>
```

---

## 15.3 Hindari dynamic WHERE yang membuat partition key opsional

Buruk:

```xml id="4nqoi2"
<select id="unsafeSearchAuditEvents" resultMap="AuditEventResultMap">
    SELECT *
    FROM app.audit_event
    WHERE 1 = 1
    <if test="tenantId != null">
        AND tenant_id = #{tenantId}
    </if>
    <if test="fromTime != null">
        AND event_time &gt;= #{fromTime}
    </if>
    <if test="toTime != null">
        AND event_time &lt; #{toTime}
    </if>
</select>
```

Lebih aman: validasi request di service layer dan gunakan mapper khusus untuk access pattern yang valid.

---

# 16. Deployment Checklist

| Checklist | Status |
|---|---|
| Parent table dibuat dengan `PARTITION BY` yang benar |
| PK/unique constraint mencakup partition key |
| Partition awal tersedia |
| Future partition tersedia minimal N bulan/hari ke depan |
| Default partition dibuat atau insert failure sengaja diterima |
| Index child partition sesuai query utama |
| Smoke test insert routing berhasil |
| Smoke test pruning berhasil |
| Default partition monitoring tersedia |
| Migration rollback strategy jelas |
| MyBatis mapper insert ke parent table |
| REST/service layer enforce partition filter |
| DDL naming convention konsisten |
| Non-transactional migration disiapkan jika memakai `CREATE INDEX CONCURRENTLY` |

---

# 17. Common DDL Mistakes

| Mistake | Dampak | Solusi |
|---|---|---|
| `PRIMARY KEY(id)` pada table partition by time | DDL gagal | Gunakan `PRIMARY KEY(id, event_time)` |
| Aplikasi insert ke child partition | Coupling ke physical layout | Insert ke parent |
| Tidak membuat future partition | Insert gagal atau masuk default | Automasi future partition |
| Default partition tidak dimonitor | Data menumpuk diam-diam | Alert row count/size |
| Query pakai `date(event_time)` | Pruning/index bisa tidak optimal | Pakai range `>=` dan `<` |
| Terlalu banyak partition dibuat dari awal | Catalog/planning overhead | Buat sesuai horizon |
| Index dibuat parent concurrent | Tidak supported | Build concurrent per partition |
| Attach table besar tanpa CHECK | Scan validasi lama | Tambahkan CHECK sesuai bound sebelum attach |
| Naming tidak konsisten | Automation sulit | Pakai naming convention ketat |

---

Struktur folder:

```text id="1ff0yx"
db/
├─ migration/
│  ├─ V20260716_001__create_audit_event_parent.sql
│  ├─ V20260716_002__create_audit_event_partitions_2026_q3.sql
│  ├─ V20260716_003__create_audit_event_indexes.sql
│  └─ V20260716_004__audit_event_smoke_checks.sql
│
└─ repeatable/
   └─ R__create_next_audit_event_partitions.sql
```

Untuk GitHub CI/CD, minimal jalankan:

```text id="dxzb2f"
1. SQL lint/basic syntax check
2. Apply migration ke ephemeral PostgreSQL container
3. Insert routing test
4. EXPLAIN pruning test
5. Rollback/drop test bila environment mendukung
```
