# Seri 5/14 - Partition Boundary & Lifecycle Design: Daily/Monthly/Yearly, Future Partition, Retention, Archive, Detach/Drop, Hot/Warm/Cold

## 1. Tujuan Seri Ini

Seri ini fokus pada keputusan operasional setelah kita tahu model dan partition key:

> **Seberapa besar setiap partition? Berapa jauh future partition harus dibuat? Kapan partition lama di-detach/drop/archive? Bagaimana desain hot/warm/cold data?**

PostgreSQL partitioning sangat berguna untuk maintenance data besar karena bulk load/delete bisa diganti menjadi operasi partition-level. Dokumentasi resmi PostgreSQL menyebut bahwa `DROP TABLE` partition atau `ALTER TABLE DETACH PARTITION` jauh lebih cepat daripada bulk operation dan menghindari overhead `VACUUM` yang biasanya muncul setelah bulk `DELETE`. PostgreSQL juga menyebut data yang jarang dipakai dapat dipindahkan ke storage yang lebih murah/lambat. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/ddl-partitioning.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 2. Mental Model Lifecycle

```text
Partition lifecycle bukan hanya "buat tabel bulanan".

Partition lifecycle =
│
├─ Boundary design
│  ├─ daily
│  ├─ weekly
│  ├─ monthly
│  ├─ quarterly/yearly
│  └─ hash/list bucket
│
├─ Data temperature
│  ├─ hot  = aktif ditulis & sering dibaca
│  ├─ warm = jarang ditulis, masih sering dibaca
│  ├─ cold = hampir read-only, jarang dibaca
│  └─ expired/archive = keluar dari primary workload
│
├─ Partition horizon
│  ├─ past partitions
│  ├─ current partition
│  ├─ future partitions
│  └─ default partition fallback
│
├─ Lifecycle action
│  ├─ create future partition
│  ├─ analyze
│  ├─ detach
│  ├─ archive/export
│  ├─ drop
│  └─ reindex/vacuum when needed
│
└─ Operational safety
   ├─ no missing partition
   ├─ no silent default partition growth
   ├─ controlled locks
   ├─ repeatable migration
   └─ monitoring/alerting
```

---

# 3. Boundary Design: Daily vs Monthly vs Yearly

## 3.1 Rule of Thumb

| Boundary | Cocok untuk | Risiko |
|---|---|---|
| **Daily** | Data sangat besar per hari, outbox/inbox/event ingestion tinggi, retention pendek | Banyak partition dan index object |
| **Weekly** | Kompromi antara daily dan monthly | Kadang tidak align dengan calendar/reporting |
| **Monthly** | Audit/event/history/transaction umum | Current partition bisa terlalu besar jika volume tinggi |
| **Quarterly** | Data sedang, reporting kuartalan | Retention kurang granular |
| **Yearly** | Data besar tapi query/retention kasar | Partition terlalu besar untuk maintenance |
| **Hash fixed bucket** | Distribusi by tenant/case/account | Tidak membantu retention by date |
| **Composite time + hash/list** | Data sangat besar + query by time dan tenant/domain | Object count cepat meledak |

---

## 3.2 Monthly Boundary - Default Enterprise Starting Point

Untuk audit/event/history table, mulai dari monthly partition biasanya paling masuk akal.

```sql
CREATE TABLE app.audit_event (
    event_id    uuid        NOT NULL,
    tenant_id   uuid        NOT NULL,
    case_id     uuid        NOT NULL,
    event_time  timestamptz NOT NULL,
    event_type  text        NOT NULL,
    payload     jsonb       NOT NULL,

    CONSTRAINT audit_event_pk
        PRIMARY KEY (event_id, event_time)
) PARTITION BY RANGE (event_time);
```

```sql
CREATE TABLE app.audit_event_p2026_07
PARTITION OF app.audit_event
FOR VALUES FROM ('2026-07-01 00:00:00+00')
             TO ('2026-08-01 00:00:00+00');

CREATE TABLE app.audit_event_p2026_08
PARTITION OF app.audit_event
FOR VALUES FROM ('2026-08-01 00:00:00+00')
             TO ('2026-09-01 00:00:00+00');
```

PostgreSQL range partition bounds menggunakan pola lower-bound inclusive dan upper-bound exclusive; ini membuat boundary bulanan/harian aman tanpa overlap jika memakai `FROM ('2026-07-01') TO ('2026-08-01')`. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/ddl-partitioning.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 3.3 Daily Boundary - Untuk High-volume Event/Outbox

Daily partition cocok kalau satu bulan terlalu besar atau retention sangat granular.

```sql
CREATE TABLE app.outbox_event (
    outbox_id     uuid        NOT NULL,
    aggregate_id  uuid        NOT NULL,
    event_type    text        NOT NULL,
    status        text        NOT NULL,
    created_at    timestamptz NOT NULL,
    processed_at  timestamptz,
    payload       jsonb       NOT NULL,

    CONSTRAINT outbox_event_pk
        PRIMARY KEY (outbox_id, created_at)
) PARTITION BY RANGE (created_at);
```

```sql
CREATE TABLE app.outbox_event_p2026_07_16
PARTITION OF app.outbox_event
FOR VALUES FROM ('2026-07-16 00:00:00+00')
             TO ('2026-07-17 00:00:00+00');

CREATE TABLE app.outbox_event_p2026_07_17
PARTITION OF app.outbox_event
FOR VALUES FROM ('2026-07-17 00:00:00+00')
             TO ('2026-07-18 00:00:00+00');
```

Daily partition cocok jika:

| Kondisi | Cocok? |
|---|---:|
| Row per hari sangat besar | Ya |
| Retention by day | Ya |
| Query umum by short time window | Ya |
| Tim punya automation partition creation | Wajib |
| Data kecil/sedang | Biasanya tidak |

---

## 3.4 Yearly Boundary - Jarang Jadi Pilihan Terbaik

Yearly partition tampak sederhana, tetapi sering terlalu besar untuk tabel event/history.

```sql
CREATE TABLE app.audit_event_p2026
PARTITION OF app.audit_event
FOR VALUES FROM ('2026-01-01 00:00:00+00')
             TO ('2027-01-01 00:00:00+00');
```

Cocok jika:

| Kondisi | Cocok? |
|---|---:|
| Data growth rendah | Ya |
| Query kebanyakan lintas tahun | Mungkin |
| Retention by year | Ya |
| Butuh maintenance granular | Tidak |
| Partition size ratusan GB/TB | Tidak ideal |

---

# 4. Boundary Selection Matrix

Gunakan matrix ini untuk memilih interval awal.

| Faktor | Daily | Monthly | Yearly |
|---|---:|---:|---:|
| Data volume sangat tinggi | Kuat | Sedang | Lemah |
| Retention granular | Kuat | Sedang | Lemah |
| Jumlah partition | Banyak | Sedang | Sedikit |
| Operational complexity | Tinggi | Sedang | Rendah |
| Query by small date range | Kuat | Kuat | Lemah/sedang |
| Reporting bulanan | Sedang | Kuat | Lemah |
| Archive/drop mudah | Kuat | Kuat | Kasar |
| Cocok untuk awal | Jika volume tinggi | Ya | Hanya data kecil/sedang |

Rekomendasi awal untuk enterprise:

```text
Audit/event/history umum     → monthly
Outbox/inbox high-volume     → daily atau weekly
Transaction/reporting fact   → monthly by business_date
Telemetry sangat besar       → daily, kadang hourly tapi hati-hati
Cold historical archive      → yearly atau external archive
```

---

# 5. Hot/Warm/Cold Data Design

## 5.1 Data Temperature

```text
HOT
- current partition
- aktif ditulis
- query latency penting
- autovacuum/analyze lebih aktif
- index harus optimal

WARM
- partition beberapa bulan terakhir
- jarang ditulis
- masih sering dibaca
- index masih penting
- maintenance terjadwal

COLD
- data lama
- read-only atau hampir read-only
- query jarang
- bisa dipindah tablespace/storage lebih murah
- archive candidate

EXPIRED / ARCHIVE
- keluar dari primary database workload
- detach/export/drop
```

---

## 5.2 Contoh Policy

Untuk `audit_event`:

| Temperature | Umur data | Lokasi | Action |
|---|---:|---|---|
| Hot | 0-1 bulan | primary storage | full index, vacuum/analyze aktif |
| Warm | 2-12 bulan | primary storage | normal index, periodic analyze |
| Cold | 13-36 bulan | cheaper storage/tablespace | mostly read-only |
| Archive | >36 bulan | object storage/archive DB | detach/export/drop |

Untuk `outbox_event`:

| Temperature | Umur data | Action |
|---|---:|---|
| Hot | 0-7 hari | worker polling, full index |
| Warm | 8-30 hari | retry/audit |
| Archive/drop | >30/90 hari | detach/drop setelah audit requirement terpenuhi |

---

# 6. Future Partition Creation

## 6.1 Kenapa Future Partition Wajib

Jika tidak ada matching partition, insert ke parent bisa gagal, kecuali ada default partition. Default partition bisa menyelamatkan availability, tetapi jika tidak dimonitor, data bisa menumpuk diam-diam.

PostgreSQL menyediakan default partition sebagai fallback untuk row yang tidak cocok dengan partition lain, tetapi default partition harus dipakai sebagai safety net, bukan tempat data permanen. Praktik yang baik adalah membuat future partitions sebelum dibutuhkan dan memonitor default partition. ([Crunchy Data](https://www.crunchydata.com/blog/postgres-partitioning-with-a-default-partition?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 6.2 Horizon Rekomendasi

| Partition interval | Future horizon minimal | Rekomendasi praktis |
|---|---:|---|
| Daily | 7-30 hari ke depan | Minimal 14 hari |
| Weekly | 4-12 minggu ke depan | Minimal 8 minggu |
| Monthly | 3-12 bulan ke depan | Minimal 3-6 bulan |
| Yearly | 1-3 tahun ke depan | Minimal 1 tahun |

Untuk production, jangan membuat hanya current partition. Buat beberapa future partitions agar ada buffer jika job maintenance gagal.

---

## 6.3 Manual Future Partition Script

Contoh monthly:

```sql
BEGIN;

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS app.audit_event_p2026_10
PARTITION OF app.audit_event
FOR VALUES FROM ('2026-10-01 00:00:00+00')
             TO ('2026-11-01 00:00:00+00');

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS audit_event_p2026_10_tenant_time_idx
ON app.audit_event_p2026_10 (tenant_id, event_time DESC);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS audit_event_p2026_10_case_time_idx
ON app.audit_event_p2026_10 (case_id, event_time DESC);

ANALYZE app.audit_event_p2026_10;

COMMIT;
```

---

## 6.4 Idempotent Function untuk Membuat Monthly Partition

Contoh PL/pgSQL sederhana:

```sql
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION app.create_audit_event_month_partition(
    p_month_start timestamptz
)
RETURNS void
LANGUAGE plpgsql
AS $$
DECLARE
    v_month_start timestamptz;
    v_month_end   timestamptz;
    v_table_name  text;
    v_sql         text;
BEGIN
    v_month_start := date_trunc('month', p_month_start);
    v_month_end   := v_month_start + interval '1 month';
    v_table_name  := format(
        'audit_event_p%s',
        to_char(v_month_start, 'YYYY_MM')
    );

    v_sql := format(
        'CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS app.%I
         PARTITION OF app.audit_event
         FOR VALUES FROM (%L) TO (%L)',
        v_table_name,
        v_month_start,
        v_month_end
    );

    EXECUTE v_sql;

    EXECUTE format(
        'CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS %I
         ON app.%I (tenant_id, event_time DESC)',
        v_table_name || '_tenant_time_idx',
        v_table_name
    );

    EXECUTE format(
        'CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS %I
         ON app.%I (case_id, event_time DESC)',
        v_table_name || '_case_time_idx',
        v_table_name
    );

    EXECUTE format('ANALYZE app.%I', v_table_name);
END;
$$;
```

Pemakaian:

```sql
SELECT app.create_audit_event_month_partition('2026-10-01 00:00:00+00');
SELECT app.create_audit_event_month_partition('2026-11-01 00:00:00+00');
SELECT app.create_audit_event_month_partition('2026-12-01 00:00:00+00');
```

---

# 7. Retention Design

## 7.1 Retention Policy Harus Tertulis

Jangan implement `DROP PARTITION` tanpa policy eksplisit.

Template:

```text
Table:
Partition key:
Partition interval:
Hot retention:
Warm retention:
Cold retention:
Archive requirement:
Legal/regulatory hold:
Drop eligibility:
Approval required:
Backup dependency:
Rollback expectation:
```

Contoh:

```text
Table:
app.audit_event

Partition key:
event_time

Partition interval:
monthly

Hot retention:
6 months

Warm retention:
36 months

Archive:
monthly partition exported to object storage after 36 months

Drop:
drop from primary DB after archive checksum and legal-hold check

Legal hold:
do not detach/drop partitions with active hold marker
```

---

## 7.2 Retention Action Options

| Action | Kapan dipakai | Catatan |
|---|---|---|
| `DROP TABLE partition` | Data sudah tidak dibutuhkan | Cepat, tapi irreversible kecuali backup |
| `DETACH PARTITION` | Mau archive/validate dulu | Partition menjadi standalone table |
| `DETACH PARTITION CONCURRENTLY` | Ingin mengurangi blocking parent | Tidak boleh di transaction block dan ada batasan |
| Export lalu drop | Data harus disimpan di luar primary DB | Butuh checksum/manifest |
| Move tablespace | Data cold tapi masih online | Butuh storage planning |
| Keep read-only | Data masih sering diaudit | Maintenance lebih ringan |

---

# 8. Detach vs Drop

## 8.1 DROP Partition

Jika benar-benar ingin menghapus data lama:

```sql
DROP TABLE app.audit_event_p2023_06;
```

Ini cepat dibanding:

```sql
DELETE FROM app.audit_event
WHERE event_time >= '2023-06-01'
  AND event_time <  '2023-07-01';
```

Karena bulk `DELETE` menghasilkan dead tuples dan membutuhkan vacuum. PostgreSQL documentation menyebut drop/detach partition dapat menghindari overhead vacuum dari bulk delete. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/ddl-partitioning.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 8.2 DETACH Partition

Jika ingin melepas partition dari parent tapi tetap menyimpan tabelnya:

```sql
ALTER TABLE app.audit_event
DETACH PARTITION app.audit_event_p2023_06;
```

Setelah detach, `app.audit_event_p2023_06` menjadi standalone table dan tidak lagi menjadi bagian dari query parent. PostgreSQL `ALTER TABLE` documentation menjelaskan bahwa detached partition tetap ada sebagai standalone table dan index yang attached ke parent index akan dilepas. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-altertable.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 8.3 DETACH PARTITION CONCURRENTLY

Untuk mengurangi lock terhadap parent table:

```sql
ALTER TABLE app.audit_event
DETACH PARTITION app.audit_event_p2023_06 CONCURRENTLY;
```

Catatan penting:

```text
DETACH PARTITION CONCURRENTLY tidak boleh dijalankan di dalam transaction block.
```

Dokumentasi `ALTER TABLE` menyediakan bentuk `DETACH PARTITION partition_name [ CONCURRENTLY | FINALIZE ]`; ini penting untuk migration tool karena statement concurrent biasanya harus dipisah dari migration transactional. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-altertable.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 8.4 Workflow Aman untuk Archive

```text
1. Pilih partition kandidat retention
2. Pastikan tidak terkena legal/regulatory hold
3. Stop/avoid write ke periode tersebut
4. DETACH PARTITION
5. Validate standalone table row count/checksum
6. Export/archive
7. Verify archive
8. Drop standalone table
9. Record audit metadata
```

Contoh:

```sql
ALTER TABLE app.audit_event
DETACH PARTITION app.audit_event_p2023_06;
```

Validasi:

```sql
SELECT COUNT(*) FROM app.audit_event_p2023_06;

SELECT
    min(event_time) AS min_event_time,
    max(event_time) AS max_event_time
FROM app.audit_event_p2023_06;
```

Export contoh dengan `COPY`:

```sql
COPY (
    SELECT *
    FROM app.audit_event_p2023_06
    ORDER BY event_time, event_id
) TO '/archive/audit_event_p2023_06.csv'
WITH (FORMAT csv, HEADER true);
```

Drop setelah archive verified:

```sql
DROP TABLE app.audit_event_p2023_06;
```

---

# 9. Legal Hold / Regulatory Hold Pattern

Untuk domain regulatory, jangan drop hanya berdasarkan umur data. Harus ada hold-check.

Contoh metadata table:

```sql
CREATE TABLE app.partition_retention_hold (
    table_name      text        NOT NULL,
    partition_name  text        NOT NULL,
    hold_reason     text        NOT NULL,
    hold_by         text        NOT NULL,
    hold_until      timestamptz,
    created_at      timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),

    CONSTRAINT partition_retention_hold_pk
        PRIMARY KEY (table_name, partition_name)
);
```

Cek sebelum detach/drop:

```sql
SELECT EXISTS (
    SELECT 1
    FROM app.partition_retention_hold
    WHERE table_name = 'audit_event'
      AND partition_name = 'audit_event_p2023_06'
      AND (hold_until IS NULL OR hold_until > now())
) AS has_active_hold;
```

Operational rule:

```text
Jika has_active_hold = true:
- jangan detach
- jangan drop
- catat skipped retention action
```

---

# 10. Partition Catalog Query untuk Lifecycle

## 10.1 List Partitions

```sql
SELECT
    parent.relname AS parent_table,
    child.relname  AS partition_table
FROM pg_inherits i
JOIN pg_class parent ON parent.oid = i.inhparent
JOIN pg_class child  ON child.oid = i.inhrelid
WHERE parent.relname = 'audit_event'
ORDER BY child.relname;
```

---

## 10.2 Size per Partition

```sql
SELECT
    child.relname AS partition_table,
    pg_size_pretty(pg_total_relation_size(child.oid)) AS total_size,
    pg_size_pretty(pg_relation_size(child.oid)) AS table_size,
    pg_size_pretty(pg_indexes_size(child.oid)) AS indexes_size
FROM pg_inherits i
JOIN pg_class parent ON parent.oid = i.inhparent
JOIN pg_class child  ON child.oid = i.inhrelid
WHERE parent.relname = 'audit_event'
ORDER BY pg_total_relation_size(child.oid) DESC;
```

---

## 10.3 Row Estimate per Partition

```sql
SELECT
    child.relname AS partition_table,
    child.reltuples::bigint AS estimated_rows
FROM pg_inherits i
JOIN pg_class parent ON parent.oid = i.inhparent
JOIN pg_class child  ON child.oid = i.inhrelid
WHERE parent.relname = 'audit_event'
ORDER BY child.relname;
```

---

## 10.4 Default Partition Monitoring

```sql
SELECT COUNT(*) AS default_rows
FROM app.audit_event_default;
```

Size:

```sql
SELECT
    pg_size_pretty(pg_total_relation_size('app.audit_event_default')) AS default_size;
```

Alert rule:

```text
default_rows > 0
atau
default_size > threshold
```

Default partition yang berisi data bukan selalu incident, tetapi harus dianggap sebagai **signal** bahwa ada data out-of-bound atau future partition creation gagal.

---

# 11. Moving Data Out of Default Partition

Misal data `2026-10` masuk ke default karena partition belum ada.

## 11.1 Buat partition target

```sql
CREATE TABLE app.audit_event_p2026_10
PARTITION OF app.audit_event
FOR VALUES FROM ('2026-10-01 00:00:00+00')
             TO ('2026-11-01 00:00:00+00');
```

Namun, jika default partition berisi row yang cocok dengan bound partition baru, PostgreSQL bisa menolak pembuatan partition karena overlap dengan default partition constraint. Praktiknya, pindahkan data keluar dari default dulu dengan staging workflow.

## 11.2 Buat staging table

```sql
CREATE TABLE app.audit_event_stage_2026_10 (
    LIKE app.audit_event INCLUDING DEFAULTS INCLUDING CONSTRAINTS
);
```

## 11.3 Pindahkan data dari default ke staging

```sql
INSERT INTO app.audit_event_stage_2026_10
SELECT *
FROM app.audit_event_default
WHERE event_time >= '2026-10-01 00:00:00+00'
  AND event_time <  '2026-11-01 00:00:00+00';
```

## 11.4 Hapus dari default

```sql
DELETE FROM app.audit_event_default
WHERE event_time >= '2026-10-01 00:00:00+00'
  AND event_time <  '2026-11-01 00:00:00+00';
```

## 11.5 Tambahkan CHECK constraint di staging

```sql
ALTER TABLE app.audit_event_stage_2026_10
ADD CONSTRAINT audit_event_stage_2026_10_bound_chk
CHECK (
    event_time >= TIMESTAMPTZ '2026-10-01 00:00:00+00'
    AND event_time < TIMESTAMPTZ '2026-11-01 00:00:00+00'
);
```

## 11.6 Attach staging sebagai partition

```sql
ALTER TABLE app.audit_event
ATTACH PARTITION app.audit_event_stage_2026_10
FOR VALUES FROM ('2026-10-01 00:00:00+00')
             TO ('2026-11-01 00:00:00+00');
```

Saat attach partition, PostgreSQL dapat melakukan scan validasi; dokumentasi merekomendasikan `CHECK` constraint yang cocok di table yang akan di-attach agar scan validasi bisa dihindari atau diminimalkan. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/ddl-partitioning.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 12. Automation Options

## 12.1 Manual SQL Migration

Cocok untuk:

```text
- partition bulanan
- volume manageable
- tim ingin explicit GitHub-reviewed DDL
- compliance butuh change control ketat
```

Pattern:

```text
Setiap release:
- buat partition N bulan ke depan
- buat index
- smoke test
```

---

## 12.2 PL/pgSQL Maintenance Function

Cocok untuk:

```text
- on-prem
- ingin logic custom
- retention perlu legal hold check
- tidak ingin extension tambahan
```

---

## 12.3 Kubernetes CronJob

Cocok untuk:

```text
- deployment microservice di Kubernetes
- partition lifecycle bagian dari platform ops
- bisa pakai psql container atau maintenance app
```

Contoh pseudo CronJob command:

```bash
psql "$DATABASE_URL" \
  -v ON_ERROR_STOP=1 \
  -f /sql/create_future_partitions.sql
```

---

## 12.4 pg_partman

`pg_partman` adalah extension populer untuk mengelola partition set PostgreSQL. Project pg_partman menyatakan extension ini membangun di atas declarative partitioning PostgreSQL dan membantu maintenance child table seperti membuat child baru dan drop old child berdasarkan retention policy. ([GitHub](https://github.com/pgpartman/pg_partman?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

AWS RDS documentation juga menjelaskan bahwa maintenance `pg_partman` dapat membuat partition baru, detach partition, atau menghapus partition lama, dan bisa dijalankan melalui `run_maintenance_proc` bersama `pg_cron`. ([AWS Documentation](https://docs.aws.amazon.com/AmazonRDS/latest/UserGuide/PostgreSQL_Partitions.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Azure PostgreSQL documentation menjelaskan pola serupa: `create_parent()` mengisi konfigurasi `part_config`, lalu `run_maintenance()` membuat partition baru atau menjalankan retention policy. ([Microsoft Learn](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/azure/postgresql/configure-maintain/how-to-use-pg-partman?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Rekomendasi praktis:

| Context | Rekomendasi |
|---|---|
| Butuh full Git-reviewed DDL | Manual migration |
| Butuh automation kuat dan extension allowed | pg_partman |
| Managed cloud dengan extension support | pg_partman + scheduler |
| Compliance ketat | Manual DDL + internal metadata + approval |
| Banyak partition harian | pg_partman atau custom job wajib |

---

# 13. Lifecycle Metadata Table

Untuk production, buat metadata agar lifecycle auditable.

```sql
CREATE TABLE app.partition_lifecycle_event (
    lifecycle_event_id uuid        NOT NULL DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    table_name         text        NOT NULL,
    partition_name     text        NOT NULL,
    action             text        NOT NULL,
    action_status      text        NOT NULL,
    lower_bound        timestamptz,
    upper_bound        timestamptz,
    row_count          bigint,
    total_size_bytes   bigint,
    checksum_value     text,
    archive_location   text,
    executed_by        text        NOT NULL,
    executed_at        timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    details            jsonb       NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb,

    CONSTRAINT partition_lifecycle_event_pk
        PRIMARY KEY (lifecycle_event_id),

    CONSTRAINT partition_lifecycle_action_chk
        CHECK (action IN (
            'CREATE',
            'ATTACH',
            'DETACH',
            'ARCHIVE',
            'DROP',
            'SKIP_LEGAL_HOLD',
            'VALIDATE'
        )),

    CONSTRAINT partition_lifecycle_status_chk
        CHECK (action_status IN ('SUCCESS', 'FAILED', 'SKIPPED'))
);
```

Insert audit lifecycle event:

```sql
INSERT INTO app.partition_lifecycle_event (
    table_name,
    partition_name,
    action,
    action_status,
    lower_bound,
    upper_bound,
    row_count,
    total_size_bytes,
    archive_location,
    executed_by,
    details
)
VALUES (
    'audit_event',
    'audit_event_p2023_06',
    'DETACH',
    'SUCCESS',
    '2023-06-01 00:00:00+00',
    '2023-07-01 00:00:00+00',
    42398234,
    98234123456,
    NULL,
    'partition-maintenance-job',
    '{"change_ticket":"CHG-12345"}'::jsonb
);
```

# 14. Java/Jersey Operational Endpoint Pattern

Untuk enterprise ops, sediakan endpoint internal/admin untuk health check partition.

```http
GET /internal/db/partitions/audit-event/health
```

Response contoh:

```json
{
  "table": "app.audit_event",
  "partitionKey": "event_time",
  "interval": "monthly",
  "currentPartitionExists": true,
  "futurePartitionsAvailableUntil": "2027-01-01T00:00:00Z",
  "defaultPartitionRows": 0,
  "largestPartition": {
    "name": "audit_event_p2026_07",
    "sizeBytes": 81234567890
  },
  "alerts": []
}
```

MyBatis query untuk cek future horizon:

```xml
<select id="listAuditEventPartitions" resultType="PartitionInfo">
    SELECT
        child.relname AS partition_name,
        pg_total_relation_size(child.oid) AS total_size_bytes,
        child.reltuples::bigint AS estimated_rows
    FROM pg_inherits i
    JOIN pg_class parent ON parent.oid = i.inhparent
    JOIN pg_class child  ON child.oid = i.inhrelid
    JOIN pg_namespace nsp ON nsp.oid = parent.relnamespace
    WHERE nsp.nspname = #{schemaName}
      AND parent.relname = #{tableName}
    ORDER BY child.relname
</select>
```

Service-level rule:

```java
public PartitionHealth evaluate(List<PartitionInfo> partitions, long defaultRows) {
    boolean hasDefaultRows = defaultRows > 0;

    boolean hasFuturePartition = partitions.stream()
        .anyMatch(p -> p.partitionName().compareTo("audit_event_p2026_10") >= 0);

    List<String> alerts = new ArrayList<>();

    if (!hasFuturePartition) {
        alerts.add("Future partition horizon is below required threshold");
    }

    if (hasDefaultRows) {
        alerts.add("Default partition contains rows");
    }

    return new PartitionHealth(hasFuturePartition, defaultRows, alerts);
}
```

---

# 15. CI/CD Lifecycle Checks

Untuk GitHub pipeline atau deployment migration, minimal cek:

```text
1. Parent partitioned table exists
2. Current partition exists
3. Future partitions exist
4. Default partition exists or intentionally absent
5. Required indexes exist on new partitions
6. Insert routing works
7. EXPLAIN shows partition pruning
8. Default partition row count is acceptable
9. Retention script does not target partition under legal hold
10. DDL is idempotent where appropriate
```

Smoke test SQL:

```sql
-- 1. Insert routing
INSERT INTO app.audit_event (
    event_id,
    tenant_id,
    case_id,
    event_time,
    event_type,
    payload
)
VALUES (
    gen_random_uuid(),
    gen_random_uuid(),
    gen_random_uuid(),
    '2026-07-16 10:00:00+00',
    'SMOKE_TEST',
    '{"smoke":true}'
);

-- 2. Actual partition
SELECT tableoid::regclass AS actual_partition
FROM app.audit_event
WHERE event_type = 'SMOKE_TEST'
ORDER BY event_time DESC
LIMIT 1;

-- 3. Pruning check
EXPLAIN
SELECT *
FROM app.audit_event
WHERE event_time >= '2026-07-01 00:00:00+00'
  AND event_time <  '2026-08-01 00:00:00+00';
```

---

# 16. Retention Job Pseudocode

```text
For each partition older than retention threshold:
  1. Parse partition boundary
  2. Check legal hold
  3. Check partition is not current/future/default
  4. Record planned action
  5. Detach partition
  6. Archive/export
  7. Verify archive checksum
  8. Drop detached table
  9. Record success/failure
  10. Emit metrics
```

---

# 17. Practical Retention SQL: Find Old Monthly Partitions

Jika naming convention `audit_event_pYYYY_MM` konsisten:

```sql
WITH partitions AS (
    SELECT
        child.relname AS partition_name,
        to_date(
            substring(child.relname FROM 'audit_event_p([0-9]{4}_[0-9]{2})'),
            'YYYY_MM'
        ) AS month_start
    FROM pg_inherits i
    JOIN pg_class parent ON parent.oid = i.inhparent
    JOIN pg_class child  ON child.oid = i.inhrelid
    WHERE parent.relname = 'audit_event'
      AND child.relname ~ '^audit_event_p[0-9]{4}_[0-9]{2}$'
)
SELECT
    partition_name,
    month_start
FROM partitions
WHERE month_start < date_trunc('month', current_date) - interval '36 months'
ORDER BY month_start;
```

Catatan: untuk production, jangan hanya bergantung pada parsing nama. Lebih baik simpan metadata lifecycle partition atau ambil bound dari PostgreSQL catalog.

---

# 18. Failure Modes

| Failure mode | Gejala | Dampak | Pencegahan |
|---|---|---|---|
| Future partition missing | Insert gagal atau masuk default | Incident write path | Create ahead-of-time + alert |
| Default partition tumbuh | Row count default > 0 | Pruning kacau, maintenance berat | Monitor default row count |
| Boundary overlap | DDL gagal | Deployment gagal | Generate boundary otomatis |
| Boundary gap | Insert gagal | Data loss risk jika app tidak retry | Smoke test per boundary |
| Partition terlalu kecil | Ribuan object | Planning/ops overhead | Pilih interval lebih besar |
| Partition terlalu besar | Query/maintenance berat | Vacuum/index berat | Pilih interval lebih kecil/subpartition |
| Drop tanpa archive | Data hilang | Compliance incident | Legal hold + archive workflow |
| Detach blocking | Lock contention | App latency naik | Gunakan maintenance window / concurrently |
| Retention job salah target | Partition aktif terhapus | Severe incident | Dry-run + approval + metadata |
| Late-arriving data | Masuk default/old partition | Operational confusion | Grace period + default monitoring |

---

# 19. Design Pattern untuk Late-arriving Data

Late-arriving data umum di event integration, batch import, dan regulatory feed.

Pilihan:

| Pattern | Penjelasan |
|---|---|
| Keep old partitions longer | Jangan drop terlalu cepat |
| Grace period | Misalnya drop hanya setelah 3 bulan + 7 hari |
| Default partition | Fallback untuk out-of-bound |
| Staging table | Validasi dulu sebelum masuk parent |
| Reject old data at API | Jika domain memang tidak boleh late |
| Route to archive path | Data sangat lama langsung archive |

Contoh validasi di service:

```java
OffsetDateTime oldestAccepted = OffsetDateTime.now(ZoneOffset.UTC).minusYears(3);

if (event.eventTime().isBefore(oldestAccepted)) {
    throw new BadRequestException("eventTime is older than accepted retention window");
}
```

Untuk regulatory/audit domain, hati-hati: menolak old data bisa salah secara bisnis. Lebih aman staging + review jika data lama masih legal evidence.

---

# 20. Partition Lifecycle Checklist

| Checklist | Status |
|---|---|
| Partition interval dipilih berdasarkan volume/query/retention |
| Current partition tersedia |
| Future partition tersedia sesuai horizon |
| Default partition policy jelas |
| Default partition dimonitor |
| Hot/warm/cold policy tertulis |
| Retention threshold jelas |
| Legal hold check tersedia |
| Archive/export workflow tersedia |
| Detach/drop workflow diuji |
| Row count/checksum validation tersedia |
| Lifecycle event dicatat |
| CI/CD smoke test tersedia |
| Java/Jersey admin health endpoint tersedia |
| Alert untuk future partition horizon tersedia |
| Alert untuk default partition rows tersedia |

---

# 21. Practical Recommendation

Untuk stack Anda - Java/Jersey/MyBatis, Camunda, PostgreSQL, on-prem/AWS/Azure - rekomendasi awal:

| Table type | Boundary | Future horizon | Retention action |
|---|---|---:|---|
| `audit_event` | Monthly by `event_time` | 6 bulan | Detach/export/drop after policy |
| `workflow_task_history` | Monthly by `started_at`/`completed_at` | 3-6 bulan | Archive old partitions |
| `outbox_event` | Daily or weekly by `created_at` | 14-30 hari | Drop after processed + retention |
| `inbox_event` | Daily/monthly by `received_at` | 14-30 hari | Drop/archive |
| `transaction_history` | Monthly by `business_date` | 6-12 bulan | Archive after statutory retention |
| `case_activity` | Hash by `case_id` or monthly by `created_at` | Fixed hash/no date horizon | Depends on domain |
| `reporting_fact` | Monthly by `business_date` | 6-12 bulan | Archive or summarize |

---

# Kesimpulan Seri 5

Partition boundary dan lifecycle design adalah inti operasional PostgreSQL partitioning.

Keputusan paling penting:

```text
1. Daily/monthly/yearly harus dipilih dari volume + query + retention.
2. Future partition harus dibuat sebelum dibutuhkan.
3. Default partition boleh ada, tapi harus dimonitor.
4. Retention sebaiknya detach/archive/drop, bukan massive DELETE.
5. Legal/regulatory hold harus menjadi guardrail.
6. Lifecycle action harus auditable.
7. Java/Jersey/MyBatis perlu endpoint/health check agar issue partition terlihat sebelum incident.
```

**Seri 5/14 selesai.**
