Expression index berguna jika query memang selalu memakai expression tertentu. PostgreSQL docs menjelaskan index bisa dibuat pada hasil expression, bukan hanya nilai kolom biasa. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/indexes-expressional.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Contoh case-insensitive search:

```sql id="1ysnir"
CREATE INDEX case_file_p2026_07_external_ref_lower_idx
ON app.case_file_p2026_07 (lower(external_reference));
```

Query:

```sql id="xc362i"
SELECT *
FROM app.case_file
WHERE lower(external_reference) = lower(:externalReference);
```

Namun untuk partition key time, jangan jadikan expression index sebagai pengganti predicate partition-friendly.

Buruk:

```sql id="lu0y4m"
WHERE date(event_time) = DATE '2026-07-16'
```

Lebih baik:

```sql id="3wynzb"
WHERE event_time >= TIMESTAMPTZ '2026-07-16 00:00:00+00'
  AND event_time <  TIMESTAMPTZ '2026-07-17 00:00:00+00'
```

---

# 10. Unique Index, Primary Key, dan Partition Key

PostgreSQL otomatis membuat unique index saat `PRIMARY KEY` atau `UNIQUE` constraint dibuat. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/indexes-unique.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Untuk partitioned table, constraint uniqueness punya aturan khusus: `PRIMARY KEY` atau `UNIQUE` pada partitioned table harus mencakup semua partition key. Ini karena uniqueness lintas partition tidak bisa dijamin hanya oleh index lokal tiap partition jika partition key tidak ikut. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/ddl-partitioning.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

## 10.1 Valid

```sql id="gul7nl"
CREATE TABLE app.audit_event (
    event_id    uuid        NOT NULL,
    event_time  timestamptz NOT NULL,
    tenant_id   uuid        NOT NULL,
    payload     jsonb       NOT NULL,

    CONSTRAINT audit_event_pk
        PRIMARY KEY (event_id, event_time)
) PARTITION BY RANGE (event_time);
```

## 10.2 Tidak Valid

```sql id="f80jl8"
CREATE TABLE app.audit_event (
    event_id    uuid        NOT NULL,
    event_time  timestamptz NOT NULL,

    CONSTRAINT audit_event_pk
        PRIMARY KEY (event_id)
) PARTITION BY RANGE (event_time);
```

## 10.3 Practical Alternative

Jika domain ingin `event_id` globally unique tetapi partition key harus ikut:

```text id="2j65u6"
Option A:
PRIMARY KEY (event_id, event_time)
dan event_id tetap UUID globally unique secara aplikasi.

Option B:
Buat separate registry table untuk global uniqueness jika benar-benar wajib DB-enforced.

Option C:
Pilih partition key yang ikut natural identity, misalnya tenant_id, jika domain mendukung.
```

---

# 11. Index Strategy per Table Type

## 11.1 Audit Event - Range by `event_time`

Common queries:

```text id="osry38"
1. Search by tenant + time
2. Search by case + time
3. Report by event_type + time
4. Payload search tertentu
```

Recommended per monthly partition:

```sql id="8ko6z1"
CREATE INDEX audit_event_p2026_07_tenant_time_idx
ON app.audit_event_p2026_07 (tenant_id, event_time DESC);

CREATE INDEX audit_event_p2026_07_case_time_idx
ON app.audit_event_p2026_07 (case_id, event_time DESC);

CREATE INDEX audit_event_p2026_07_event_type_time_idx
ON app.audit_event_p2026_07 (event_type, event_time DESC);
```

Optional:

```sql id="8q05dl"
CREATE INDEX audit_event_p2026_07_event_time_brin_idx
ON app.audit_event_p2026_07
USING BRIN (event_time);
```

JSONB hanya jika query benar-benar sering:

```sql id="radupx"
CREATE INDEX audit_event_p2026_07_payload_gin_idx
ON app.audit_event_p2026_07
USING GIN (payload);
```

---

## 11.2 Outbox Event - Range by `created_at`

Worker query:

```sql id="igzgn8"
SELECT *
FROM app.outbox_event
WHERE status = 'PENDING'
  AND created_at >= :fromTime
  AND created_at < :toTime
ORDER BY created_at
LIMIT 100
FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED;
```

Index current/hot partitions:

```sql id="z1ge4t"
CREATE INDEX outbox_event_p2026_07_pending_idx
ON app.outbox_event_p2026_07 (created_at, outbox_id)
WHERE status = 'PENDING';
```

History lookup:

```sql id="2ggkqh"
CREATE INDEX outbox_event_p2026_07_aggregate_created_idx
ON app.outbox_event_p2026_07 (aggregate_id, created_at DESC);
```

Avoid:

```sql id="xsojyl"
PARTITION BY LIST (status)
```

Karena status mutable.

---

## 11.3 Workflow Task History

Common query:

```sql id="sntcxi"
WHERE process_instance_id = ?
ORDER BY started_at DESC
```

Index:

```sql id="9fcr21"
CREATE INDEX workflow_task_history_p2026_07_process_started_idx
ON app.workflow_task_history_p2026_07
(process_instance_id, started_at DESC);
```

SLA report:

```sql id="f31qmn"
WHERE completed_at >= ?
  AND completed_at < ?
  AND task_name = ?
```

Index:

```sql id="rkke03"
CREATE INDEX workflow_task_history_p2026_07_task_completed_idx
ON app.workflow_task_history_p2026_07
(task_name, completed_at DESC);
```

---

## 11.4 Transaction History - Range by `business_date`

Lookup:

```sql id="f8j7cg"
CREATE INDEX transaction_history_p2026_07_account_date_idx
ON app.transaction_history_p2026_07 (account_id, business_date DESC);

CREATE INDEX transaction_history_p2026_07_transaction_id_idx
ON app.transaction_history_p2026_07 (transaction_id);
```

Reporting:

```sql id="hdemlx"
CREATE INDEX transaction_history_p2026_07_status_date_idx
ON app.transaction_history_p2026_07 (status, business_date);
```

For large scan/report, consider BRIN:

```sql id="ejl0j2"
CREATE INDEX transaction_history_p2026_07_business_date_brin_idx
ON app.transaction_history_p2026_07
USING BRIN (business_date);
```

---

# 12. Parent Index Creation Strategy

## 12.1 Fresh Table

Untuk table baru:

```sql id="5d625b"
CREATE TABLE app.audit_event (
    event_id    uuid        NOT NULL,
    tenant_id   uuid        NOT NULL,
    case_id     uuid        NOT NULL,
    event_time  timestamptz NOT NULL,
    event_type  text        NOT NULL,
    payload     jsonb       NOT NULL,

    CONSTRAINT audit_event_pk
        PRIMARY KEY (event_id, event_time)
) PARTITION BY RANGE (event_time);

CREATE INDEX audit_event_tenant_time_idx
ON app.audit_event (tenant_id, event_time DESC);

CREATE INDEX audit_event_case_time_idx
ON app.audit_event (case_id, event_time DESC);
```

Future partition yang dibuat dengan `PARTITION OF` akan mendapatkan matching index dari parent definition. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/ddl-partitioning.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 12.2 Existing Table Besar

Gunakan staged approach:

```text id="fhy0wk"
1. List partitions
2. Build local index CONCURRENTLY per partition
3. Monitor pg_stat_progress_create_index
4. Analyze partition
5. Attach/create parent partitioned index
6. Verify pg_indexes / EXPLAIN
7. Roll out ke future partition automation
```

---

# 13. Index Existing Partitions secara Otomatis

## 13.1 Generate DDL

```sql id="wmj571"
SELECT format(
    'CREATE INDEX CONCURRENTLY IF NOT EXISTS %I ON %s (tenant_id, event_time DESC);',
    child.relname || '_tenant_time_idx',
    child.oid::regclass
) AS ddl
FROM pg_inherits i
JOIN pg_class parent ON parent.oid = i.inhparent
JOIN pg_class child  ON child.oid = i.inhrelid
JOIN pg_namespace nsp ON nsp.oid = child.relnamespace
WHERE parent.oid = 'app.audit_event'::regclass
  AND child.relname <> 'audit_event_default'
ORDER BY child.relname;
```

Output contoh:

```sql id="ssubp6"
CREATE INDEX CONCURRENTLY IF NOT EXISTS audit_event_p2026_06_tenant_time_idx
ON app.audit_event_p2026_06 (tenant_id, event_time DESC);

CREATE INDEX CONCURRENTLY IF NOT EXISTS audit_event_p2026_07_tenant_time_idx
ON app.audit_event_p2026_07 (tenant_id, event_time DESC);
```

Catatan: jalankan hasilnya satu per satu atau batch terkontrol, bukan sembarang parallel semua partition besar.

---

# 14. Verify Index Coverage per Partition

## 14.1 List index per partition

```sql id="2lvknd"
SELECT
    tablename,
    indexname,
    indexdef
FROM pg_indexes
WHERE schemaname = 'app'
  AND tablename LIKE 'audit_event_p2026_%'
ORDER BY tablename, indexname;
```

## 14.2 Find partition without tenant-time index

```sql id="3pj576"
WITH partitions AS (
    SELECT child.relname AS partition_name
    FROM pg_inherits i
    JOIN pg_class parent ON parent.oid = i.inhparent
    JOIN pg_class child  ON child.oid = i.inhrelid
    WHERE parent.oid = 'app.audit_event'::regclass
),
indexes AS (
    SELECT tablename, indexname
    FROM pg_indexes
    WHERE schemaname = 'app'
      AND indexname LIKE '%tenant_time_idx'
)
SELECT p.partition_name
FROM partitions p
LEFT JOIN indexes i ON i.tablename = p.partition_name
WHERE i.indexname IS NULL
ORDER BY p.partition_name;
```

---

# 15. Check Index Usage

Gunakan `pg_stat_user_indexes`.

```sql id="hzl1hd"
SELECT
    schemaname,
    relname AS table_name,
    indexrelname AS index_name,
    idx_scan,
    idx_tup_read,
    idx_tup_fetch
FROM pg_stat_user_indexes
WHERE schemaname = 'app'
  AND relname LIKE 'audit_event_p2026_%'
ORDER BY idx_scan ASC, relname, indexrelname;
```

Interpretasi:

| Metric | Arti |
|---|---|
| `idx_scan = 0` | Index belum pernah dipakai sejak stats reset |
| `idx_tup_read` tinggi | Banyak entry index dibaca |
| `idx_tup_fetch` tinggi | Banyak heap row diambil |
| Banyak index unused | Kandidat review/drop |
| Index baru unused | Bisa belum ada workload atau query tidak cocok |

Hati-hati: stats reset saat restart/reset stats, jadi jangan langsung drop index hanya dari `idx_scan = 0`.

---

# 16. Duplicate / Redundant Index

Contoh redundant:

```sql id="yq7pm0"
CREATE INDEX audit_event_p2026_07_tenant_idx
ON app.audit_event_p2026_07 (tenant_id);

CREATE INDEX audit_event_p2026_07_tenant_time_idx
ON app.audit_event_p2026_07 (tenant_id, event_time DESC);
```

Index `(tenant_id)` mungkin redundant jika semua query tenant juga memakai time dan planner bisa memakai prefix dari `(tenant_id, event_time DESC)`.

Tapi tidak selalu redundant:

| Situasi | `(tenant_id)` masih berguna? |
|---|---:|
| Query count semua tenant row tanpa time | Mungkin |
| FK validation | Mungkin |
| Smaller index jauh lebih murah | Mungkin |
| Semua query pakai tenant + time | Mungkin redundant |

Review dengan `EXPLAIN` dan `pg_stat_user_indexes`.

---

# 17. Hot/Warm/Cold Index Policy

Tidak semua partition harus punya index sama.

## 17.1 Hot partition

```text id="qxtmy4"
- current month/day
- write-heavy
- query-heavy
- butuh index worker/OLTP
```

Index:

```text id="1mgjgh"
tenant_time
case_time
status_created partial
maybe payload GIN if needed
```

## 17.2 Warm partition

```text id="xbbcsd"
- historical recent
- mostly read
- reporting/search
```

Index:

```text id="akek45"
tenant_time
case_time
reporting indexes
possibly BRIN
```

## 17.3 Cold partition

```text id="7lt3nq"
- old
- rarely queried
- archive candidate
```

Index:

```text id="qccptn"
minimal indexes
BRIN by time
drop expensive GIN if rarely used
```

Practical warning:

```text id="6gca9h"
Dropping index on cold partition can save storage,
but make audit/regulatory retrieval slower.
Harus sesuai SLA retrieval dan compliance requirement.
```

---

# 18. Index Strategy untuk Default Partition

Default partition harus kecil. Jangan optimize default partition sebagai storage normal.

Minimal index:

```sql id="qkvrwm"
CREATE INDEX audit_event_default_event_time_idx
ON app.audit_event_default (event_time);
```

Optional diagnostic:

```sql id="zhnr96"
CREATE INDEX audit_event_default_tenant_time_idx
ON app.audit_event_default (tenant_id, event_time DESC);
```

Tujuan index default:

```text id="oe34qd"
1. Memudahkan menemukan/memindahkan data out-of-bound
2. Mengurangi dampak query fallback
3. Mendukung cleanup
```

Bukan untuk membiarkan default partition tumbuh.

---

# 19. BRIN vs B-tree untuk Time Partition

| Faktor | B-tree | BRIN |
|---|---|---|
| Size | Lebih besar | Sangat kecil |
| Equality lookup | Bagus | Kurang presisi |
| Narrow range | Bagus | Bisa bagus jika data correlated |
| Wide range/report | Bagus tapi besar | Bagus untuk scan skipping |
| Insert overhead | Sedang | Rendah |
| Random data order | Tetap bagus | Kurang efektif |
| Append-only time order | Bagus | Sangat cocok |
| Query `ORDER BY event_time LIMIT` | Bagus | Tidak cocok untuk order seek |

Practical recommendation:

```text id="re19hr"
Untuk audit/event monthly partition:
- B-tree untuk OLTP lookup: (tenant_id, event_time), (case_id, event_time)
- BRIN untuk broad time scan/report jika partition besar dan data append-ordered
```

---

# 20. GIN JSONB Index Strategy

JSONB GIN pada partitioned audit table sering menggoda, tapi harus hati-hati.

## 20.1 Full payload GIN

```sql id="0d5az0"
CREATE INDEX audit_event_p2026_07_payload_gin_idx
ON app.audit_event_p2026_07
USING GIN (payload);
```

Cocok untuk query:

```sql id="b3dzjn"
WHERE payload @> '{"source":"case-service"}'::jsonb
```

## 20.2 Expression index untuk field spesifik

Jika query selalu field tertentu:

```sql id="ued8ad"
CREATE INDEX audit_event_p2026_07_payload_source_idx
ON app.audit_event_p2026_07 ((payload ->> 'source'));
```

Query:

```sql id="k1593a"
WHERE payload ->> 'source' = :source
```

## 20.3 Recommendation

| Query | Index |
|---|---|
| Banyak containment JSONB fleksibel | GIN payload |
| Field spesifik equality | Expression B-tree |
| Full-text di payload | GIN tsvector expression |
| Query jarang/ad hoc | Jangan index dulu |
| Reporting JSON berat | Pertimbangkan extracted columns/materialized view |

---

# 21. Index dan `ORDER BY`

PostgreSQL dapat memakai index untuk mengembalikan row dalam order tertentu, menghindari sort terpisah jika index cocok. Ini sangat penting untuk endpoint `ORDER BY ... LIMIT`.

## 21.1 Descending time query

```sql id="ifd99e"
SELECT *
FROM app.audit_event
WHERE tenant_id = :tenantId
  AND event_time >= :fromTime
  AND event_time < :toTime
ORDER BY event_time DESC
LIMIT 100;
```

Index:

```sql id="dc4n1q"
CREATE INDEX audit_event_p2026_07_tenant_time_desc_idx
ON app.audit_event_p2026_07 (tenant_id, event_time DESC);
```

Expected:

```text id="9puexj"
Limit
  -> Index Scan using audit_event_p2026_07_tenant_time_desc_idx
```

Bad smell:

```text id="sxt1dc"
Sort Method: external merge Disk: ...
```

---

# 22. Index dan `FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED`

Untuk job queue/outbox:

```sql id="g43hk0"
SELECT outbox_id
FROM app.outbox_event
WHERE status = 'PENDING'
  AND created_at >= :fromTime
  AND created_at < :toTime
ORDER BY created_at
LIMIT 100
FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED;
```

Index:

```sql id="16vr0g"
CREATE INDEX outbox_event_p2026_07_pending_created_idx
ON app.outbox_event_p2026_07 (created_at, outbox_id)
WHERE status = 'PENDING';
```

Why:

```text id="vsvuhx"
- partial index kecil
- cocok status worker
- cocok order by created_at
- mendukung batch locking
```

Jangan membuat banyak index untuk semua status kalau worker hanya ambil `PENDING`.

---

# 23. Index Build Strategy untuk New Future Partition

Untuk partition kosong:

```sql id="l39yqi"
BEGIN;

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS app.audit_event_p2026_10
PARTITION OF app.audit_event
FOR VALUES FROM ('2026-10-01 00:00:00+00')
             TO ('2026-11-01 00:00:00+00');

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS audit_event_p2026_10_tenant_time_idx
ON app.audit_event_p2026_10 (tenant_id, event_time DESC);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS audit_event_p2026_10_case_time_idx
ON app.audit_event_p2026_10 (case_id, event_time DESC);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS audit_event_p2026_10_event_time_brin_idx
ON app.audit_event_p2026_10 USING BRIN (event_time);

ANALYZE app.audit_event_p2026_10;

COMMIT;
```

Karena partition kosong, biasanya tidak perlu `CONCURRENTLY`.

---

# 24. Index Build Strategy untuk Backfill/Attach Partition

Workflow:

```text id="jblgji"
1. Create standalone staging table
2. Load data
3. Add CHECK bound
4. Create indexes on staging table
5. ANALYZE staging table
6. ATTACH PARTITION
```

SQL:

```sql id="gcwgmv"
CREATE TABLE app.audit_event_stage_2026_06 (
    LIKE app.audit_event INCLUDING DEFAULTS INCLUDING CONSTRAINTS
);

INSERT INTO app.audit_event_stage_2026_06
SELECT *
FROM app.audit_event_old
WHERE event_time >= '2026-06-01 00:00:00+00'
  AND event_time <  '2026-07-01 00:00:00+00';

ALTER TABLE app.audit_event_stage_2026_06
ADD CONSTRAINT audit_event_stage_2026_06_bound_chk
CHECK (
    event_time >= TIMESTAMPTZ '2026-06-01 00:00:00+00'
    AND event_time < TIMESTAMPTZ '2026-07-01 00:00:00+00'
);

CREATE INDEX audit_event_stage_2026_06_tenant_time_idx
ON app.audit_event_stage_2026_06 (tenant_id, event_time DESC);

CREATE INDEX audit_event_stage_2026_06_case_time_idx
ON app.audit_event_stage_2026_06 (case_id, event_time DESC);

ANALYZE app.audit_event_stage_2026_06;

ALTER TABLE app.audit_event
ATTACH PARTITION app.audit_event_stage_2026_06
FOR VALUES FROM ('2026-06-01 00:00:00+00')
             TO ('2026-07-01 00:00:00+00');
```

Dokumentasi PostgreSQL menyarankan menggunakan `CHECK` constraint yang sesuai sebelum `ATTACH PARTITION` agar validasi bisa lebih efisien dan tidak perlu scan penuh yang mahal pada table besar. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/ddl-partitioning.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 25. Index Maintenance

## 25.1 Reindex per partition

Jika index bloat/corruption/maintenance:

```sql id="4ky0be"
REINDEX INDEX CONCURRENTLY app.audit_event_p2026_07_tenant_time_idx;
```

Atau per table partition:

```sql id="f7xzrw"
REINDEX TABLE CONCURRENTLY app.audit_event_p2026_07;
```

Gunakan hati-hati dan monitor resource.

---

## 25.2 Drop unused index

```sql id="ne83t0"
DROP INDEX CONCURRENTLY IF EXISTS app.audit_event_p2025_01_payload_gin_idx;
```

Catatan:

```text id="4hbosu"
DROP INDEX CONCURRENTLY juga tidak boleh di transaction block.
```

---

# 26. Index Size Monitoring

```sql id="f0yrcn"
SELECT
    schemaname,
    tablename,
    indexname,
    pg_size_pretty(pg_relation_size(indexname::regclass)) AS index_size
FROM pg_indexes
WHERE schemaname = 'app'
  AND tablename LIKE 'audit_event_p%'
ORDER BY pg_relation_size(indexname::regclass) DESC;
```

Total index size per partition:

```sql id="7cb33t"
SELECT
    child.relname AS partition_table,
    pg_size_pretty(pg_relation_size(child.oid)) AS table_size,
    pg_size_pretty(pg_indexes_size(child.oid)) AS indexes_size,
    ROUND(
        pg_indexes_size(child.oid)::numeric
        / NULLIF(pg_relation_size(child.oid), 0),
        2
    ) AS index_to_table_ratio
FROM pg_inherits i
JOIN pg_class parent ON parent.oid = i.inhparent
JOIN pg_class child ON child.oid = i.inhrelid
WHERE parent.oid = 'app.audit_event'::regclass
ORDER BY pg_indexes_size(child.oid) DESC;
```

---

# 27. EXPLAIN Validation per Index

## 27.1 Tenant-time query

```sql id="pskwff"
EXPLAIN (ANALYZE, BUFFERS)
SELECT event_id, tenant_id, event_time, event_type
FROM app.audit_event
WHERE tenant_id = '00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000001'
  AND event_time >= TIMESTAMPTZ '2026-07-01 00:00:00+00'
  AND event_time <  TIMESTAMPTZ '2026-08-01 00:00:00+00'
ORDER BY event_time DESC
LIMIT 100;
```

Expected:

```text id="xq23xf"
Index Scan using audit_event_p2026_07_tenant_time_idx
```

## 27.2 Case-time query

```sql id="e1noz2"
EXPLAIN (ANALYZE, BUFFERS)
SELECT event_id, case_id, event_time, event_type
FROM app.audit_event
WHERE case_id = '11111111-1111-1111-1111-111111111111'
  AND event_time >= TIMESTAMPTZ '2026-07-01 00:00:00+00'
  AND event_time <  TIMESTAMPTZ '2026-08-01 00:00:00+00'
ORDER BY event_time DESC
LIMIT 100;
```

Expected:

```text id="v7mbgf"
Index Scan using audit_event_p2026_07_case_time_idx
```

## 27.3 Broad monthly aggregate

```sql id="20vzh9"
EXPLAIN (ANALYZE, BUFFERS)
SELECT event_type, count(*)
FROM app.audit_event
WHERE event_time >= TIMESTAMPTZ '2026-07-01 00:00:00+00'
  AND event_time <  TIMESTAMPTZ '2026-08-01 00:00:00+00'
GROUP BY event_type;
```

Expected bisa saja:

```text id="xhlx1l"
Seq Scan on audit_event_p2026_07
```

Ini tidak selalu buruk jika query memang membaca mayoritas partition. Jangan memaksa index untuk semua aggregate.

---

# 28. MyBatis/Jersey Integration Pattern

## 28.1 Mapper harus match index

Index:

```sql id="0daxuf"
CREATE INDEX audit_event_p2026_07_tenant_time_idx
ON app.audit_event_p2026_07 (tenant_id, event_time DESC);
```

Mapper:

```xml id="7s6les"
<select id="searchByTenantAndTime" resultMap="AuditEventResultMap">
    SELECT
        event_id,
        tenant_id,
        case_id,
        event_time,
        event_type,
        payload
    FROM app.audit_event
    WHERE tenant_id = #{tenantId, javaType=java.util.UUID, jdbcType=OTHER}
      AND event_time &gt;= #{fromTime}
      AND event_time &lt;  #{toTime}
    ORDER BY event_time DESC
    LIMIT #{limit}
</select>
```

Jika mapper query:

```xml id="qzvg5d"
WHERE case_id = #{caseId}
  AND event_time &gt;= #{fromTime}
  AND event_time &lt; #{toTime}
```

Maka butuh index berbeda:

```sql id="4kzdpx"
CREATE INDEX audit_event_p2026_07_case_time_idx
ON app.audit_event_p2026_07 (case_id, event_time DESC);
```

---

## 28.2 Hindari “one mapper to rule them all”

Buruk:

```xml id="lbl1v1"
<select id="searchAuditEventsFlexible" resultMap="AuditEventResultMap">
    SELECT *
    FROM app.audit_event
    WHERE 1 = 1
    <if test="tenantId != null">
        AND tenant_id = #{tenantId}
    </if>
    <if test="caseId != null">
        AND case_id = #{caseId}
    </if>
    <if test="eventType != null">
        AND event_type = #{eventType}
    </if>
    <if test="fromTime != null">
        AND event_time &gt;= #{fromTime}
    </if>
    <if test="toTime != null">
        AND event_time &lt; #{toTime}
    </if>
</select>
```

Lebih baik buat mapper per access pattern:

```text id="b8bkh5"
searchByTenantAndTime
searchByCaseAndTime
searchByEventTypeAndTime
searchByPayloadSourceAndTime
```

Setiap mapper punya index strategy yang jelas.

---

# 29. Index Review Checklist

| Checklist | Ya/Tidak |
|---|---|
| Query utama sudah diidentifikasi dari `pg_stat_statements` / mapper |
| Partition pruning sudah bekerja |
| Index per partition cocok dengan predicate setelah pruning |
| Composite index order sesuai equality → range/order |
| Query `ORDER BY ... LIMIT` punya index order yang cocok |
| Tidak ada function di partition key yang memaksa filter |
| Index GIN/JSONB hanya dibuat jika query sering |
| Partial index predicate cocok dengan query worker |
| Covering index tidak memasukkan kolom besar |
| Existing large indexes dibuat `CONCURRENTLY` |
| Migration tool mendukung non-transactional index build |
| Index future partition otomatis dibuat |
| Default partition punya index minimal untuk cleanup |
| Index usage dimonitor |
| Duplicate/unused index direview sebelum drop |
| EXPLAIN evidence disimpan untuk query kritikal |

---

# 30. Common Index Failure Modes

| Failure mode | Gejala | Root cause | Fix |
|---|---|---|---|
| Partition pruning berhasil tapi query lambat | Hanya 1 partition discan tapi Seq Scan besar | Index tidak cocok | Tambah index per partition |
| Index ada tapi tidak dipakai | EXPLAIN pakai Seq Scan | Table kecil/stats stale/predicate tidak cocok | ANALYZE, rewrite query, cek selectivity |
| Index Cond tidak lengkap | Banyak `Rows Removed by Filter` | Kolom order salah/function di WHERE | Rewrite index/query |
| Deployment stuck | Index build lama/blocking | Build non-concurrent pada table besar | Use `CREATE INDEX CONCURRENTLY` per partition |
| Future partition lambat | Index tidak dibuat | Automation lupa index | Parent index atau partition creation template |
| Storage membengkak | Index lebih besar dari table | Terlalu banyak index/GIN | Review usage/drop |
| Outbox worker lambat | Scan banyak processed row | Tidak ada partial index pending | Partial index `WHERE status='PENDING'` |
| Audit search lambat by case | Hanya tenant index tersedia | Access pattern berbeda | Tambah `(case_id, event_time)` |
| Reporting lambat | Index tidak membantu aggregate besar | Query baca mayoritas data | Summary/materialized view/OLAP |

---

# 31. Practical Starting Index Set

Untuk `audit_event` partitioned by monthly `event_time`:

```sql id="0r219i"
-- Query tenant timeline
CREATE INDEX audit_event_p2026_07_tenant_time_idx
ON app.audit_event_p2026_07 (tenant_id, event_time DESC);

-- Query case timeline
CREATE INDEX audit_event_p2026_07_case_time_idx
ON app.audit_event_p2026_07 (case_id, event_time DESC);

-- Query by type within time
CREATE INDEX audit_event_p2026_07_event_type_time_idx
ON app.audit_event_p2026_07 (event_type, event_time DESC);

-- Broad range scan support, optional
CREATE INDEX audit_event_p2026_07_event_time_brin_idx
ON app.audit_event_p2026_07 USING BRIN (event_time);
```

Jangan langsung tambahkan:

```sql id="219u8w"
CREATE INDEX ... USING GIN (payload);
```

kecuali query JSONB memang penting dan sering.

---

# 32. Minimal Production Rollout Plan

```text id="p0ph4d"
1. Ambil top query dari pg_stat_statements.
2. Kelompokkan access pattern.
3. Untuk setiap access pattern, tentukan index per partition.
4. Jalankan EXPLAIN baseline.
5. Build index di staging.
6. Benchmark insert/update overhead.
7. Build CONCURRENTLY per existing partition di production.
8. Monitor pg_stat_progress_create_index.
9. ANALYZE partition.
10. Jalankan EXPLAIN setelah index.
11. Tambahkan index ke future partition template.
12. Monitor pg_stat_user_indexes.
13. Review unused/duplicate index setelah workload cukup.
```

---

# Kesimpulan Seri 8

Index strategy untuk PostgreSQL partitioning harus mengikuti prinsip ini:

```text id="6j1lla"
1. Partition pruning memilih partition.
2. Index mempercepat pencarian di dalam partition.
3. Buat index berdasarkan query nyata, bukan semua kolom.
4. Untuk OLTP, B-tree composite adalah default utama.
5. Untuk broad time scan besar, BRIN bisa sangat hemat.
6. Untuk JSONB/full-text, GIN berguna tapi mahal.
7. Covering index bagus untuk read-heavy query kecil.
8. Partial index bagus untuk subset seperti PENDING outbox.
9. Existing large partition harus di-index CONCURRENTLY per partition.
10. Future partition creation harus otomatis membuat index.
11. Selalu validasi dengan EXPLAIN dan monitor index usage.
```

**Seri 8/14 selesai.**

Rekomendasi materi berikutnya: **Seri 9 - Constraint, Primary Key, Unique Key, dan Foreign Key Strategy pada Partitioned Table: limitation PostgreSQL, desain identity, referential integrity, cascade risk, dan pattern aman untuk enterprise system.**

---

**Sources:**

- [Documentation: 18: Chapter 11. Indexes](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/indexes.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com)

## Response:
7/16/2026, 8:14:21 AM

Saya lanjut ke **Seri 9/14**. Fokusnya constraint strategy: primary key, unique key, foreign key, referential integrity, cascade risk, dan pattern identity yang aman untuk partitioned table enterprise.

# Seri 9/14 - Constraint, Primary Key, Unique Key, dan Foreign Key Strategy pada Partitioned Table

## 1. Tujuan Seri Ini

Seri ini fokus ke desain integrity pada PostgreSQL partitioning:

> **Bagaimana mendesain primary key, unique key, foreign key, dan constraint agar tetap benar, scalable, dan aman untuk production?**

Partitioning sering gagal bukan karena DDL `PARTITION BY`, tetapi karena desain identity dan referential integrity tidak cocok dengan batasan PostgreSQL.

Batasan paling penting: untuk membuat `PRIMARY KEY` atau `UNIQUE` constraint pada partitioned table, kolom constraint harus mencakup semua kolom partition key, dan partition key tidak boleh berupa expression/function call. Alasannya, index unik di masing-masing partition hanya bisa menjamin uniqueness di partition itu sendiri; PostgreSQL membutuhkan partition structure untuk menjamin tidak ada duplicate lintas partition. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/ddl-partitioning.html))

---

## 2. Mental Model

```text
Partitioned Table Constraint Strategy
│
├─ Primary Key
│  ├─ Harus mencakup partition key
│  ├─ Biasanya menjadi composite key
│  └─ Berdampak ke FK dan query aplikasi
│
├─ Unique Key
│  ├─ Harus mencakup semua partition key
│  ├─ Tidak bisa global unique by non-partition key saja
│  └─ Kadang perlu registry table
│
├─ Foreign Key
│  ├─ Bisa menjaga referential integrity
│  ├─ FK column harus match referenced key
│  ├─ Referencing column tidak otomatis dibuatkan index
│  └─ Cascade/delete/update harus hati-hati
│
├─ Check / Not Null
│  ├─ Diwariskan ke partitions
│  ├─ Bisa dipakai untuk attach validation
│  └─ Berguna untuk lifecycle safety
│
└─ Operational Risk
   ├─ Lock saat add constraint
   ├─ Validate constraint pada data besar
   ├─ Cascade besar lintas partition
   ├─ Detach/drop partition dengan FK reference
   └─ Migration compatibility
```

---

## 3. Constraint Type Overview

| Constraint | Pada partitioned table | Catatan praktis |
|---|---|---|
| `NOT NULL` | Aman dan diwariskan | Bagus untuk partition key dan FK column |
| `CHECK` | Aman dan diwariskan | Dipakai untuk domain rule dan attach optimization |
| `PRIMARY KEY` | Bisa, tapi harus include partition key | Sering menjadi composite PK |
| `UNIQUE` | Bisa, tapi harus include partition key | Tidak bisa global unique by `id` saja jika partition by time |
| `FOREIGN KEY` | Bisa, tapi perlu desain key matang | FK referencing column tidak otomatis di-index |
| `EXCLUDE` | Ada batasan mirip unique | Harus include partition key dengan equality |
| Trigger constraint | Bisa, tapi hati-hati | Jangan jadikan trigger sebagai pengganti integrity utama tanpa alasan kuat |

PostgreSQL juga menyatakan bahwa `CHECK` dan `NOT NULL` constraint dari parent partitioned table selalu diwariskan ke semua partition; berbeda dengan beberapa constraint lain yang memiliki aturan khusus. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/ddl-partitioning.html))

---

# 4. Primary Key Strategy

## 4.1 Masalah Umum

Misal tabel audit dipartisi by `event_time`:

```sql
CREATE TABLE app.audit_event (
    event_id    uuid        NOT NULL,
    tenant_id   uuid        NOT NULL,
    case_id     uuid        NOT NULL,
    event_time  timestamptz NOT NULL,
    event_type  text        NOT NULL,
    payload     jsonb       NOT NULL,

    CONSTRAINT audit_event_pk
        PRIMARY KEY (event_id)
) PARTITION BY RANGE (event_time);
```

Ini **tidak valid** karena `event_time` adalah partition key tetapi tidak ikut di primary key.

PostgreSQL mensyaratkan `PRIMARY KEY` atau `UNIQUE` pada partitioned table mencakup semua partition key. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/ddl-partitioning.html))

---

## 4.2 Pattern Valid: Composite Primary Key

```sql
CREATE TABLE app.audit_event (
    event_id    uuid        NOT NULL,
    tenant_id   uuid        NOT NULL,
    case_id     uuid        NOT NULL,
    event_time  timestamptz NOT NULL,
    event_type  text        NOT NULL,
    payload     jsonb       NOT NULL,

    CONSTRAINT audit_event_pk
        PRIMARY KEY (event_id, event_time)
) PARTITION BY RANGE (event_time);
```

Implikasi:

| Area | Dampak |
|---|---|
| Identity | Row identity DB-level adalah `(event_id, event_time)` |
| FK reference | Tabel lain yang ingin FK ke audit event harus menyimpan `event_id` + `event_time` |
| API | Bisa tetap expose `eventId` sebagai public ID |
| Query | Lookup by `event_id` saja tidak cukup untuk pruning |
| Integrity | Uniqueness dijamin lintas partition karena partition key ikut |

---

## 4.3 Pattern Valid untuk Multi-tenant + Time

```sql
CREATE TABLE app.audit_event (
    event_id    uuid        NOT NULL,
    tenant_id   uuid        NOT NULL,
    event_time  timestamptz NOT NULL,
    case_id     uuid        NOT NULL,
    payload     jsonb       NOT NULL,

    CONSTRAINT audit_event_pk
        PRIMARY KEY (tenant_id, event_id, event_time)
) PARTITION BY RANGE (event_time);
```

Kapan dipakai:

```text
Jika hampir semua query punya tenant_id
dan domain identity memang tenant-scoped.
```

Namun, jangan asal memasukkan `tenant_id` ke PK jika semua FK dan aplikasi akan menjadi rumit. PK harus mencerminkan identity yang benar, bukan hanya kebutuhan index.

---

## 4.4 Pattern Valid untuk Hash Partition by Case

```sql
CREATE TABLE app.case_activity (
    activity_id    uuid        NOT NULL,
    case_id        uuid        NOT NULL,
    tenant_id      uuid        NOT NULL,
    activity_type  text        NOT NULL,
    created_at     timestamptz NOT NULL,
    payload        jsonb       NOT NULL,

    CONSTRAINT case_activity_pk
        PRIMARY KEY (activity_id, case_id)
) PARTITION BY HASH (case_id);
```

Karena partition key adalah `case_id`, primary key harus mencakup `case_id`.

---

# 5. “Global ID” vs “Database-enforced Global Uniqueness”

Ini sering membingungkan.

## 5.1 UUID Bisa Global secara Praktis, tapi Tidak Selalu DB-enforced

Misal:

```sql
event_id uuid NOT NULL
```

Jika `event_id` dibuat dengan UUID v4/v7 di aplikasi, secara praktis collision sangat kecil. Tetapi PostgreSQL **tidak akan mengizinkan** `UNIQUE(event_id)` pada table partitioned by `event_time`, karena `event_time` tidak ikut unique constraint.

Valid:

```sql
UNIQUE (event_id, event_time)
```

Tidak valid:

```sql
UNIQUE (event_id)
```

---

## 5.2 Kapan Butuh DB-enforced Global Uniqueness?

| Use case | Perlu global unique di DB? |
|---|---:|
| Audit event id untuk trace/log | Biasanya tidak wajib; UUID cukup |
| Payment transaction external reference | Sering wajib |
| Idempotency key | Sering wajib |
| Regulatory case number | Sering wajib |
| Human-facing reference number | Wajib |
| Event sourcing stream sequence | Wajib dalam stream scope |

Jika wajib global unique terhadap kolom yang bukan partition key, gunakan pattern alternatif.

---

# 6. Pattern Alternatif untuk Global Uniqueness

## 6.1 Registry Table Pattern

Buat table kecil non-partitioned untuk menjamin uniqueness.

```sql
CREATE TABLE app.audit_event_registry (
    event_id    uuid        NOT NULL,
    event_time  timestamptz NOT NULL,
    tenant_id   uuid        NOT NULL,
    created_at  timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),

    CONSTRAINT audit_event_registry_pk
        PRIMARY KEY (event_id)
);
```

Partitioned table:

```sql
CREATE TABLE app.audit_event (
    event_id    uuid        NOT NULL,
    event_time  timestamptz NOT NULL,
    tenant_id   uuid        NOT NULL,
    case_id     uuid        NOT NULL,
    event_type  text        NOT NULL,
    payload     jsonb       NOT NULL,

    CONSTRAINT audit_event_pk
        PRIMARY KEY (event_id, event_time)
) PARTITION BY RANGE (event_time);
```

Insert transaction:

```sql
BEGIN;

INSERT INTO app.audit_event_registry (
    event_id,
    event_time,
    tenant_id
) VALUES (
    :event_id,
    :event_time,
    :tenant_id
);

INSERT INTO app.audit_event (
    event_id,
    event_time,
    tenant_id,
    case_id,
    event_type,
    payload
) VALUES (
    :event_id,
    :event_time,
    :tenant_id,
    :case_id,
    :event_type,
    CAST(:payload AS jsonb)
);

COMMIT;
```

Kelebihan:

| Kelebihan | Penjelasan |
|---|---|
| Global uniqueness benar-benar DB-enforced | `PRIMARY KEY(event_id)` di registry |
| Partitioned table tetap by time | Retention dan pruning tetap bagus |
| Registry kecil | Hanya identity metadata |
| Bisa dipakai untuk idempotency | Insert duplicate gagal cepat |

Kekurangan:

| Kekurangan | Penjelasan |
|---|---|
| Extra write | Dua insert dalam transaction |
| Extra table | Operasional bertambah |
| Retention perlu dipikirkan | Registry tidak boleh di-drop sembarang |
| FK ke event tetap kompleks | Harus pilih referential model |

---

## 6.2 Idempotency Key Registry

Untuk event ingestion/outbox/inbox:

```sql
CREATE TABLE app.idempotency_key_registry (
    tenant_id        uuid        NOT NULL,
    idempotency_key  text        NOT NULL,
    request_hash     text        NOT NULL,
    first_seen_at    timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),

    CONSTRAINT idempotency_key_registry_pk
        PRIMARY KEY (tenant_id, idempotency_key)
);
```

Lalu partitioned event table tetap punya PK yang include partition key:

```sql
CREATE TABLE app.inbox_event (
    inbox_event_id   uuid        NOT NULL,
    tenant_id        uuid        NOT NULL,
    received_at      timestamptz NOT NULL,
    idempotency_key  text        NOT NULL,
    payload          jsonb       NOT NULL,

    CONSTRAINT inbox_event_pk
        PRIMARY KEY (inbox_event_id, received_at)
) PARTITION BY RANGE (received_at);
```

Pattern ini sangat berguna untuk microservice event ingestion.

---

## 6.3 Tenant-scoped Uniqueness

Kalau uniqueness hanya perlu per tenant dan partition key juga tenant, hash/list partition by tenant bisa lebih sederhana.

```sql
CREATE TABLE app.tenant_document (
    tenant_id      uuid        NOT NULL,
    document_no    text        NOT NULL,
    document_id    uuid        NOT NULL,
    created_at     timestamptz NOT NULL,

    CONSTRAINT tenant_document_pk
        PRIMARY KEY (tenant_id, document_no)
) PARTITION BY HASH (tenant_id);
```

Di sini `PRIMARY KEY(tenant_id, document_no)` valid karena partition key `tenant_id` ikut.

---

# 7. Unique Key Strategy

## 7.1 Unique External Reference pada Time-partitioned Table

Misal external reference harus unique per tenant:

```sql
CREATE TABLE app.transaction_history (
    transaction_id      uuid        NOT NULL,
    tenant_id           uuid        NOT NULL,
    business_date       date        NOT NULL,
    external_reference  text        NOT NULL,
    amount              numeric(19,4) NOT NULL,

    CONSTRAINT transaction_history_pk
        PRIMARY KEY (transaction_id, business_date),

    CONSTRAINT transaction_history_external_ref_uk
        UNIQUE (tenant_id, external_reference, business_date)
) PARTITION BY RANGE (business_date);
```

Ini valid karena `business_date` ikut unique constraint.

Namun, perhatikan semantics:

```text
UNIQUE (tenant_id, external_reference, business_date)
berarti external_reference bisa berulang di business_date berbeda.
```

Jika domain mengharuskan `tenant_id + external_reference` unique lintas seluruh waktu, constraint ini **tidak cukup**. Pakai registry table:

```sql
CREATE TABLE app.transaction_reference_registry (
    tenant_id           uuid NOT NULL,
    external_reference  text NOT NULL,
    transaction_id      uuid NOT NULL,
    business_date       date NOT NULL,
    created_at          timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),

    CONSTRAINT transaction_reference_registry_pk
        PRIMARY KEY (tenant_id, external_reference)
);
```

---

## 7.2 Decision Rule

| Requirement | Pattern |
|---|---|
| Unique hanya dalam partition window | Unique include partition key |
| Unique lintas semua waktu | Registry table |
| Unique per tenant dan partition by tenant | PK/unique include tenant |
| Unique by generated UUID saja | UUID practical uniqueness, atau registry jika wajib DB-enforced |
| Unique external business reference | Registry lebih aman |

---

# 8. Foreign Key Strategy

## 8.1 Dasar Foreign Key

Foreign key memastikan nilai di referencing column cocok dengan row di referenced table. PostgreSQL menjelaskan FK bisa mencakup satu atau beberapa kolom, dan jumlah serta tipe kolom referencing harus cocok dengan referenced columns. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/ddl-constraints.html))

Contoh sederhana:

```sql
CREATE TABLE app.case_file (
    case_id     uuid NOT NULL,
    tenant_id   uuid NOT NULL,
    created_at  timestamptz NOT NULL,

    CONSTRAINT case_file_pk
        PRIMARY KEY (case_id)
);

CREATE TABLE app.case_comment (
    comment_id  uuid NOT NULL,
    case_id     uuid NOT NULL,
    created_at  timestamptz NOT NULL,
    comment     text NOT NULL,

    CONSTRAINT case_comment_pk
        PRIMARY KEY (comment_id, created_at),

    CONSTRAINT case_comment_case_fk
        FOREIGN KEY (case_id)
        REFERENCES app.case_file (case_id)
) PARTITION BY RANGE (created_at);
```

Ini valid karena referencing table partitioned by `created_at`, tetapi FK mengacu ke non-partitioned `case_file`.

---

## 8.2 FK ke Partitioned Table dengan Composite PK

Misal `audit_event` punya PK:

```sql
PRIMARY KEY (event_id, event_time)
```

Maka tabel yang ingin FK ke audit event harus menyimpan dua kolom:

```sql
CREATE TABLE app.audit_event_attachment (
    attachment_id  uuid        NOT NULL,
    event_id       uuid        NOT NULL,
    event_time     timestamptz NOT NULL,
    file_id        uuid        NOT NULL,
    created_at     timestamptz NOT NULL,

    CONSTRAINT audit_event_attachment_pk
        PRIMARY KEY (attachment_id, created_at),

    CONSTRAINT audit_event_attachment_event_fk
        FOREIGN KEY (event_id, event_time)
        REFERENCES app.audit_event (event_id, event_time)
) PARTITION BY RANGE (created_at);
```

Practical consequence:

| Dampak | Penjelasan |
|---|---|
| FK lebih panjang | Child table perlu simpan partition key referenced row |
| Query join lebih verbose | Join by `(event_id, event_time)` |
| API DTO lebih kompleks | Perlu membawa eventTime jika reference DB-level |
| Integrity lebih kuat | FK valid lintas partitions |

---

## 8.3 Jangan FK ke Audit/Event History Jika Tidak Perlu

Untuk audit/event table, sering kali lebih baik audit event menjadi **append-only evidence log**, bukan parent relational entity yang banyak direferensikan.

Hindari:

```text
business table → FK ke audit_event
```

Lebih baik:

```text
business table menyimpan business entity ID
audit_event menyimpan case_id / aggregate_id / correlation_id
```

Alasan:

| Alasan | Dampak |
|---|---|
| Audit event sangat besar | FK/cascade/validation mahal |
| Retention/archive kompleks | Row lain bisa menghalangi detach/drop |
| Audit log append-only | Tidak ideal sebagai relational parent |
| Query audit biasanya by entity + time | Tidak butuh FK ke event row |

---

# 9. Index untuk Foreign Key

PostgreSQL tidak otomatis membuat index pada referencing columns foreign key. Dokumentasi resmi menyatakan index pada referencing columns sering berguna, tetapi declaration FK tidak otomatis membuatnya karena kebutuhan index bervariasi. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/ddl-constraints.html))

## 9.1 Contoh FK tanpa index

```sql
CONSTRAINT case_comment_case_fk
    FOREIGN KEY (case_id)
    REFERENCES app.case_file (case_id)
```

Tambahkan index di partition:

```sql
CREATE INDEX case_comment_p2026_07_case_id_idx
ON app.case_comment_p2026_07 (case_id);
```

Jika query umum by case + created_at:

```sql
CREATE INDEX case_comment_p2026_07_case_created_idx
ON app.case_comment_p2026_07 (case_id, created_at DESC);
```

---

## 9.2 Composite FK Index

Jika FK:

```sql
FOREIGN KEY (event_id, event_time)
REFERENCES app.audit_event (event_id, event_time)
```

Index referencing side:

```sql
CREATE INDEX audit_event_attachment_p2026_07_event_fk_idx
ON app.audit_event_attachment_p2026_07 (event_id, event_time);
```

Kalau sering query attachment by event:

```sql
CREATE INDEX audit_event_attachment_p2026_07_event_created_idx
ON app.audit_event_attachment_p2026_07 (event_id, event_time, created_at DESC);
```

---

# 10. Cascade Risk

## 10.1 FK Actions

PostgreSQL mendukung beberapa aksi FK seperti `NO ACTION`, `RESTRICT`, `CASCADE`, `SET NULL`, dan `SET DEFAULT`. Default-nya adalah `NO ACTION`; `CASCADE` akan menghapus row referencing ketika row referenced dihapus. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/ddl-constraints.html))

Contoh:

```sql
CONSTRAINT case_comment_case_fk
    FOREIGN KEY (case_id)
    REFERENCES app.case_file (case_id)
    ON DELETE CASCADE
```

Untuk partitioned large table, `CASCADE` harus sangat hati-hati.

---

## 10.2 Kapan `ON DELETE CASCADE` Berbahaya

| Kondisi | Risiko |
|---|---|
| Referencing table sangat besar | Delete parent bisa menghapus jutaan row |
| Referencing table partitioned by time | Cascade menyentuh banyak partitions |
| FK column tidak di-index | Cascade check/delete lambat |
| Audit/history table ikut cascade | Evidence bisa hilang |
| Delete dilakukan dari API biasa | Incident data loss/performance |
| Retention job memakai parent delete | Bisa cascade tak terduga |

Untuk regulatory/audit system, default yang lebih aman:

```sql
ON DELETE RESTRICT
```

atau explicit no cascade:

```sql
ON DELETE NO ACTION
```

---

## 10.3 Recommended Cascade Policy

| Table type | FK delete action |
|---|---|
| Master data reference | `RESTRICT` / `NO ACTION` |
| Case master → audit event | Hindari cascade |
| Case master → comments/tasks | Tergantung domain, biasanya soft delete |
| Outbox/inbox | Biasanya tidak FK cascade |
| Audit/event history | Jangan cascade delete |
| Join table kecil | `CASCADE` bisa valid |
| Temporary/session child data | `CASCADE` bisa valid |

---

# 11. Constraint Deployment pada Tabel Besar

## 11.1 Menambah FK Bisa Mengunci

PostgreSQL `ALTER TABLE ADD FOREIGN KEY` membutuhkan lock pada referencing dan referenced table. Dokumentasi `ALTER TABLE` menyebut `ADD FOREIGN KEY` memerlukan `SHARE ROW EXCLUSIVE` lock pada table tempat constraint dideklarasikan dan juga pada referenced table. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-altertable.html))

Untuk tabel besar/live, pakai `NOT VALID` lalu `VALIDATE CONSTRAINT`.

---

## 11.2 Pattern Aman: `NOT VALID`

```sql
ALTER TABLE app.case_comment
ADD CONSTRAINT case_comment_case_fk
FOREIGN KEY (case_id)
REFERENCES app.case_file (case_id)
NOT VALID;
```

Setelah itu, constraint berlaku untuk row baru, tetapi existing data belum divalidasi.

Lalu validasi saat window yang aman:

```sql
ALTER TABLE app.case_comment
VALIDATE CONSTRAINT case_comment_case_fk;
```

PostgreSQL documentation menjelaskan bahwa `NOT VALID` berguna ketika table berisi pre-existing data; constraint akan mencegah violation baru, sedangkan existing data bisa diperbaiki sampai `VALIDATE CONSTRAINT` berhasil. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-altertable.html))

---

## 11.3 Pattern Validasi per Partition

Untuk partitioned child besar, kadang lebih aman melakukan constraint/index management per partition, terutama jika migration tool dan lock profile perlu dikontrol.

```sql
ALTER TABLE app.case_comment_p2026_07
ADD CONSTRAINT case_comment_p2026_07_case_fk
FOREIGN KEY (case_id)
REFERENCES app.case_file (case_id)
NOT VALID;

ALTER TABLE app.case_comment_p2026_07
VALIDATE CONSTRAINT case_comment_p2026_07_case_fk;
```

Namun, konsekuensinya metadata constraint bisa tidak sesederhana parent-level constraint. Pilih berdasarkan kebutuhan operasional dan governance.

---

# 12. Attach / Detach Partition dan Constraint

## 12.1 Attach Partition

Saat `ATTACH PARTITION`, PostgreSQL akan memeriksa kecocokan kolom, `NOT NULL`, dan `CHECK` constraint. Dokumentasi `ALTER TABLE` menyatakan bahwa `UNIQUE` dan `PRIMARY KEY` dari parent akan dibuat di partition jika belum ada; saat attach table regular, full scan bisa terjadi untuk memvalidasi partition constraint kecuali ada `CHECK` constraint yang valid. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-altertable.html))

Pattern:

```sql
CREATE TABLE app.audit_event_stage_2026_06 (
    LIKE app.audit_event INCLUDING DEFAULTS INCLUDING CONSTRAINTS
);

ALTER TABLE app.audit_event_stage_2026_06
ADD CONSTRAINT audit_event_stage_2026_06_bound_chk
CHECK (
    event_time >= TIMESTAMPTZ '2026-06-01 00:00:00+00'
    AND event_time < TIMESTAMPTZ '2026-07-01 00:00:00+00'
);

ALTER TABLE app.audit_event
ATTACH PARTITION app.audit_event_stage_2026_06
FOR VALUES FROM ('2026-06-01 00:00:00+00')
             TO ('2026-07-01 00:00:00+00');
```

---

## 12.2 Detach Partition dengan FK Reference

`DETACH PARTITION` membuat partition menjadi standalone table. PostgreSQL documentation menyebut saat detach, index yang attached ke parent index dilepas, trigger clone dihapus, dan `SHARE` lock diperoleh pada table yang mereferensikan partitioned table melalui FK. `DETACH PARTITION CONCURRENTLY` memakai lock lebih rendah tetapi tidak bisa dijalankan di transaction block dan tidak boleh digunakan jika partitioned table memiliki default partition. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-altertable.html))

Practical implication:

```text
Jika banyak table punya FK ke parent partitioned table,
retention detach/drop bisa terkena locking dan dependency risk.
```

Karena itu, jangan mudah membuat FK ke table history/audit yang direncanakan sering detach/drop.

---

# 13. Check Constraint Strategy

## 13.1 Domain Check di Parent

```sql
CREATE TABLE app.audit_event (
    event_id    uuid        NOT NULL,
    event_time  timestamptz NOT NULL,
    event_type  text        NOT NULL,
    payload     jsonb       NOT NULL,

    CONSTRAINT audit_event_type_not_blank_chk
        CHECK (length(trim(event_type)) > 0),

    CONSTRAINT audit_event_payload_object_chk
        CHECK (jsonb_typeof(payload) = 'object'),

    CONSTRAINT audit_event_pk
        PRIMARY KEY (event_id, event_time)
) PARTITION BY RANGE (event_time);
```

Bagus untuk invariant yang berlaku semua partition.

---

## 13.2 Bound Check untuk Staging Attach

```sql
ALTER TABLE app.audit_event_stage_2026_06
ADD CONSTRAINT audit_event_stage_2026_06_bound_chk
CHECK (
    event_time >= TIMESTAMPTZ '2026-06-01 00:00:00+00'
    AND event_time < TIMESTAMPTZ '2026-07-01 00:00:00+00'
);
```

Ini bukan domain constraint permanen utama, melainkan operational helper agar attach bisa lebih aman/efisien.

---

## 13.3 Constraint untuk Immutable Semantics

PostgreSQL tidak punya built-in immutable column constraint. Untuk partition key, secara desain sebaiknya jangan di-update. Jika perlu guardrail, bisa pakai trigger, tetapi hati-hati karena trigger menambah complexity.

Contoh trigger guard:

```sql
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION app.prevent_event_time_update()
RETURNS trigger
LANGUAGE plpgsql
AS $$
BEGIN
    IF NEW.event_time IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.event_time THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'event_time is immutable';
    END IF;

    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$;

CREATE TRIGGER audit_event_prevent_event_time_update_trg
BEFORE UPDATE ON app.audit_event
FOR EACH ROW
EXECUTE FUNCTION app.prevent_event_time_update();
```

Catatan: PostgreSQL menyatakan `BEFORE ROW` triggers pada `INSERT` tidak boleh mengubah partition tujuan final row pada partitioned table. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/ddl-partitioning.html))

---

# 14. Identity Design Patterns

## 14.1 Pattern A - Append-only Event History

```sql
CREATE TABLE app.audit_event (
    event_id    uuid        NOT NULL,
    event_time  timestamptz NOT NULL,
    tenant_id   uuid        NOT NULL,
    case_id     uuid        NOT NULL,
    event_type  text        NOT NULL,
    payload     jsonb       NOT NULL,

    CONSTRAINT audit_event_pk
        PRIMARY KEY (event_id, event_time)
) PARTITION BY RANGE (event_time);
```

Use when:

```text
- event_id generated UUID
- global uniqueness practical, not necessarily DB-enforced by event_id alone
- query always by time range
- no many FKs to audit_event
```

---

## 14.2 Pattern B - Transaction with External Reference Registry

```sql
CREATE TABLE app.transaction_reference_registry (
    tenant_id           uuid NOT NULL,
    external_reference  text NOT NULL,
    transaction_id      uuid NOT NULL,
    business_date       date NOT NULL,
    created_at          timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),

    CONSTRAINT transaction_reference_registry_pk
        PRIMARY KEY (tenant_id, external_reference)
);
```

Partitioned history:

```sql
CREATE TABLE app.transaction_history (
    transaction_id      uuid        NOT NULL,
    tenant_id           uuid        NOT NULL,
    external_reference  text        NOT NULL,
    business_date       date        NOT NULL,
    amount              numeric(19,4) NOT NULL,
    status              text        NOT NULL,

    CONSTRAINT transaction_history_pk
        PRIMARY KEY (transaction_id, business_date)
) PARTITION BY RANGE (business_date);
```

Use when:

```text
- external_reference harus unique lintas waktu
- history table tetap perlu partition by business_date
```

---

## 14.3 Pattern C - Tenant-scoped Master Table Hash Partition

```sql
CREATE TABLE app.tenant_case_file (
    tenant_id     uuid        NOT NULL,
    case_id       uuid        NOT NULL,
    case_number   text        NOT NULL,
    created_at    timestamptz NOT NULL,

    CONSTRAINT tenant_case_file_pk
        PRIMARY KEY (tenant_id, case_id),

    CONSTRAINT tenant_case_file_case_number_uk
        UNIQUE (tenant_id, case_number)
) PARTITION BY HASH (tenant_id);
```

Use when:

```text
- identity tenant-scoped
- query always by tenant
- partition key tenant_id naturally belongs in PK/unique
```

---

## 14.4 Pattern D - Non-partitioned Master, Partitioned Children

Ini sering paling bersih.

```sql
CREATE TABLE app.case_file (
    case_id      uuid        NOT NULL,
    tenant_id    uuid        NOT NULL,
    case_number  text        NOT NULL,
    created_at   timestamptz NOT NULL,

    CONSTRAINT case_file_pk
        PRIMARY KEY (case_id),

    CONSTRAINT case_file_tenant_case_number_uk
        UNIQUE (tenant_id, case_number)
);
```

Partitioned child/history:

```sql
CREATE TABLE app.case_event (
    event_id    uuid        NOT NULL,
    case_id     uuid        NOT NULL,
    event_time  timestamptz NOT NULL,
    event_type  text        NOT NULL,
    payload     jsonb       NOT NULL,

    CONSTRAINT case_event_pk
        PRIMARY KEY (event_id, event_time),

    CONSTRAINT case_event_case_fk
        FOREIGN KEY (case_id)
        REFERENCES app.case_file (case_id)
        ON DELETE RESTRICT
) PARTITION BY RANGE (event_time);
```

Kelebihan:

| Kelebihan | Penjelasan |
|---|---|
| Master identity sederhana | `case_id` tetap PK biasa |
| History bisa partition by time | Retention/pruning bagus |
| FK child → master sederhana | Hanya `case_id` |
| Retention child bisa dikontrol | Selama tidak ada FK ke child |

Ini biasanya pattern paling aman untuk case management/regulatory systems.

---

# 15. Anti-patterns

## 15.1 Time-partitioned Table dengan Business FK ke Row History

Buruk:

```text
case_decision.decision_event_id → audit_event.event_id/event_time
```

Masalah:

| Problem | Dampak |
|---|---|
| FK composite | Semua referencer perlu event_time |
| Retention sulit | Event tidak bisa detach/drop jika direferensikan |
| Query rumit | Join harus by composite key |
| Audit log jadi parent entity | Salah boundary domain |

Lebih baik:

```text
case_decision references case_file / decision table
audit_event records decision_id/case_id as evidence metadata
```

---

## 15.2 Cascade Delete ke History

Buruk:

```sql
FOREIGN KEY (case_id)
REFERENCES app.case_file (case_id)
ON DELETE CASCADE
```

pada `case_event` atau `audit_event`.

Risiko:

```text
Menghapus case bisa menghapus audit evidence.
```

Untuk regulatory system, ini biasanya tidak dapat diterima.

Gunakan:

```sql
ON DELETE RESTRICT
```

atau soft delete di master:

```sql
closed_at timestamptz
deleted_at timestamptz
```

---

## 15.3 Unique Constraint yang Semantics-nya Salah

```sql
UNIQUE (tenant_id, external_reference, business_date)
```

Jika requirement sebenarnya:

```text
external_reference unique per tenant sepanjang masa
```

Maka constraint di atas salah karena reference bisa muncul lagi di tanggal berbeda.

Gunakan registry table.

---

# 16. Java/Jersey/MyBatis Impact

## 16.1 Composite PK DTO

Jika table memakai composite PK:

```java
public record AuditEventKey(
    UUID eventId,
    OffsetDateTime eventTime
) {}
```

Mapper:

```xml
<select id="findAuditEventByKey" resultMap="AuditEventResultMap">
    SELECT
        event_id,
        event_time,
        tenant_id,
        case_id,
        event_type,
        payload
    FROM app.audit_event
    WHERE event_id = #{eventId}
      AND event_time = #{eventTime}
</select>
```

Namun, untuk pruning dan performance, lebih baik lookup tetap membawa range:

```xml
<select id="findAuditEventInRange" resultMap="AuditEventResultMap">
    SELECT
        event_id,
        event_time,
        tenant_id,
        case_id,
        event_type,
        payload
    FROM app.audit_event
    WHERE event_id = #{eventId}
      AND event_time &gt;= #{fromTime}
      AND event_time &lt;  #{toTime}
</select>
```

---

## 16.2 Registry Insert Transaction di Service Layer

```java
public void appendAuditEvent(AuditEvent event) {
    sqlSessionFactory.openSession(false);

    try (SqlSession session = sqlSessionFactory.openSession(false)) {
        AuditEventMapper mapper = session.getMapper(AuditEventMapper.class);

        mapper.insertRegistry(
            event.eventId(),
            event.eventTime(),
            event.tenantId()
        );

        mapper.insertAuditEvent(event);

        session.commit();
    } catch (PersistenceException ex) {
        session.rollback();
        throw ex;
    }
}
```

Mapper:

```xml
<insert id="insertRegistry">
    INSERT INTO app.audit_event_registry (
        event_id,
        event_time,
        tenant_id
    )
    VALUES (
        #{eventId},
        #{eventTime},
        #{tenantId}
    )
</insert>

<insert id="insertAuditEvent">
    INSERT INTO app.audit_event (
        event_id,
        event_time,
        tenant_id,
        case_id,
        event_type,
        payload
    )
    VALUES (
        #{eventId},
        #{eventTime},
        #{tenantId},
        #{caseId},
        #{eventType},
        CAST(#{payloadJson} AS jsonb)
    )
</insert>
```

---

## 16.3 Handling Unique Violation

PostgreSQL SQLSTATE untuk unique violation adalah `23505`.

Service-level pattern:

```java
try {
    mapper.insertRegistry(eventId, eventTime, tenantId);
    mapper.insertAuditEvent(event);
    session.commit();
} catch (PersistenceException ex) {
    if (isUniqueViolation(ex)) {
        throw new ConflictException("Duplicate event id or idempotency key");
    }
    throw ex;
}
```

Practical rule:

```text
Jangan ubah unique violation menjadi 500.
Untuk idempotency/reference duplicate, return 409 Conflict atau idempotent success sesuai contract.
```

---

## 16.4 Composite FK Mapper Join

```xml
<select id="findAttachmentsByEvent" resultMap="AttachmentResultMap">
    SELECT
        attachment_id,
        event_id,
        event_time,
        file_id,
        created_at
    FROM app.audit_event_attachment
    WHERE event_id = #{eventId}
      AND event_time = #{eventTime}
    ORDER BY created_at DESC
</select>
```

Index:

```sql
CREATE INDEX audit_event_attachment_p2026_07_event_created_idx
ON app.audit_event_attachment_p2026_07
(event_id, event_time, created_at DESC);
```

---

# 17. Constraint Validation Toolkit

## 17.1 Find constraints on parent/partitions

```sql
SELECT
    conrelid::regclass AS table_name,
    conname,
    contype,
    convalidated,
    pg_get_constraintdef(oid) AS constraint_def
FROM pg_constraint
WHERE conrelid IN (
    SELECT inhrelid
    FROM pg_inherits
    WHERE inhparent = 'app.audit_event'::regclass
    UNION
    SELECT 'app.audit_event'::regclass
)
ORDER BY table_name::text, conname;
```

`contype` values commonly:

```text
p = primary key
u = unique
f = foreign key
c = check
```

---

## 17.2 Find invalid constraints

```sql
SELECT
    conrelid::regclass AS table_name,
    conname,
    contype,
    pg_get_constraintdef(oid) AS constraint_def
FROM pg_constraint
WHERE convalidated = false
ORDER BY table_name::text, conname;
```

---

## 17.3 Find FK without referencing-side index

Heuristic query for single-column FK:

```sql
WITH fk AS (
    SELECT
        conrelid,
        conname,
        unnest(conkey) AS attnum
    FROM pg_constraint
    WHERE contype = 'f'
),
fk_cols AS (
    SELECT
        fk.conrelid,
        fk.conname,
        a.attname
    FROM fk
    JOIN pg_attribute a
      ON a.attrelid = fk.conrelid
     AND a.attnum = fk.attnum
),
idx_cols AS (
    SELECT
        i.indrelid,
        a.attname
    FROM pg_index i
    JOIN pg_attribute a
      ON a.attrelid = i.indrelid
     AND a.attnum = ANY(i.indkey)
)
SELECT
    fk_cols.conrelid::regclass AS table_name,
    fk_cols.conname,
    fk_cols.attname AS fk_column
FROM fk_cols
LEFT JOIN idx_cols
  ON idx_cols.indrelid = fk_cols.conrelid
 AND idx_cols.attname = fk_cols.attname
WHERE idx_cols.attname IS NULL
ORDER BY table_name::text, conname;
```

Catatan: ini heuristic sederhana; composite FK perlu analisis urutan kolom index.

---

# 18. Constraint Design Review Checklist

| Checklist | Ya/Tidak |
|---|---|
| Partition key ikut dalam `PRIMARY KEY` |
| Partition key ikut dalam semua `UNIQUE` constraint parent |
| Tidak ada unique requirement global yang salah dimodelkan |
| Registry table dipakai jika perlu global uniqueness lintas partition |
| FK ke partitioned history table benar-benar diperlukan |
| FK composite tidak membebani API/model terlalu besar |
| Referencing-side FK columns punya index |
| `ON DELETE CASCADE` tidak dipakai pada audit/history evidence |
| Retention/detach tidak terhalang FK dependency |
| Constraint addition memakai `NOT VALID` untuk table besar |
| `VALIDATE CONSTRAINT` dijadwalkan dengan aman |
| Check constraint parent merepresentasikan invariant domain |
| Check constraint staging dipakai sebelum attach |
| Default partition dan attach workflow mempertimbangkan constraint |
| Error handling Java membedakan unique/FK violation dari system error |

---

# 19. Recommended Patterns per Table Type

| Table type | PK pattern | Unique pattern | FK pattern |
|---|---|---|---|
| `case_file` master | `PRIMARY KEY(case_id)` | `UNIQUE(tenant_id, case_number)` | Non-partitioned master recommended |
| `audit_event` | `PRIMARY KEY(event_id, event_time)` | Registry if global uniqueness required | Avoid being referenced |
| `case_event` | `PRIMARY KEY(event_id, event_time)` | Usually no business unique | FK `case_id → case_file` |
| `outbox_event` | `PRIMARY KEY(outbox_id, created_at)` | Idempotency registry | Avoid FK cascade |
| `inbox_event` | `PRIMARY KEY(inbox_id, received_at)` | Idempotency registry | Avoid FK cascade |
| `transaction_history` | `PRIMARY KEY(transaction_id, business_date)` | External ref registry | FK to account/master as needed |
| `workflow_task_history` | `PRIMARY KEY(task_history_id, started_at)` | Usually none | FK to case/process master if stable |
| `tenant_document` hash by tenant | `PRIMARY KEY(tenant_id, document_id)` | `UNIQUE(tenant_id, document_no)` | Tenant-scoped FK |

---

# 20. Failure Modes

| Failure mode | Gejala | Root cause | Pencegahan |
|---|---|---|---|
| PK creation gagal | Error partition key missing | PK tidak include partition key | Composite PK |
| Unique semantics salah | Duplicate muncul lintas bulan | Unique include date padahal requirement global | Registry table |
| FK terlalu berat | Delete/update lambat | FK column tidak di-index | Index referencing side |
| Cascade incident | Data history hilang | `ON DELETE CASCADE` pada audit/history | Use `RESTRICT`/soft delete |
| Detach gagal/berisiko | Lock/dependency issue | Table lain FK ke partitioned parent/history | Hindari FK ke history |
| Migration blocking | App latency saat add FK | Add constraint langsung validasi besar | `NOT VALID` + validate later |
| FK composite menyebar | Banyak table simpan `event_time` | Referencing partitioned PK | Hindari FK ke event row |
| Registry tidak dibersihkan | Registry tumbuh tak terkendali | No lifecycle policy | Retention registry policy |
| Constraint invalid terlupakan | `convalidated=false` lama | Validate step tidak jalan | Monitoring invalid constraints |
| API error 500 | Unique/FK violation tidak dimapping | Exception handling buruk | Map SQLSTATE ke 409/400 |

---

# 21. Practical Recommendation untuk Enterprise Regulatory System

Untuk sistem case management/regulatory lifecycle:

```text
1. Master entity seperti case_file sebaiknya tidak dipartisi dulu kecuali benar-benar perlu.
2. History/event/audit table dipartisi by time.
3. History table boleh FK ke master, tetapi master jangan cascade delete ke history.
4. Jangan banyak table FK ke audit_event/history row.
5. Gunakan registry table untuk idempotency/external reference/global uniqueness.
6. Hindari unique constraint yang seolah global padahal hanya unique per partition key.
7. Semua FK referencing column harus di-index.
8. Tambahkan constraint besar dengan NOT VALID lalu VALIDATE.
9. Retention/detach harus dicek terhadap FK dependency.
10. Error handling aplikasi harus paham unique violation dan FK violation.
```

---

# Kesimpulan Seri 9

Constraint strategy pada partitioned table adalah soal **integrity boundary**.

Rule paling penting:

```text
1. PRIMARY KEY/UNIQUE pada partitioned table harus include partition key.
2. Jika butuh global uniqueness by non-partition key, gunakan registry table.
3. Composite PK akan berdampak ke FK, DTO, mapper, dan query.
4. Jangan membuat audit/history table menjadi parent relational entity jika retention penting.
5. FK referencing column tidak otomatis di-index; buat index sendiri.
6. Hindari ON DELETE CASCADE pada audit/history/regulatory evidence.
7. Gunakan NOT VALID + VALIDATE CONSTRAINT untuk table besar.
8. Desain constraint harus konsisten dengan lifecycle partition: attach, detach, archive, drop.
```

**Seri 9/14 selesai.**

Rekomendasi materi berikutnya: **Seri 10 - Write Path & Concurrency: insert routing, update partition key movement, hot current partition, locking, batch insert, COPY, `FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED`, dan failure mode write-heavy partitioned table.**

---

**Sources:**

- [PostgreSQL: Documentation: 18: 5.12. Table Partitioning](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/ddl-partitioning.html)

## Response:
7/16/2026, 8:17:05 AM

# Seri 10/14 - Write Path & Concurrency: Insert Routing, Update Partition Key Movement, Hot Partition, Locking, Batch Insert, COPY, dan `SKIP LOCKED`

## 1. Tujuan Seri Ini

Seri ini fokus ke sisi **write path** pada PostgreSQL partitioning:

> **Apa yang terjadi saat aplikasi insert/update/delete ke partitioned table, bagaimana concurrency bekerja, dan failure mode apa yang harus diantisipasi pada workload tulis tinggi?**

Pada read path, kita bicara partition pruning. Pada write path, fokusnya berbeda:

```text id="o1xpeo"
Write path partitioning
│
├─ Insert routing
│  ├─ Aplikasi insert ke parent
│  ├─ PostgreSQL route ke child partition
│  └─ Gagal jika tidak ada matching partition/default
│
├─ Update behavior
│  ├─ Update normal tetap di partition asal
│  ├─ Update partition key bisa memindahkan row
│  └─ Row movement punya cost dan concurrency risk
│
├─ Hot partition
│  ├─ Current day/month menjadi write hotspot
│  ├─ Index pada hot partition ikut panas
│  └─ Autovacuum/statistics perlu perhatian
│
├─ Bulk write
│  ├─ batch insert
│  ├─ COPY
│  ├─ staging table
│  └─ attach partition
│
├─ Queue/concurrency pattern
│  ├─ FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED
│  ├─ worker sharding
│  ├─ retry/failure state
│  └─ partial index
│
└─ Locking & operations
   ├─ DDL locks
   ├─ attach/detach locks
   ├─ index build
   ├─ retention job
   └─ migration safety
```

PostgreSQL mendukung declarative partitioning, dan insert ke parent table akan diarahkan ke partition yang cocok berdasarkan partition key. Jika tidak ada partition yang cocok, insert akan gagal kecuali ada default partition. PostgreSQL juga menyebut bahwa update terhadap partition key bisa memindahkan row ke partition lain jika row tidak lagi cocok dengan bound partition asal. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/ddl-partitioning.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 2. Insert Routing

## 2.1 Prinsip Dasar

Aplikasi sebaiknya selalu insert ke **parent table**, bukan child partition.

```sql id="dlh3ny"
INSERT INTO app.audit_event (
    event_id,
    tenant_id,
    case_id,
    event_time,
    event_type,
    payload
)
VALUES (
    gen_random_uuid(),
    '00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000001',
    '11111111-1111-1111-1111-111111111111',
    TIMESTAMPTZ '2026-07-16 10:00:00+00',
    'CASE_SUBMITTED',
    '{"source":"case-service"}'::jsonb
);
```

Jika table dipartisi by `event_time`, PostgreSQL akan route row ke partition yang bound-nya mencakup `2026-07-16`.

```text id="z3cxnl"
app.audit_event
└─ app.audit_event_p2026_07
```

## 2.2 Jangan Insert ke Child dari Aplikasi

Buruk:

```sql id="xnp9uq"
INSERT INTO app.audit_event_p2026_07 (...)
VALUES (...);
```

Kenapa buruk:

| Problem | Dampak |
|---|---|
| Aplikasi tahu physical layout | Coupling tinggi |
| Partition rotation mengubah aplikasi | Deployment risk |
| Boundary bug pindah ke aplikasi | Data bisa salah masuk |
| Composite partition makin rumit | Mapper/service jadi kompleks |
| Testing lebih sulit | Harus mock physical partition |

Rule:

```text id="84c0xv"
Application layer hanya tahu logical table.
Database layer yang route ke physical partition.
```

---

# 3. Failure Mode: Missing Partition

## 3.1 Gejala

Jika tidak ada matching partition:

```text id="9mjj5u"
ERROR: no partition of relation "audit_event" found for row
```

Contoh:

```sql id="6w9swi"
INSERT INTO app.audit_event (
    event_id,
    tenant_id,
    case_id,
    event_time,
    event_type,
    payload
)
VALUES (
    gen_random_uuid(),
    gen_random_uuid(),
    gen_random_uuid(),
    TIMESTAMPTZ '2027-01-01 00:00:00+00',
    'TEST',
    '{}'::jsonb
);
```

Jika partition `audit_event_p2027_01` belum ada dan tidak ada default partition, insert gagal.

## 3.2 Mitigasi

| Mitigasi | Penjelasan |
|---|---|
| Create future partition ahead-of-time | Minimal 3-6 bulan untuk monthly, 14-30 hari untuk daily |
| Default partition | Safety net, tapi wajib dimonitor |
| Startup health check | Service gagal start jika partition horizon kurang |
| CI/CD smoke test | Insert routing test untuk current/future partition |
| Admin endpoint | Expose partition health ke ops |
| Alert | Future partition horizon rendah, default partition berisi row |

## 3.3 SQL Cek Future Partition

Jika naming convention monthly:

```sql id="ppugq9"
WITH partitions AS (
    SELECT
        child.relname AS partition_name
    FROM pg_inherits i
    JOIN pg_class parent ON parent.oid = i.inhparent
    JOIN pg_class child  ON child.oid = i.inhrelid
    WHERE parent.oid = 'app.audit_event'::regclass
)
SELECT *
FROM partitions
WHERE partition_name >= 'audit_event_p2026_10'
ORDER BY partition_name;
```

Lebih robust: simpan metadata partition bounds di table internal lifecycle, karena parsing nama saja rawan.

---

# 4. Insert Routing Smoke Test

Setelah membuat partition baru, jalankan test:

```sql id="vo8z4d"
INSERT INTO app.audit_event (
    event_id,
    tenant_id,
    case_id,
    event_time,
    event_type,
    payload
)
VALUES (
    gen_random_uuid(),
    gen_random_uuid(),
    gen_random_uuid(),
    TIMESTAMPTZ '2026-10-15 10:00:00+00',
    'SMOKE_TEST',
    '{"smoke":true}'::jsonb
);
```

Cek actual partition:

```sql id="hih14c"
SELECT
    tableoid::regclass AS actual_partition,
    event_time,
    event_type
FROM app.audit_event
WHERE event_type = 'SMOKE_TEST'
ORDER BY event_time DESC
LIMIT 1;
```

Expected:

```text id="ozops2"
actual_partition = app.audit_event_p2026_10
```

Clean up:

```sql id="a0dp3i"
DELETE FROM app.audit_event
WHERE event_type = 'SMOKE_TEST'
  AND event_time >= TIMESTAMPTZ '2026-10-01 00:00:00+00'
  AND event_time <  TIMESTAMPTZ '2026-11-01 00:00:00+00';
```

---

# 5. Update Path dan Row Movement

## 5.1 Update Non-partition Key

Aman secara partition routing:

```sql id="1kbonu"
UPDATE app.audit_event
SET payload = jsonb_set(payload, '{reviewed}', 'true'::jsonb)
WHERE event_id = :eventId
  AND event_time >= :fromTime
  AND event_time <  :toTime;
```

Row tetap di partition asal karena `event_time` tidak berubah.

## 5.2 Update Partition Key

Berisiko:

```sql id="hfy1iw"
UPDATE app.audit_event
SET event_time = TIMESTAMPTZ '2026-08-01 10:00:00+00'
WHERE event_id = :eventId
  AND event_time = TIMESTAMPTZ '2026-07-31 23:59:59+00';
```

Jika row awal ada di `audit_event_p2026_07`, update ini bisa memindahkan row ke `audit_event_p2026_08`. PostgreSQL mendokumentasikan bahwa update pada partition key dapat memindahkan row ke partition lain jika row tidak lagi memenuhi partition constraint asal. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/ddl-partitioning.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

## 5.3 Kenapa Row Movement Perlu Dihindari

| Risiko | Dampak |
|---|---|
| Write lebih mahal | Secara konseptual mirip delete + insert antar partition |
| Trigger/FK/index ikut terlibat | Cost naik |
| Concurrency conflict | Race dengan transaction lain |
| Audit trail membingungkan | Event time berubah setelah masuk log |
| Retention boundary rusak | Data bisa pindah ke partition lifecycle berbeda |
| API semantics lemah | Timestamp identity berubah |

Rule:

```text id="kjgb9h"
Untuk audit/event/history table:
partition key harus immutable secara domain.
```

---

# 6. Guardrail: Prevent Partition Key Update

PostgreSQL tidak punya built-in “immutable column constraint”. Gunakan design discipline, permission, atau trigger jika perlu.

## 6.1 Trigger Guard

```sql id="rciic5"
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION app.prevent_audit_event_time_update()
RETURNS trigger
LANGUAGE plpgsql
AS $$
BEGIN
    IF NEW.event_time IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.event_time THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'event_time is immutable for audit_event';
    END IF;

    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$;

CREATE TRIGGER audit_event_prevent_event_time_update_trg
BEFORE UPDATE OF event_time ON app.audit_event
FOR EACH ROW
EXECUTE FUNCTION app.prevent_audit_event_time_update();
```

## 6.2 Permission Guard

Untuk service account aplikasi, jangan beri permission update partition key jika tidak perlu:

```sql id="0r3jo8"
REVOKE UPDATE (event_time) ON app.audit_event FROM app_service_role;
```

Lalu beri update kolom tertentu:

```sql id="jctzo5"
GRANT UPDATE (payload) ON app.audit_event TO app_service_role;
```

## 6.3 Application Guard

Di entity/service layer:

```java id="rbrrtn"
public void updateAuditEventPayload(AuditEvent existing, JsonNode newPayload) {
    if (!existing.eventTime().equals(existing.eventTime())) {
        throw new IllegalStateException("eventTime is immutable");
    }

    mapper.updatePayload(existing.eventId(), existing.eventTime(), newPayload.toString());
}
```

Lebih baik lagi: jangan sediakan method update `eventTime`.

---

# 7. Delete Path

## 7.1 Row-level Delete

```sql id="l7ykn9"
DELETE FROM app.audit_event
WHERE event_time >= TIMESTAMPTZ '2026-07-01 00:00:00+00'
  AND event_time <  TIMESTAMPTZ '2026-08-01 00:00:00+00'
  AND event_type = 'TEST_EVENT';
```

Untuk delete kecil, ini normal.

## 7.2 Massive Delete Anti-pattern

Buruk:

```sql id="myq5t9"
DELETE FROM app.audit_event
WHERE event_time < TIMESTAMPTZ '2023-01-01 00:00:00+00';
```

Masalah:

| Problem | Dampak |
|---|---|
| Banyak dead tuples | Butuh vacuum |
| WAL besar | Replication/storage pressure |
| Lock row banyak | Concurrency impact |
| Index cleanup mahal | Write amplification |
| Lama | Bisa timeout |

Lebih baik untuk retention:

```sql id="o1t177"
ALTER TABLE app.audit_event
DETACH PARTITION app.audit_event_p2022_12;
```

atau:

```sql id="f3gw9p"
DROP TABLE app.audit_event_p2022_12;
```

PostgreSQL documentation menjelaskan bahwa drop/detach partition bisa jauh lebih cepat daripada bulk operation dan menghindari overhead `VACUUM` akibat bulk delete. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/ddl-partitioning.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 8. Hot Current Partition

## 8.1 Apa Itu Hot Partition?

Pada range partition by time, semua write terbaru biasanya masuk ke current partition.

```text id="mkptou"
audit_event_p2026_07
│
├─ semua insert July masuk sini
├─ index July paling sering berubah
├─ autovacuum/analyze lebih aktif
├─ cache pressure tinggi
└─ contention lebih mungkin muncul
```

Partitioning tidak otomatis menyebar write jika partition key-nya time-based. Ia membuat lifecycle/read pruning bagus, tetapi current partition tetap bisa panas.

## 8.2 Gejala Hot Partition

| Gejala | Indikasi |
|---|---|
| Insert latency naik | Current partition/index bottleneck |
| WAL meningkat tajam | Write-heavy hot partition |
| Autovacuum sering jalan | Update/delete tinggi |
| Index bloat | Banyak update/delete |
| Lock wait | Concurrent update/delete/DDL |
| CPU tinggi | Index maintenance/trigger/check/FK |
| I/O tinggi | Heap/index writes |

## 8.3 Mitigasi

| Mitigasi | Kapan |
|---|---|
| Kurangi index di hot partition | Jika write overhead dominan |
| Partial index untuk worker | Outbox/job queue |
| Subpartition hash tenant/case | Jika current partition terlalu besar/hot |
| Batch insert | Mengurangi round-trip |
| COPY/staging | Bulk ingest |
| Queue sharding | Multiple workers tanpa tabrakan |
| Autovacuum tuning per table | Jika dead tuples tinggi |
| Move reporting off hot table | Jika read/report mengganggu write |

---

# 9. Insert Batch dari Java/MyBatis

## 9.1 Prinsip

Untuk high-throughput insert:

```text id="lx3hgf"
1. Jangan insert satu row per transaction.
2. Gunakan batch executor / JDBC batch.
3. Commit per chunk.
4. Pastikan chunk tidak terlalu besar.
5. Retry harus idempotent.
```

## 9.2 MyBatis Batch Executor

```java id="fsmj26"
try (SqlSession session = sqlSessionFactory.openSession(ExecutorType.BATCH, false)) {
    AuditEventMapper mapper = session.getMapper(AuditEventMapper.class);

    int count = 0;

    for (AuditEvent event : events) {
        mapper.insertAuditEvent(event);
        count++;

        if (count % 1000 == 0) {
            session.flushStatements();
            session.commit();
        }
    }

    session.flushStatements();
    session.commit();
}
```

Mapper:

```xml id="kkj08f"
<insert id="insertAuditEvent">
    INSERT INTO app.audit_event (
        event_id,
        tenant_id,
        case_id,
        event_time,
        event_type,
        payload
    )
    VALUES (
        #{eventId},
        #{tenantId},
        #{caseId},
        #{eventTime},
        #{eventType},
        CAST(#{payloadJson} AS jsonb)
    )
</insert>
```

## 9.3 Chunk Size Rule

| Workload | Starting chunk |
|---|---:|
| Small row | 1000-5000 |
| JSONB payload sedang | 500-1000 |
| JSONB payload besar | 100-500 |
| FK/trigger banyak | 100-1000 |
| Outbox transactional | Ikuti aggregate transaction boundary |

Jangan pakai angka ini sebagai dogma. Benchmark dengan data dan latency nyata.

---

# 10. Multi-row Insert

Alternatif:

```sql id="35dcqf"
INSERT INTO app.audit_event (
    event_id,
    tenant_id,
    case_id,
    event_time,
    event_type,
    payload
)
VALUES
    (:event_id_1, :tenant_id_1, :case_id_1, :event_time_1, :event_type_1, CAST(:payload_1 AS jsonb)),
    (:event_id_2, :tenant_id_2, :case_id_2, :event_time_2, :event_type_2, CAST(:payload_2 AS jsonb)),
    (:event_id_3, :tenant_id_3, :case_id_3, :event_time_3, :event_type_3, CAST(:payload_3 AS jsonb));
```

Kelebihan:

| Kelebihan | Penjelasan |
|---|---|
| Round-trip lebih sedikit | Throughput naik |
| Transaction overhead lebih rendah | Commit per batch |
| Routing tetap ke parent | PostgreSQL route tiap row |

Risiko:

| Risiko | Penjelasan |
|---|---|
| Statement terlalu besar | Parse/memory overhead |
| Satu row gagal semua gagal | Perlu idempotent retry |
| Logging besar | SQL log berat |
| MyBatis dynamic SQL kompleks | Mapper jadi sulit |

---

# 11. COPY untuk Bulk Load

## 11.1 COPY ke Partitioned Table

PostgreSQL `COPY FROM` dapat digunakan dengan partitioned table. Dokumentasi `COPY` menyebut `COPY FROM` dapat dipakai dengan plain, foreign, atau partitioned tables, atau view yang memiliki trigger `INSTEAD OF INSERT`. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-copy.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Contoh:

```sql id="xxv296"
COPY app.audit_event (
    event_id,
    tenant_id,
    case_id,
    event_time,
    event_type,
    payload
)
FROM '/data/audit_event_2026_07.csv'
WITH (FORMAT csv, HEADER true);
```

Row akan diarahkan sesuai partition routing declarative partitioning.

## 11.2 COPY ke Child/Staging untuk Backfill Besar

Untuk backfill partition tertentu, sering lebih aman COPY ke staging table lalu attach.

```sql id="dg1bx9"
CREATE TABLE app.audit_event_stage_2026_07 (
    LIKE app.audit_event INCLUDING DEFAULTS INCLUDING CONSTRAINTS
);

COPY app.audit_event_stage_2026_07 (
    event_id,
    tenant_id,
    case_id,
    event_time,
    event_type,
    payload
)
```
