```sql id="dg1bx9"
FROM '/data/audit_event_2026_07.csv'
WITH (FORMAT csv, HEADER true);
```

Lalu validasi bound:

```sql id="rswhpj"
ALTER TABLE app.audit_event_stage_2026_07
ADD CONSTRAINT audit_event_stage_2026_07_bound_chk
CHECK (
    event_time >= TIMESTAMPTZ '2026-07-01 00:00:00+00'
    AND event_time < TIMESTAMPTZ '2026-08-01 00:00:00+00'
);
```

Index dan attach:

```sql id="2wjmcn"
CREATE INDEX audit_event_stage_2026_07_tenant_time_idx
ON app.audit_event_stage_2026_07 (tenant_id, event_time DESC);

CREATE INDEX audit_event_stage_2026_07_case_time_idx
ON app.audit_event_stage_2026_07 (case_id, event_time DESC);

ANALYZE app.audit_event_stage_2026_07;

ALTER TABLE app.audit_event
ATTACH PARTITION app.audit_event_stage_2026_07
FOR VALUES FROM ('2026-07-01 00:00:00+00')
             TO ('2026-08-01 00:00:00+00');
```

Saat attach, PostgreSQL bisa perlu scan validasi; `CHECK` constraint yang sesuai membantu menghindari/mengurangi validasi penuh. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/ddl-partitioning.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 12. COPY Caveat: Old Inheritance/Rules vs Declarative Partitioning

Dokumentasi partitioning PostgreSQL masih menyebut caveat historis: `COPY` mengabaikan rules, sehingga jika memakai pendekatan partitioning lama berbasis rules, COPY harus diarahkan ke child table yang benar. Namun untuk declarative partitioned table modern, rujuk dokumentasi `COPY`: `COPY FROM` dapat digunakan dengan partitioned table. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/ddl-partitioning.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Practical takeaway:

```text id="o3uf2c"
Untuk declarative partitioning modern:
- COPY FROM parent partitioned table bisa dipakai.
- Untuk backfill sangat besar, staging + attach sering lebih aman.
- Jangan menggunakan rule-based partitioning baru.
```

---

# 13. Write Path dengan Default Partition

## 13.1 Kapan Default Partition Membantu

```sql id="gg72vx"
CREATE TABLE app.audit_event_default
PARTITION OF app.audit_event
DEFAULT;
```

Membantu saat:

| Situasi | Benefit |
|---|---|
| Future partition belum dibuat | Insert tidak langsung gagal |
| Late-arriving data | Masuk fallback |
| External feed kotor | Data bisa ditangani belakangan |
| Availability lebih penting dari strict failure | Degrade lebih aman |

## 13.2 Risiko

| Risiko | Dampak |
|---|---|
| Data menumpuk diam-diam | Query/default partition berat |
| Future partition bug tidak terlihat | Incident tertunda |
| Partition pruning melemah | Default harus dipertimbangkan |
| Cleanup rumit | Harus move data keluar default |

## 13.3 Operational Rule

```text id="6d1gel"
Default partition boleh ada sebagai airbag,
bukan sebagai storage normal.
```

Monitoring:

```sql id="b75wqf"
SELECT COUNT(*) AS default_rows
FROM app.audit_event_default;
```

Alert:

```text id="t9c5l6"
default_rows > 0 untuk audit_event production
```

---

# 14. Queue Pattern dengan Partitioned Table dan `SKIP LOCKED`

## 14.1 Kapan Dipakai

Untuk outbox/inbox/job queue:

```text id="w486yl"
Multiple workers mengambil row PENDING
tanpa saling menunggu row yang sedang diproses worker lain.
```

PostgreSQL `SELECT ... FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED` akan melewati row yang tidak bisa segera di-lock. Dokumentasi PostgreSQL memperingatkan bahwa `SKIP LOCKED` memberi view yang tidak konsisten dan tidak cocok untuk general-purpose query, tetapi berguna untuk menghindari lock contention pada table queue-like dengan banyak consumer. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-select.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

## 14.2 Tabel Outbox Partitioned by `created_at`

```sql id="x1hzgy"
CREATE TABLE app.outbox_event (
    outbox_id     uuid        NOT NULL,
    aggregate_id  uuid        NOT NULL,
    event_type    text        NOT NULL,
    status        text        NOT NULL,
    created_at    timestamptz NOT NULL,
    processed_at  timestamptz,
    retry_count   integer     NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    payload       jsonb       NOT NULL,

    CONSTRAINT outbox_event_pk
        PRIMARY KEY (outbox_id, created_at)
) PARTITION BY RANGE (created_at);
```

Partial index di current partition:

```sql id="x5cjti"
CREATE INDEX outbox_event_p2026_07_pending_idx
ON app.outbox_event_p2026_07 (created_at, outbox_id)
WHERE status = 'PENDING';
```

## 14.3 Claim Batch Pattern

```sql id="npibbs"
WITH candidate AS (
    SELECT
        outbox_id,
        created_at
    FROM app.outbox_event
    WHERE status = 'PENDING'
      AND created_at >= :fromTime
      AND created_at <  :toTime
    ORDER BY created_at, outbox_id
    LIMIT :batchSize
    FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED
)
UPDATE app.outbox_event e
SET
    status = 'PROCESSING',
    retry_count = retry_count + 1
FROM candidate c
WHERE e.outbox_id = c.outbox_id
  AND e.created_at = c.created_at
RETURNING
    e.outbox_id,
    e.created_at,
    e.aggregate_id,
    e.event_type,
    e.payload;
```

Kenapa pakai CTE + UPDATE?

| Alasan | Penjelasan |
|---|---|
| Claim atomik | Row langsung berubah status |
| Worker lain skip locked row | Mengurangi contention |
| Return payload | Worker dapat data untuk publish |
| Composite key | `created_at` ikut untuk PK/partition key |
| ORDER BY stabil | Mengurangi deadlock/chaos |

PostgreSQL documentation untuk `UPDATE` juga menunjukkan pola batch update dengan CTE dan menyebut `SKIP LOCKED` dapat ditambahkan jika lock contention menjadi perhatian agar beberapa command tidak update row yang sama. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-update.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 15. Queue Worker Transaction Pattern

## 15.1 Jangan Tahan Transaction Terlalu Lama

Buruk:

```text id="m9lqou"
BEGIN
  SELECT ... FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED
  publish to Kafka/external HTTP
  UPDATE status
COMMIT
```

Masalah:

| Problem | Dampak |
|---|---|
| External call di dalam DB transaction | Lock ditahan lama |
| Kafka/HTTP lambat | Connection pool habis |
| Transaction timeout | Row stuck |
| Retry sulit | Ambigu publish berhasil atau tidak |

## 15.2 Lebih Aman: Claim → Commit → Process → Complete

```text id="ae42cs"
1. TX1: claim batch PENDING → PROCESSING, commit
2. Outside TX: publish/process
3. TX2: mark PROCESSED or FAILED
```

Pattern ini butuh recovery job:

```text id="cjccbn"
PROCESSING terlalu lama → reset ke PENDING atau FAILED
```

## 15.3 Claim SQL dengan Lease

```sql id="efnf18"
WITH candidate AS (
    SELECT
        outbox_id,
        created_at
    FROM app.outbox_event
    WHERE status = 'PENDING'
      AND created_at >= :fromTime
      AND created_at <  :toTime
    ORDER BY created_at, outbox_id
    LIMIT :batchSize
    FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED
)
UPDATE app.outbox_event e
SET
    status = 'PROCESSING',
    processing_started_at = now(),
    retry_count = retry_count + 1
FROM candidate c
WHERE e.outbox_id = c.outbox_id
  AND e.created_at = c.created_at
RETURNING e.*;
```

Recovery:

```sql id="v4vwy5"
UPDATE app.outbox_event
SET status = 'PENDING'
WHERE status = 'PROCESSING'
  AND processing_started_at < now() - interval '10 minutes'
  AND retry_count < 5;
```

---

# 16. Partitioning Queue Table: Time Range Problem

Jika outbox dipartisi by `created_at`, worker query harus membawa time range agar pruning terjadi.

Buruk:

```sql id="f1yb94"
SELECT *
FROM app.outbox_event
WHERE status = 'PENDING'
ORDER BY created_at
LIMIT 100
FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED;
```

Ini bisa menyentuh banyak partitions.

Lebih baik:

```sql id="j3ktpd"
SELECT *
FROM app.outbox_event
WHERE status = 'PENDING'
  AND created_at >= TIMESTAMPTZ '2026-07-01 00:00:00+00'
  AND created_at <  TIMESTAMPTZ '2026-08-01 00:00:00+00'
ORDER BY created_at
LIMIT 100
FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED;
```

Practical worker strategy:

```text id="e9qypk"
Worker scan active window:
- current day/month
- previous day/month for retry/late items
- failed/retry partition windows separately
```

---

# 17. Avoid Partition by Mutable Queue Status

Buruk:

```sql id="22jbc7"
PARTITION BY LIST (status)
```

Karena outbox status berubah:

```text id="bmjs4q"
PENDING → PROCESSING → PROCESSED
PENDING → PROCESSING → FAILED → PENDING
```

Jika `status` partition key, update status bisa memindahkan row antar partition. Ini write-heavy dan berisiko. Lebih baik:

```text id="y80l6c"
PARTITION BY RANGE(created_at)
+ partial index WHERE status='PENDING'
```

---

# 18. Locking pada DDL Partition Operations

## 18.1 ATTACH / DETACH / DROP

Partition lifecycle operation melibatkan lock. Untuk `DETACH PARTITION`, PostgreSQL menyediakan `DETACH PARTITION ... CONCURRENTLY` untuk mengurangi lock level pada parent, tetapi ada batasan: tidak bisa dijalankan dalam transaction block dan ada kondisi tertentu yang tidak didukung. Dokumentasi `ALTER TABLE` menjelaskan bentuk `DETACH PARTITION partition_name [ CONCURRENTLY | FINALIZE ]` dan behavior locking/dependency terkait partitioned table. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-altertable.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Practical rule:

```text id="j5ouec"
Jangan jalankan DDL partition besar sembarangan di jam traffic tinggi.
```

## 18.2 DDL Safety Checklist

| Operation | Risk | Safer pattern |
|---|---|---|
| Create future empty partition | Rendah | Transactional migration |
| Create index on big partition | Tinggi | `CREATE INDEX CONCURRENTLY` |
| Attach big staging table | Medium/high | Add CHECK bound first |
| Detach old partition | Medium | Maintenance window / concurrently |
| Drop old partition | Medium/high | Archive + approval + low traffic |
| Add FK/check validation | Medium/high | `NOT VALID` + validate later |

---

# 19. Lock Monitoring

Cek lock yang sedang menunggu:

```sql id="uh35va"
SELECT
    blocked.pid AS blocked_pid,
    blocked_activity.usename AS blocked_user,
    blocked_activity.query AS blocked_query,
    blocking.pid AS blocking_pid,
    blocking_activity.usename AS blocking_user,
    blocking_activity.query AS blocking_query
FROM pg_locks blocked
JOIN pg_stat_activity blocked_activity
  ON blocked_activity.pid = blocked.pid
JOIN pg_locks blocking
  ON blocking.locktype = blocked.locktype
 AND blocking.database IS NOT DISTINCT FROM blocked.database
 AND blocking.relation IS NOT DISTINCT FROM blocked.relation
 AND blocking.page IS NOT DISTINCT FROM blocked.page
 AND blocking.tuple IS NOT DISTINCT FROM blocked.tuple
 AND blocking.virtualxid IS NOT DISTINCT FROM blocked.virtualxid
 AND blocking.transactionid IS NOT DISTINCT FROM blocked.transactionid
 AND blocking.classid IS NOT DISTINCT FROM blocked.classid
 AND blocking.objid IS NOT DISTINCT FROM blocked.objid
 AND blocking.objsubid IS NOT DISTINCT FROM blocked.objsubid
 AND blocking.pid <> blocked.pid
JOIN pg_stat_activity blocking_activity
  ON blocking_activity.pid = blocking.pid
WHERE NOT blocked.granted
  AND blocking.granted;
```

Operationally, juga set timeout untuk migration/session:

```sql id="on1ss6"
SET lock_timeout = '5s';
SET statement_timeout = '10min';
```

Gunakan agar migration gagal cepat daripada menggantung dan memblokir traffic.

---

# 20. Transaction Isolation dan Queue Worker

Untuk queue worker dengan `SKIP LOCKED`, biasanya gunakan default `READ COMMITTED`.

Kenapa:

| Isolation | Catatan |
|---|---|
| Read committed | Umum untuk queue worker |
| Repeatable read | Bisa melihat snapshot lama, tidak ideal untuk queue dinamis |
| Serializable | Bisa menyebabkan serialization failure pada high concurrency |

`SKIP LOCKED` bukan mekanisme correctness umum; ia alat untuk menghindari blocking di queue-like workload. Dokumentasi PostgreSQL secara eksplisit menyebut hasilnya inconsistent view, sehingga jangan dipakai untuk query bisnis biasa yang butuh snapshot konsisten. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-select.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 21. Idempotency pada Write Path

Write-heavy partitioned table sering dipakai untuk event/outbox/inbox. Retry akan terjadi. Harus idempotent.

## 21.1 Registry Table

```sql id="xbplko"
CREATE TABLE app.idempotency_key_registry (
    tenant_id        uuid        NOT NULL,
    idempotency_key  text        NOT NULL,
    request_hash     text        NOT NULL,
    first_seen_at    timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),

    CONSTRAINT idempotency_key_registry_pk
        PRIMARY KEY (tenant_id, idempotency_key)
);
```

## 21.2 Insert Flow

```sql id="432hdq"
BEGIN;

INSERT INTO app.idempotency_key_registry (
    tenant_id,
    idempotency_key,
    request_hash
)
VALUES (
    :tenant_id,
    :idempotency_key,
    :request_hash
);

INSERT INTO app.inbox_event (
    inbox_event_id,
    tenant_id,
    received_at,
    idempotency_key,
    payload
)
VALUES (
    :inbox_event_id,
    :tenant_id,
    :received_at,
    :idempotency_key,
    CAST(:payload AS jsonb)
);

COMMIT;
```

Jika duplicate key terjadi, map ke:

```text id="8x3uk9"
409 Conflict
atau idempotent success jika request_hash sama
```

---

# 22. Write Amplification dari Index dan Constraint

Setiap insert ke partitioned table tidak hanya menulis heap row. Ia juga menulis:

```text id="2xzlnw"
- primary key index
- secondary indexes
- GIN/BRIN/B-tree indexes
- FK validation reads
- trigger execution
- WAL
- visibility metadata
```

Pada hot partition, index terlalu banyak bisa menjadi bottleneck.

## 22.1 Index Budget

Untuk hot write-heavy table, mulai minimal:

| Table | Minimal hot indexes |
|---|---|
| `audit_event` | PK, tenant_time, case_time |
| `outbox_event` | PK, partial pending index, aggregate lookup jika perlu |
| `inbox_event` | PK, idempotency registry separate, received_at/consumer index |
| `transaction_history` | PK, account_date, transaction lookup |
| `workflow_history` | PK, process_time/case_time |

Jangan langsung membuat GIN JSONB full payload di hot partition kecuali query-nya benar-benar kritikal.

---

# 23. Autovacuum dan ANALYZE pada Hot Partition

Partition aktif berubah paling cepat. Statistics stale bisa membuat planner memilih plan buruk.

## 23.1 Cek stats

```sql id="xie9q2"
SELECT
    relname,
    n_live_tup,
    n_dead_tup,
    last_vacuum,
    last_autovacuum,
    last_analyze,
    last_autoanalyze,
    autovacuum_count,
    autoanalyze_count
FROM pg_stat_user_tables
WHERE relname LIKE 'audit_event_p2026_%'
ORDER BY relname;
```

## 23.2 Manual Analyze Setelah Bulk Load

```sql id="c3u4sk"
ANALYZE app.audit_event_p2026_07;
```

`ANALYZE` mengumpulkan statistics yang dipakai planner untuk memilih plan. Ini penting setelah bulk load atau attach partition besar. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-altertable.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

## 23.3 Per-partition Tuning

Untuk hot partition tertentu:

```sql id="umq3lo"
ALTER TABLE app.outbox_event_p2026_07
SET (
    autovacuum_vacuum_scale_factor = 0.02,
    autovacuum_analyze_scale_factor = 0.01
);
```

Catatan: nilai harus dibenchmark. Jangan asal copy ke semua table.

---

# 24. Write Path dari Jersey/MyBatis

## 24.1 Insert Audit Event Resource

```java id="gmnxk1"
@Path("/cases/{caseId}/audit-events")
public class AuditEventResource {

    private final AuditEventService service;

    @POST
    public Response append(
            @PathParam("caseId") UUID caseId,
            AppendAuditEventRequest request
    ) {
        AuditEvent event = service.append(caseId, request);
        return Response.status(Response.Status.CREATED).entity(event).build();
    }
}
```

Service:

```java id="r7zli6"
public AuditEvent append(UUID caseId, AppendAuditEventRequest request) {
    OffsetDateTime eventTime = request.eventTime() != null
        ? request.eventTime().withOffsetSameInstant(ZoneOffset.UTC)
        : OffsetDateTime.now(ZoneOffset.UTC);

    AuditEvent event = new AuditEvent(
        UUID.randomUUID(),
        request.tenantId(),
        caseId,
        eventTime,
        request.eventType(),
        request.payload()
    );

    mapper.insertAuditEvent(event);

    return event;
}
```

Mapper:

```xml id="gl5ecr"
<insert id="insertAuditEvent">
    INSERT INTO app.audit_event (
        event_id,
        tenant_id,
        case_id,
        event_time,
        event_type,
        payload
    )
    VALUES (
        #{eventId},
        #{tenantId},
        #{caseId},
        #{eventTime},
        #{eventType},
        CAST(#{payloadJson} AS jsonb)
    )
</insert>
```

## 24.2 Error Mapping

| SQLSTATE | Meaning | HTTP mapping |
|---|---|---|
| `23505` | unique violation | 409 Conflict |
| `23503` | foreign key violation | 400/409 |
| `23514` | check violation | 400 Bad Request |
| `23502` | not null violation | 400 Bad Request |
| `40P01` | deadlock detected | retryable 503/409 depending context |
| `40001` | serialization failure | retryable |

Missing partition error does not always map cleanly to common integrity SQLSTATE in app frameworks; treat it as operational/configuration failure and alert. In service response, it is usually safer as `503 Service Unavailable` or internal error depending whether caller can retry.

---

# 25. Retry Strategy

## 25.1 Retryable

| Error | Retry? | Notes |
|---|---:|---|
| Deadlock detected | Yes | Retry with backoff |
| Serialization failure | Yes | Retry transaction |
| Lock timeout | Maybe | Retry if operation idempotent |
| Connection failure | Maybe | Depends transaction status |
| Missing partition | Not blind retry | Fix partition first |
| Unique violation | No blind retry | Idempotency handling |
| Check violation | No | Bad data/request |

## 25.2 Idempotent Retry Requirement

```text id="snzqqt"
Jangan retry write tanpa idempotency key
jika efek samping bisa dobel.
```

Untuk outbox/inbox/event ingestion, registry table sangat membantu.

---

# 26. Backpressure Pattern

Jika hot partition/write path overload:

| Layer | Guardrail |
|---|---|
| API | rate limit / max batch size |
| Service | bounded queue |
| DB pool | max pool size, timeout |
| PostgreSQL | lock_timeout, statement_timeout |
| Worker | batch size, concurrency limit |
| Outbox | retry with exponential backoff |
| Ops | alert WAL, lag, lock wait, autovacuum |

Jangan menaikkan worker concurrency terus-menerus. Pada titik tertentu, concurrency hanya menambah lock contention, WAL pressure, dan cache churn.

---

# 27. Observability Write Path

## 27.1 Table write stats

```sql id="s0psbj"
SELECT
    relname,
    n_tup_ins,
    n_tup_upd,
    n_tup_del,
    n_live_tup,
    n_dead_tup,
    last_autovacuum,
    last_autoanalyze
FROM pg_stat_user_tables
WHERE relname LIKE 'audit_event_p2026_%'
ORDER BY relname;
```

## 27.2 Index usage/write budget

```sql id="4ig6rc"
SELECT
    relname AS table_name,
    indexrelname AS index_name,
    idx_scan,
    idx_tup_read,
    idx_tup_fetch
FROM pg_stat_user_indexes
WHERE relname LIKE 'audit_event_p2026_%'
ORDER BY relname, idx_scan;
```

## 27.3 Long transactions

```sql id="f99mwp"
SELECT
    pid,
    usename,
    state,
    now() - xact_start AS xact_age,
    wait_event_type,
    wait_event,
    query
FROM pg_stat_activity
WHERE xact_start IS NOT NULL
ORDER BY xact_age DESC;
```

Long transactions are dangerous for vacuum and write-heavy tables.

---

# 28. Common Write Path Failure Modes

| Failure mode | Gejala | Root cause | Pencegahan |
|---|---|---|---|
| Missing partition | Insert gagal | Future partition belum dibuat | Partition horizon alert |
| Default partition membesar | Query lambat, cleanup susah | Data fallback terus | Monitor default rows |
| Hot partition bottleneck | Insert latency naik | Current partition/index terlalu panas | Index budget, subpartition, batch |
| Row movement | Update mahal/conflict | Partition key mutable | Immutable key guard |
| Batch terlalu besar | Timeout/memory/WAL spike | Chunk size berlebihan | Tune batch size |
| Queue worker tabrakan | Duplicate processing/lock wait | Claim pattern salah | `FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED` + status claim |
| Long transaction | Vacuum tertahan | External call di transaction | Claim-commit-process-complete |
| Index build blocking | App latency naik | Non-concurrent index on big partition | `CREATE INDEX CONCURRENTLY` |
| FK validation lambat | Migration blocking | Add FK langsung validasi | `NOT VALID` + validate later |
| Cascade delete incident | Data history hilang | `ON DELETE CASCADE` | `RESTRICT`, soft delete |
| Write amplification tinggi | CPU/WAL/storage tinggi | Terlalu banyak indexes/triggers | Review index/constraint budget |

---

# 29. Practical Checklist

| Checklist | Done |
|---|---|
| Aplikasi insert ke parent table, bukan child |
| Current dan future partition tersedia |
| Default partition policy jelas |
| Insert routing smoke test tersedia |
| Partition key immutable secara domain |
| Trigger/permission guard dipakai jika perlu |
| Batch insert chunk size dibenchmark |
| Bulk load besar pakai staging + attach |
| COPY strategy jelas |
| Hot partition index budget direview |
| Queue table tidak partition by mutable status |
| `SKIP LOCKED` hanya dipakai untuk queue-like workload |
| Worker tidak melakukan external call dalam long DB transaction |
| Retry write idempotent |
| Autovacuum/analyze hot partition dimonitor |
| Lock timeout/statement timeout untuk migration tersedia |
| Write path error mapping jelas |
| Observability lock/long transaction tersedia |

---

# 30. Production Design Recommendation

Untuk stack Java/Jersey/MyBatis + PostgreSQL:

```text id="8wrmyj"
Audit/Event History:
- insert ke parent
- partition by event_time
- event_time immutable
- batch insert untuk high throughput
- no update partition key
- no FK cascade

Outbox/Inbox:
- partition by created_at/received_at
- partial index WHERE status='PENDING'
- claim with FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED
- claim-commit-process-complete
- recovery for stuck PROCESSING
- idempotency registry

Bulk Backfill:
- load to staging table
- add CHECK bound
- build indexes
- ANALYZE
- ATTACH PARTITION

Operations:
- create future partitions ahead
- monitor default partition
- use lock_timeout for DDL
- create indexes concurrently for existing big partitions
```

---

# Kesimpulan Seri 10

Write path pada partitioned table harus didesain sebagai **operational workflow**, bukan hanya SQL insert.

Rule paling penting:

```text id="ri0flu"
1. Insert dari aplikasi selalu ke parent table.
2. Future partition harus tersedia sebelum data masuk.
3. Default partition adalah safety net, bukan tempat tinggal data.
4. Partition key sebaiknya immutable.
5. Update partition key berarti row movement dan harus dihindari.
6. Current partition bisa menjadi hot partition.
7. Jangan terlalu banyak index di hot write-heavy partition.
8. Bulk load besar lebih aman lewat staging + attach.
9. Queue/outbox gunakan SKIP LOCKED hanya untuk queue-like workload.
10. Jangan tahan DB transaction saat external call.
11. Retry write harus idempotent.
12. DDL/index/retention operation harus lock-aware.
```

**Seri 10/14 selesai.**

Rekomendasi materi berikutnya: **Seri 11 - Bulk Load, Backfill, dan Migration from Non-partitioned Table: shadow table, staging partition, dual-write, cutover, validation, rollback, dan migration pipeline production-grade.**

---

**Sources:**

- [Documentation: 18: 5.12. Table Partitioning](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/ddl-partitioning.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com)

## Response:
7/16/2026, 8:20:04 AM

Saya lanjut ke **Seri 11/14**. Fokusnya migration production-grade: dari tabel non-partitioned ke partitioned table, bulk load, backfill, shadow table, dual-write, cutover, validation, dan rollback.

# Seri 11/14 - Bulk Load, Backfill, dan Migration from Non-partitioned Table

## 1. Tujuan Seri Ini

Seri ini fokus ke skenario production yang paling sensitif:

> **Bagaimana memindahkan tabel besar non-partitioned menjadi partitioned table tanpa merusak data, tanpa downtime besar, dan dengan rollback plan yang jelas?**

PostgreSQL partitioning biasanya mudah untuk tabel baru, tetapi migration dari tabel lama jauh lebih berisiko karena menyentuh:

```text id="7l3diz"
1. Data historis besar
2. Write traffic yang masih berjalan
3. Index dan constraint
4. Foreign key dependency
5. Application cutover
6. Validation data
7. Rollback strategy
8. Deployment pipeline
```

Dokumentasi PostgreSQL saat ini menekankan beberapa hal penting untuk migration: `ATTACH PARTITION` dapat memakai table existing sebagai partition, `CHECK` constraint yang cocok dengan partition bound dapat membantu menghindari scan validasi penuh, `COPY FROM` bisa dipakai pada partitioned table, dan operasi seperti `DETACH PARTITION CONCURRENTLY` memiliki batasan transaksi/default partition yang harus diperhatikan. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/ddl-partitioning.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 2. Mental Model Migration

```text id="6o8e3v"
Migration non-partitioned → partitioned
│
├─ Strategy A: Offline rebuild
│  ├─ Stop writes
│  ├─ Create partitioned table
│  ├─ Copy data
│  ├─ Validate
│  ├─ Rename/swap
│  └─ Restart writes
│
├─ Strategy B: Online shadow table
│  ├─ Create new partitioned table
│  ├─ Backfill historical data
│  ├─ Dual-write / change capture
│  ├─ Validate parity
│  ├─ Cutover reads/writes
│  └─ Keep rollback window
│
├─ Strategy C: Staging partition + attach
│  ├─ Create standalone table per partition range
│  ├─ Load data into staging
│  ├─ Add CHECK bound
│  ├─ Create indexes
│  ├─ ANALYZE
│  └─ ATTACH PARTITION
│
└─ Strategy D: Hybrid
   ├─ New writes to partitioned table
   ├─ Old data backfilled gradually
   ├─ Read path handles old + new
   └─ Final merge/cutover later
```

Tidak ada satu strategi yang selalu benar. Pilihan tergantung downtime tolerance, ukuran data, write rate, FK dependency, SLA, dan kemampuan rollback.

---

# 3. Strategy Selection Matrix

| Kondisi | Strategi paling cocok |
|---|---|
| Tabel kecil/sedang, downtime boleh | Offline rebuild |
| Tabel besar, write traffic tinggi | Online shadow table |
| Data historis bisa diproses per bulan/hari | Staging partition + attach |
| Ingin risiko kecil dan cutover bertahap | Hybrid old/new |
| Ada FK kompleks ke tabel lama | Shadow + careful compatibility layer |
| Regulatory/audit data tidak boleh hilang | Shadow + full validation + rollback window |
| Migration harus GitHub-reviewed | Explicit migration scripts |
| Extension/scheduler terbatas di managed cloud | Manual/CI-driven DDL |

Rule praktis:

```text id="44rw8p"
Jika data besar dan production aktif:
jangan langsung CREATE TABLE partitioned lalu INSERT SELECT semua data dalam satu transaksi besar.
```

---

# 4. Baseline: Tabel Lama Non-partitioned

Misal tabel lama:

```sql id="wqoh7g"
CREATE TABLE app.audit_event_old (
    event_id    uuid        NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id   uuid        NOT NULL,
    case_id     uuid        NOT NULL,
    event_time  timestamptz NOT NULL,
    event_type  text        NOT NULL,
    actor_id    uuid,
    payload     jsonb       NOT NULL,
    created_at  timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);
```

Masalah:

```text id="fsu9dw"
- event_id single-column PK
- tabel besar
- query banyak by event_time
- retention by month
- ingin migrate ke RANGE(event_time)
```

Target partitioned table:

```sql id="tboz10"
CREATE TABLE app.audit_event_new (
    event_id    uuid        NOT NULL,
    tenant_id   uuid        NOT NULL,
    case_id     uuid        NOT NULL,
    event_time  timestamptz NOT NULL,
    event_type  text        NOT NULL,
    actor_id    uuid,
    payload     jsonb       NOT NULL,
    created_at  timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),

    CONSTRAINT audit_event_new_pk
        PRIMARY KEY (event_id, event_time)
) PARTITION BY RANGE (event_time);
```

Perhatikan perubahan penting:

```text id="3sgk19"
PK lama:    event_id
PK baru:    event_id, event_time
```

Ini bukan detail kecil. Ini berdampak ke FK, mapper, DTO, dan query. PostgreSQL mewajibkan primary key/unique pada partitioned table mencakup semua partition key. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/ddl-partitioning.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 5. Migration Risk Inventory

Sebelum menulis script, jawab ini dulu.

| Pertanyaan | Kenapa penting |
|---|---|
| Berapa total row dan ukuran tabel? | Menentukan batch/backfill/cutover |
| Berapa write rate per menit? | Menentukan dual-write/change capture |
| Apakah ada FK ke tabel lama? | Bisa menghambat rename/drop/cutover |
| Apakah PK berubah dari single ke composite? | Aplikasi/FK harus disesuaikan |
| Apakah ada unique constraint global? | Mungkin butuh registry table |
| Apakah query aplikasi bisa membawa partition key? | Cutover bisa lambat jika tidak |
| Apakah downtime boleh? | Menentukan offline vs online |
| Apakah ada legal hold/retention rule? | Tidak boleh salah drop/archive |
| Apakah ada CDC/logical replication? | Bisa bantu sync shadow table |
| Apakah migration tool support non-transactional migration? | Penting untuk `CREATE INDEX CONCURRENTLY` |

---

# 6. Strategy A - Offline Rebuild

## 6.1 Kapan Cocok

| Cocok jika | Tidak cocok jika |
|---|---|
| Data tidak terlalu besar | Tabel ratusan GB/TB |
| Downtime boleh | SLA ketat |
| Write bisa dihentikan | Traffic 24/7 |
| Dependency sedikit | Banyak FK |
| Rollback sederhana | Data berubah terus |

## 6.2 Flow

```text id="e2wzne"
1. Announce maintenance window
2. Stop application writes
3. Create partitioned table
4. Create partitions
5. Copy data
6. Create indexes
7. Analyze
8. Validate row count/checksum
9. Rename old → backup
10. Rename new → original name
11. Start application
12. Monitor
```

## 6.3 SQL Example

```sql id="3brx8m"
BEGIN;

ALTER TABLE app.audit_event_old RENAME TO audit_event_backup;

CREATE TABLE app.audit_event (
    event_id    uuid        NOT NULL,
    tenant_id   uuid        NOT NULL,
    case_id     uuid        NOT NULL,
    event_time  timestamptz NOT NULL,
    event_type  text        NOT NULL,
    actor_id    uuid,
    payload     jsonb       NOT NULL,
    created_at  timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),

    CONSTRAINT audit_event_pk
        PRIMARY KEY (event_id, event_time)
) PARTITION BY RANGE (event_time);

CREATE TABLE app.audit_event_p2026_07
PARTITION OF app.audit_event
FOR VALUES FROM ('2026-07-01 00:00:00+00')
             TO ('2026-08-01 00:00:00+00');

CREATE TABLE app.audit_event_p2026_08
PARTITION OF app.audit_event
FOR VALUES FROM ('2026-08-01 00:00:00+00')
             TO ('2026-09-01 00:00:00+00');

INSERT INTO app.audit_event (
    event_id,
    tenant_id,
    case_id,
    event_time,
    event_type,
    actor_id,
    payload,
    created_at
)
SELECT
    event_id,
    tenant_id,
    case_id,
    event_time,
    event_type,
    actor_id,
    payload,
    created_at
FROM app.audit_event_backup;

CREATE INDEX audit_event_p2026_07_tenant_time_idx
ON app.audit_event_p2026_07 (tenant_id, event_time DESC);

CREATE INDEX audit_event_p2026_07_case_time_idx
ON app.audit_event_p2026_07 (case_id, event_time DESC);

CREATE INDEX audit_event_p2026_08_tenant_time_idx
ON app.audit_event_p2026_08 (tenant_id, event_time DESC);

CREATE INDEX audit_event_p2026_08_case_time_idx
ON app.audit_event_p2026_08 (case_id, event_time DESC);

ANALYZE app.audit_event;

COMMIT;
```

## 6.4 Risiko Offline Rebuild

| Risiko | Mitigasi |
|---|---|
| Maintenance window membengkak | Dry-run dengan data clone |
| Insert gagal karena missing partition | Generate all historical partitions dulu |
| PK/FK mismatch | Pre-migration schema compatibility review |
| Index build lama | Build after copy, benchmark |
| Rollback rename rumit | Keep old table untouched until validation |
| App query belum siap composite PK | Deploy app compatibility sebelum cutover |

---

# 7. Strategy B - Online Shadow Table

## 7.1 Kapan Cocok

Gunakan jika:

```text id="yhm6yg"
- tabel besar
- downtime rendah
- writes masih berjalan
- migration perlu bertahap
- validation harus lengkap
```

## 7.2 Flow

```text id="zf7f59"
1. Create shadow partitioned table: audit_event_new
2. Create partitions and indexes
3. Backfill historical data in chunks
4. Keep old table still serving production
5. Dual-write new writes to old + new
6. Validate row count/checksum per partition window
7. Switch read path to new
8. Switch write path to new
9. Keep old table as rollback
10. Drop/archive old table after confidence window
```

## 7.3 Shadow Table DDL

```sql id="d7qha3"
CREATE TABLE app.audit_event_new (
    event_id    uuid        NOT NULL,
    tenant_id   uuid        NOT NULL,
    case_id     uuid        NOT NULL,
    event_time  timestamptz NOT NULL,
    event_type  text        NOT NULL,
    actor_id    uuid,
    payload     jsonb       NOT NULL,
    created_at  timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),

    CONSTRAINT audit_event_new_pk
        PRIMARY KEY (event_id, event_time)
) PARTITION BY RANGE (event_time);
```

Partitions:

```sql id="ecw6nk"
CREATE TABLE app.audit_event_new_p2026_07
PARTITION OF app.audit_event_new
FOR VALUES FROM ('2026-07-01 00:00:00+00')
             TO ('2026-08-01 00:00:00+00');

CREATE TABLE app.audit_event_new_p2026_08
PARTITION OF app.audit_event_new
FOR VALUES FROM ('2026-08-01 00:00:00+00')
             TO ('2026-09-01 00:00:00+00');
```

---

# 8. Backfill in Chunks

## 8.1 Jangan Backfill Semua Sekaligus

Buruk:

```sql id="ejaq2n"
INSERT INTO app.audit_event_new
SELECT *
FROM app.audit_event_old;
```

Masalah:

| Problem | Dampak |
|---|---|
| Transaction besar | WAL besar, rollback mahal |
| Lock/resource pressure | Production terganggu |
| Error di tengah | Recovery sulit |
| Replication lag | Replica tertinggal |
| Autovacuum/statistics | Planner bisa buruk |
| Monitoring susah | Progress tidak granular |

## 8.2 Backfill per Partition Window

```sql id="z1dcos"
INSERT INTO app.audit_event_new (
    event_id,
    tenant_id,
    case_id,
    event_time,
    event_type,
    actor_id,
    payload,
    created_at
)
SELECT
    event_id,
    tenant_id,
    case_id,
    event_time,
    event_type,
    actor_id,
    payload,
    created_at
FROM app.audit_event_old
WHERE event_time >= TIMESTAMPTZ '2026-07-01 00:00:00+00'
  AND event_time <  TIMESTAMPTZ '2026-08-01 00:00:00+00'
ON CONFLICT (event_id, event_time) DO NOTHING;
```

`ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING` berguna jika backfill retryable. Namun, jangan gunakan untuk menyembunyikan masalah data tanpa logging. Simpan row count expected vs inserted.

## 8.3 Backfill per ID Chunk dalam Window

Jika satu bulan masih terlalu besar:

```sql id="sw0srh"
INSERT INTO app.audit_event_new (
    event_id,
    tenant_id,
    case_id,
    event_time,
    event_type,
    actor_id,
    payload,
    created_at
)
SELECT
    event_id,
    tenant_id,
    case_id,
    event_time,
    event_type,
    actor_id,
    payload,
    created_at
FROM app.audit_event_old
WHERE event_time >= TIMESTAMPTZ '2026-07-01 00:00:00+00'
  AND event_time <  TIMESTAMPTZ '2026-08-01 00:00:00+00'
  AND event_id > :last_event_id
ORDER BY event_id
LIMIT 10000
ON CONFLICT (event_id, event_time) DO NOTHING;
```

Caveat: UUID ordering tidak selalu correlated dengan time. Untuk chunking stabil, bisa gunakan synthetic sequence, created_at, atau CTID pattern dengan hati-hati.

---

# 9. Backfill Metadata Table

Jangan migrasi tanpa progress tracking.

```sql id="u3ka73"
CREATE TABLE app.partition_migration_batch (
    migration_name     text        NOT NULL,
    batch_id           bigserial   NOT NULL,
    source_table       text        NOT NULL,
    target_table       text        NOT NULL,
    lower_bound        timestamptz NOT NULL,
    upper_bound        timestamptz NOT NULL,
    status             text        NOT NULL,
    expected_count     bigint,
    inserted_count     bigint,
    started_at         timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    finished_at        timestamptz,
    error_message      text,

    CONSTRAINT partition_migration_batch_pk
        PRIMARY KEY (migration_name, batch_id),

    CONSTRAINT partition_migration_batch_status_chk
        CHECK (status IN ('PENDING', 'RUNNING', 'SUCCESS', 'FAILED', 'SKIPPED'))
);
```

Insert planned batch:

```sql id="rwxact"
INSERT INTO app.partition_migration_batch (
    migration_name,
    source_table,
    target_table,
    lower_bound,
    upper_bound,
    status
)
VALUES (
    'audit_event_partitioning_2026',
    'app.audit_event_old',
    'app.audit_event_new',
    '2026-07-01 00:00:00+00',
    '2026-08-01 00:00:00+00',
    'PENDING'
);
```

---

# 10. Strategy C - Staging Partition + ATTACH

Ini sering paling production-friendly untuk historical data.

## 10.1 Flow

```text id="cabre8"
For each partition window:
1. Create standalone staging table
2. Load data for that window
3. Validate row range
4. Add CHECK constraint matching bound
5. Create indexes
6. ANALYZE
7. ATTACH PARTITION to parent
8. Validate attached partition
9. Record migration metadata
```

Keuntungan:

| Keuntungan | Penjelasan |
|---|---|
| Backfill terisolasi | Parent tidak terus-menerus menerima massive insert |
| Index build terkontrol | Index staging sebelum attach |
| Attach cepat jika CHECK valid | Mengurangi scan validasi |
| Rollback per partition | Bisa drop staging jika gagal |
| Progress granular | Per bulan/hari |

PostgreSQL docs menjelaskan bahwa `ALTER TABLE ... ATTACH PARTITION` dapat dipakai untuk memasukkan table existing ke partition hierarchy. Jika table yang akan di-attach memiliki `CHECK` constraint yang cocok dengan partition constraint, PostgreSQL dapat menghindari scan validasi penuh yang mahal. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/ddl-partitioning.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 10.2 Create Parent

```sql id="ryuqha"
CREATE TABLE app.audit_event_new (
    event_id    uuid        NOT NULL,
    tenant_id   uuid        NOT NULL,
    case_id     uuid        NOT NULL,
    event_time  timestamptz NOT NULL,
    event_type  text        NOT NULL,
    actor_id    uuid,
    payload     jsonb       NOT NULL,
    created_at  timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),

    CONSTRAINT audit_event_new_pk
        PRIMARY KEY (event_id, event_time)
) PARTITION BY RANGE (event_time);
```

## 10.3 Create Staging Table

```sql id="zet2if"
CREATE TABLE app.audit_event_stage_2026_07 (
    LIKE app.audit_event_new INCLUDING DEFAULTS INCLUDING CONSTRAINTS
);
```

## 10.4 Load Data

```sql id="c0drpi"
INSERT INTO app.audit_event_stage_2026_07 (
    event_id,
    tenant_id,
    case_id,
    event_time,
    event_type,
    actor_id,
    payload,
    created_at
)
SELECT
    event_id,
    tenant_id,
    case_id,
    event_time,
    event_type,
    actor_id,
    payload,
    created_at
FROM app.audit_event_old
WHERE event_time >= TIMESTAMPTZ '2026-07-01 00:00:00+00'
  AND event_time <  TIMESTAMPTZ '2026-08-01 00:00:00+00';
```

## 10.5 Validate Range

```sql id="ymyr3j"
SELECT COUNT(*) AS invalid_rows
FROM app.audit_event_stage_2026_07
WHERE NOT (
    event_time >= TIMESTAMPTZ '2026-07-01 00:00:00+00'
    AND event_time < TIMESTAMPTZ '2026-08-01 00:00:00+00'
);
```

Expected:

```text id="5j3xvd"
invalid_rows = 0
```

## 10.6 Add CHECK Bound

```sql id="6vlp0w"
ALTER TABLE app.audit_event_stage_2026_07
ADD CONSTRAINT audit_event_stage_2026_07_bound_chk
CHECK (
    event_time >= TIMESTAMPTZ '2026-07-01 00:00:00+00'
    AND event_time < TIMESTAMPTZ '2026-08-01 00:00:00+00'
);
```

## 10.7 Create Indexes

```sql id="pp5m2z"
CREATE INDEX audit_event_stage_2026_07_tenant_time_idx
ON app.audit_event_stage_2026_07 (tenant_id, event_time DESC);

CREATE INDEX audit_event_stage_2026_07_case_time_idx
ON app.audit_event_stage_2026_07 (case_id, event_time DESC);

CREATE INDEX audit_event_stage_2026_07_event_type_time_idx
ON app.audit_event_stage_2026_07 (event_type, event_time DESC);
```

## 10.8 Analyze

```sql id="x0l2rf"
ANALYZE app.audit_event_stage_2026_07;
```

`ANALYZE` penting setelah bulk load agar planner punya statistics yang benar sebelum query production diarahkan ke partition baru.

## 10.9 Attach

```sql id="e5yzzq"
ALTER TABLE app.audit_event_new
ATTACH PARTITION app.audit_event_stage_2026_07
FOR VALUES FROM ('2026-07-01 00:00:00+00')
             TO ('2026-08-01 00:00:00+00');
```

## 10.10 Rename Attached Partition

```sql id="g0myy8"
ALTER TABLE app.audit_event_stage_2026_07
RENAME TO audit_event_new_p2026_07;
```

---

# 11. COPY-based Bulk Load

`COPY FROM` bisa digunakan pada partitioned table maupun staging table. Dokumentasi PostgreSQL menyatakan `COPY FROM` dapat digunakan dengan plain, foreign, atau partitioned tables, serta view dengan `INSTEAD OF INSERT` trigger. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-copy.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

## 11.1 COPY ke Parent Partitioned Table

```sql id="uzxhc8"
COPY app.audit_event_new (
    event_id,
    tenant_id,
    case_id,
    event_time,
    event_type,
    actor_id,
    payload,
    created_at
)
FROM '/data/audit_event_2026_07.csv'
WITH (FORMAT csv, HEADER true);
```

Kelebihan:

| Kelebihan | Catatan |
|---|---|
| Routing otomatis | Data masuk partition sesuai key |
| Sederhana | Tidak perlu staging per window |
| Cepat dibanding row insert | Cocok untuk bulk import |

Risiko:

| Risiko | Catatan |
|---|---|
| Error satu row bisa menggagalkan load | Perlu clean file/staging |
| Routing ke banyak partition | Bisa menyebar I/O |
| Index maintenance langsung | Bisa lebih lambat jika index sudah ada |
| Validation kurang granular | Sulit isolasi error per partition |

## 11.2 COPY ke Staging Table

```sql id="7lrgi8"
COPY app.audit_event_stage_2026_07 (
    event_id,
    tenant_id,
    case_id,
    event_time,
    event_type,
    actor_id,
    payload,
    created_at
)
FROM '/data/audit_event_2026_07.csv'
WITH (FORMAT csv, HEADER true);
```

Rekomendasi untuk backfill besar:

```text id="pu2j36"
COPY ke staging table → validate → index → analyze → attach.
```

---

# 12. Dual-write Strategy

## 12.1 Tujuan

Selama backfill berjalan, data baru tetap masuk ke old table. Agar shadow table tidak tertinggal, ada beberapa opsi:

```text id="tph8ft"
1. Application dual-write
2. Database trigger mirroring
3. CDC/logical replication
4. Stop writes during final delta copy
```

## 12.2 Application Dual-write

Service menulis ke old dan new dalam satu transaction jika masih satu database.

```java id="3ur7dx"
public void appendAuditEvent(AuditEvent event) {
    try (SqlSession session = sqlSessionFactory.openSession(false)) {
        AuditEventMapper mapper = session.getMapper(AuditEventMapper.class);

        mapper.insertAuditEventOld(event);
        mapper.insertAuditEventNew(event);

        session.commit();
    } catch (Exception e) {
        session.rollback();
        throw e;
    }
}
```

Kelebihan:

| Kelebihan | Penjelasan |
|---|---|
| Explicit di aplikasi | Mudah dipahami |
| Transactional jika satu DB | Old/new konsisten |
| Mudah feature flag | Bisa disable |
| Observable | Log aplikasi bisa melacak |

Kekurangan:

| Kekurangan | Penjelasan |
|---|---|
| Aplikasi jadi kompleks sementara | Migration logic masuk code |
| Write latency naik | Dua insert |
| Error handling rumit | Old success/new fail harus atomic |
| Harus dibersihkan setelah cutover | Technical debt |

## 12.3 Trigger Mirroring

Trigger di old table menulis ke new table.

```sql id="9jxkjk"
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION app.mirror_audit_event_to_new()
RETURNS trigger
LANGUAGE plpgsql
AS $$
BEGIN
    INSERT INTO app.audit_event_new (
        event_id,
        tenant_id,
        case_id,
        event_time,
        event_type,
        actor_id,
        payload,
        created_at
    )
    VALUES (
        NEW.event_id,
        NEW.tenant_id,
        NEW.case_id,
        NEW.event_time,
        NEW.event_type,
        NEW.actor_id,
        NEW.payload,
        NEW.created_at
    )
    ON CONFLICT (event_id, event_time) DO NOTHING;

    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$;

CREATE TRIGGER audit_event_old_mirror_to_new_trg
AFTER INSERT ON app.audit_event_old
FOR EACH ROW
EXECUTE FUNCTION app.mirror_audit_event_to_new();
```

Kelebihan:

| Kelebihan | Penjelasan |
|---|---|
| Aplikasi tidak perlu dual-write | DB menangani mirror |
| Transactional | Insert old dan mirror new satu transaction |
| Cocok untuk temporary migration | Bisa drop trigger setelah cutover |

Kekurangan:

| Kekurangan | Penjelasan |
|---|---|
| Hidden write cost | Aplikasi tidak terlihat langsung |
| Trigger bisa memperlambat old table | Write latency naik |
| Error di trigger bisa menggagalkan insert old | Harus hati-hati |
| Debug lebih sulit | Logic tersembunyi di DB |

## 12.4 Final Delta Copy

Jika tidak dual-write sepanjang backfill, lakukan final delta saat write freeze singkat.

```text id="opx9mf"
1. Backfill historical data sampai T0
2. Stop writes briefly
3. Copy rows created_at >= T0
4. Validate
5. Cutover
6. Resume writes to new
```

SQL delta:

```sql id="rvtznj"
INSERT INTO app.audit_event_new (
    event_id,
    tenant_id,
    case_id,
    event_time,
    event_type,
    actor_id,
    payload,
    created_at
)
SELECT
    event_id,
    tenant_id,
    case_id,
    event_time,
    event_type,
    actor_id,
    payload,
    created_at
FROM app.audit_event_old
WHERE created_at >= :backfill_cutoff
ON CONFLICT (event_id, event_time) DO NOTHING;
```

---

# 13. Cutover Patterns

## 13.1 Rename Swap

Setelah validation:

```sql id="juq87u"
BEGIN;

ALTER TABLE app.audit_event_old RENAME TO audit_event_legacy;
ALTER TABLE app.audit_event_new RENAME TO audit_event;

COMMIT;
```

Kelebihan:

| Kelebihan | Penjelasan |
|---|---|
| Aplikasi tetap pakai nama table sama | Mapper minimal berubah |
| Rollback rename mungkin | Jika belum ada write baru |
| Sederhana | Mudah dipahami |

Risiko:

| Risiko | Penjelasan |
|---|---|
| FK/view/dependency | Rename bisa berdampak |
| Sequence/default/index names | Perlu dirapikan |
| Privileges | Pastikan grants sama |
| Trigger/function references | Cek dependency |
| Rollback setelah write baru | Sulit jika new sudah menerima data |

## 13.2 View Compatibility Layer

Buat view dengan nama lama:

```sql id="r0cvss"
CREATE VIEW app.audit_event AS
SELECT
    event_id,
    tenant_id,
    case_id,
    event_time,
    event_type,
    actor_id,
    payload,
    created_at
FROM app.audit_event_new;
```

Cocok untuk read compatibility, tetapi untuk write butuh `INSTEAD OF` trigger atau aplikasi diarahkan ke table baru. Untuk high-write table, view write layer biasanya tidak ideal.

## 13.3 Application Feature Flag

Aplikasi punya flag:

```text id="xp3eva"
auditEventWriteTarget = OLD | DUAL | NEW
auditEventReadTarget  = OLD | NEW
```

Flow:

```text id="tw64vf"
1. OLD/OLD
2. DUAL/OLD
3. DUAL/NEW
4. NEW/NEW
5. Remove old path
```

Ini lebih kompleks tetapi rollback lebih terkendali.

---

# 14. Validation Strategy

## 14.1 Row Count per Partition Window

```sql id="v1spw0"
SELECT COUNT(*) AS old_count
FROM app.audit_event_old
WHERE event_time >= TIMESTAMPTZ '2026-07-01 00:00:00+00'
  AND event_time <  TIMESTAMPTZ '2026-08-01 00:00:00+00';

SELECT COUNT(*) AS new_count
FROM app.audit_event_new
WHERE event_time >= TIMESTAMPTZ '2026-07-01 00:00:00+00'
  AND event_time <  TIMESTAMPTZ '2026-08-01 00:00:00+00';
```

## 14.2 Boundary Min/Max

```sql id="1gfq86"
SELECT
    min(event_time) AS min_event_time,
    max(event_time) AS max_event_time
FROM app.audit_event_new_p2026_07;
```

Expected:

```text id="pvclio"
min_event_time >= 2026-07-01
max_event_time <  2026-08-01
```

## 14.3 Checksum per Window

Simple checksum:

```sql id="7g2owf"
SELECT
    md5(string_agg(event_id::text, ',' ORDER BY event_id)) AS checksum
FROM app.audit_event_old
WHERE event_time >= TIMESTAMPTZ '2026-07-01 00:00:00+00'
  AND event_time <  TIMESTAMPTZ '2026-08-01 00:00:00+00';
```

Untuk data besar, `string_agg` bisa berat. Alternatif lebih ringan:

```sql id="usso4l"
SELECT
    COUNT(*) AS row_count,
    SUM(hashtext(event_id::text)) AS event_id_hash_sum,
    MIN(event_time) AS min_event_time,
    MAX(event_time) AS max_event_time
FROM app.audit_event_old
WHERE event_time >= TIMESTAMPTZ '2026-07-01 00:00:00+00'
  AND event_time <  TIMESTAMPTZ '2026-08-01 00:00:00+00';
```

Lakukan query yang sama di new table dan bandingkan.

Catatan: hash sum bukan proof cryptographic. Untuk regulatory-grade validation, gunakan stronger checksum strategy per chunk, manifest, dan sampling payload.

---

## 14.4 Anti-join Validation

Cari row old yang belum ada di new:

```sql id="fj16qz"
SELECT o.event_id, o.event_time
FROM app.audit_event_old o
LEFT JOIN app.audit_event_new n
  ON n.event_id = o.event_id
 AND n.event_time = o.event_time
WHERE o.event_time >= TIMESTAMPTZ '2026-07-01 00:00:00+00'
  AND o.event_time <  TIMESTAMPTZ '2026-08-01 00:00:00+00'
  AND n.event_id IS NULL
LIMIT 100;
```

Cari unexpected extra row di new:

```sql id="1r1mnc"
SELECT n.event_id, n.event_time
FROM app.audit_event_new n
LEFT JOIN app.audit_event_old o
  ON o.event_id = n.event_id
 AND o.event_time = n.event_time
WHERE n.event_time >= TIMESTAMPTZ '2026-07-01 00:00:00+00'
  AND n.event_time <  TIMESTAMPTZ '2026-08-01 00:00:00+00'
  AND o.event_id IS NULL
LIMIT 100;
```

---

# 15. Query Plan Validation Setelah Cutover

Pastikan partition pruning berjalan.

```sql id="niqw8j"
EXPLAIN (ANALYZE, BUFFERS)
SELECT
    event_id,
    tenant_id,
    case_id,
    event_time,
    event_type
FROM app.audit_event_new
WHERE tenant_id = '00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000001'
  AND event_time >= TIMESTAMPTZ '2026-07-01 00:00:00+00'
  AND event_time <  TIMESTAMPTZ '2026-08-01 00:00:00+00'
ORDER BY event_time DESC
LIMIT 100;
```

Expected:

```text id="stki3m"
- hanya partition July
- Index Scan / Bitmap Scan sesuai access pattern
- Index Cond mengandung tenant_id dan event_time
- no full append semua partition
```

---

# 16. Rollback Plan

## 16.1 Rollback sebelum cutover

Mudah:

```text id="f6jg3l"
- drop audit_event_new
- drop trigger dual-write jika ada
- aplikasi tetap old
```

## 16.2 Rollback setelah read cutover tapi write masih dual

Masih relatif aman:

```text id="g4rulo"
- switch read target back to OLD
- keep dual-write
- investigate new
```

## 16.3 Rollback setelah write hanya ke new

Sulit. Harus punya reverse sync:

```text id="oevf6m"
- data baru di new harus disalin kembali ke old
- PK lama mungkin tidak kompatibel
- FK/dependency bisa berubah
- downtime mungkin diperlukan
```

Karena itu, jangan terlalu cepat mematikan old write path sampai confidence window cukup.

## 16.4 Rollback Checklist

| Tahap | Rollback |
|---|---|
| Shadow table created | Drop shadow |
| Backfill running | Stop job, truncate/drop affected staging |
| Dual-write enabled | Disable dual-write flag/trigger |
| Read cutover | Revert read flag |
| Write cutover | Re-enable old or reverse-sync |
| Rename swap | Rename back if no incompatible writes |
| Old table dropped | Restore from backup only |

Rule:

```text id="jgx740"
Jangan drop old table sampai:
1. validation selesai
2. monitoring stabil
3. rollback window lewat
4. business owner approve
```

---

# 17. Foreign Key and Dependency Check

Sebelum rename/drop old table:

```sql id="utxzws"
SELECT
    conrelid::regclass AS referencing_table,
    conname AS constraint_name,
    confrelid::regclass AS referenced_table,
    pg_get_constraintdef(oid) AS constraint_def
FROM pg_constraint
WHERE contype = 'f'
  AND confrelid = 'app.audit_event_old'::regclass;
```

Cek view/function dependency:

```sql id="0c2x0g"
SELECT
    dependent_ns.nspname AS dependent_schema,
    dependent_view.relname AS dependent_object,
    source_ns.nspname AS source_schema,
    source_table.relname AS source_table
FROM pg_depend d
JOIN pg_rewrite r ON r.oid = d.objid
JOIN pg_class dependent_view ON dependent_view.oid = r.ev_class
JOIN pg_class source_table ON source_table.oid = d.refobjid
JOIN pg_namespace dependent_ns ON dependent_ns.oid = dependent_view.relnamespace
JOIN pg_namespace source_ns ON source_ns.oid = source_table.relnamespace
WHERE source_table.oid = 'app.audit_event_old'::regclass;
```

---

# 18. Handling Primary Key Change in App

Old:

```java id="oxq6lw"
public record AuditEventKey(UUID eventId) {}
```

New:

```java id="tc3whu"
public record AuditEventKey(
    UUID eventId,
    OffsetDateTime eventTime
) {}
```

But public API might stay same if lookup endpoint requires date range:

```http id="g0tws0"
GET /audit-events/{eventId}?from=2026-07-01T00:00:00Z&to=2026-08-01T00:00:00Z
```

Mapper:

```xml id="qntmwu"
<select id="findAuditEventByIdAndRange" resultMap="AuditEventResultMap">
    SELECT
        event_id,
        tenant_id,
        case_id,
        event_time,
        event_type,
        actor_id,
        payload,
        created_at
    FROM app.audit_event
    WHERE event_id = #{eventId}
      AND event_time &gt;= #{fromTime}
      AND event_time &lt;  #{toTime}
</select>
```

Jika harus lookup by `event_id` saja, gunakan registry table:

```sql id="6y42z6"
SELECT
    e.*
FROM app.audit_event_registry r
JOIN app.audit_event e
  ON e.event_id = r.event_id
 AND e.event_time = r.event_time
WHERE r.event_id = :event_id;
```

# 19. MyBatis Backfill Job Pattern

## 19.1 Java Batch Job Skeleton

```java id="6giyva"
public final class AuditEventPartitionBackfillJob {

    private final SqlSessionFactory sqlSessionFactory;
    private final int batchSize;

    public void run(OffsetDateTime from, OffsetDateTime to) {
        OffsetDateTime cursor = from;

        while (cursor.isBefore(to)) {
            OffsetDateTime next = cursor.plusMonths(1);

            backfillWindow(cursor, next);

            cursor = next;
        }
    }

    private void backfillWindow(OffsetDateTime from, OffsetDateTime to) {
        try (SqlSession session = sqlSessionFactory.openSession(false)) {
            AuditEventMigrationMapper mapper =
                session.getMapper(AuditEventMigrationMapper.class);

            mapper.markBatchRunning(from, to);

            long inserted = mapper.backfillWindow(from, to);

            mapper.markBatchSuccess(from, to, inserted);

            session.commit();
        } catch (Exception ex) {
            // log and mark failed in a separate transaction if needed
            throw ex;
        }
    }
}
```

## 19.2 Mapper

```xml id="d33cpl"
<insert id="backfillWindow">
    INSERT INTO app.audit_event_new (
        event_id,
        tenant_id,
        case_id,
        event_time,
        event_type,
        actor_id,
        payload,
        created_at
    )
    SELECT
        event_id,
        tenant_id,
        case_id,
        event_time,
        event_type,
        actor_id,
        payload,
        created_at
    FROM app.audit_event_old
    WHERE event_time &gt;= #{fromTime}
      AND event_time &lt;  #{toTime}
    ON CONFLICT (event_id, event_time) DO NOTHING
</insert>
```

Untuk data sangat besar, jangan satu mapper call per bulan jika bulan terlalu besar. Pecah lagi per day/chunk.

---

# 20. Operational Throttling

Backfill bisa mengganggu production. Tambahkan throttle.

```text id="s707se"
- batch size limit
- sleep between batches
- max runtime window
- stop if replication lag high
- stop if lock wait high
- stop if CPU/IO above threshold
- run only in maintenance hours
```

Pseudo-code:

```java id="e5c6hx"
if (metrics.replicationLagSeconds() > 30) {
    pause("Replication lag too high");
}

if (metrics.databaseCpuPercent() > 80) {
    pause("Database CPU too high");
}

Thread.sleep(Duration.ofSeconds(2));
```

# 21. Migration Pipeline dengan GitHub/Maven

Struktur repository:

```text id="xl1aar"
db/
├─ migration/
│  ├─ V001__create_audit_event_new_parent.sql
│  ├─ V002__create_audit_event_new_partitions_2026.sql
│  ├─ V003__create_audit_event_new_indexes.sql
│  ├─ V004__create_migration_tracking.sql
│  ├─ V005__create_mirror_trigger_optional.sql
│  └─ V006__cutover_rename.sql
│
├─ backfill/
│  ├─ backfill_audit_event_2026_01.sql
│  ├─ backfill_audit_event_2026_02.sql
│  └─ validate_audit_event_window.sql
│
└─ rollback/
   ├─ rollback_disable_mirror_trigger.sql
   ├─ rollback_read_target.md
   └─ rollback_rename_swap.sql
```

CI checks:

```text id="6ieac5"
1. Apply migration to ephemeral PostgreSQL
2. Insert sample old data
3. Run backfill
4. Validate row count
5. Run EXPLAIN pruning test
6. Verify PK/unique/FK constraints
7. Test rollback scripts where possible
```

# 22. Lock and Timeout Safety

Set timeouts for migration sessions:

```sql id="vghfsf"
SET lock_timeout = '5s';
SET statement_timeout = '30min';
```

For long backfill, statement timeout may need a controlled value. For DDL lock-sensitive operations, low `lock_timeout` is useful so deployment fails fast instead of blocking production.

For `CREATE INDEX CONCURRENTLY`, remember it cannot run inside a transaction block; this matters for migration tools. PostgreSQL docs also note concurrent index builds on partitioned parent table are not directly supported; build concurrently on each partition, then create/attach the parent partitioned index. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-altertable.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

# 23. Migration Validation Dashboard Queries

## 23.1 Batch status

```sql id="txlcx1"
SELECT
    migration_name,
    lower_bound,
    upper_bound,
    status,
    expected_count,
    inserted_count,
    started_at,
    finished_at,
    error_message
FROM app.partition_migration_batch
WHERE migration_name = 'audit_event_partitioning_2026'
ORDER BY lower_bound;
```

## 23.2 Old vs new count by month

```sql id="3sq7gs"
WITH old_counts AS (
    SELECT
        date_trunc('month', event_time) AS month_bucket,
        COUNT(*) AS old_count
    FROM app.audit_event_old
    GROUP BY date_trunc('month', event_time)
),
new_counts AS (
    SELECT
        date_trunc('month', event_time) AS month_bucket,
        COUNT(*) AS new_count
    FROM app.audit_event_new
    GROUP BY date_trunc('month', event_time)
)
SELECT
    COALESCE(o.month_bucket, n.month_bucket) AS month_bucket,
    COALESCE(o.old_count, 0) AS old_count,
    COALESCE(n.new_count, 0) AS new_count,
    COALESCE(o.old_count, 0) - COALESCE(n.new_count, 0) AS diff
FROM old_counts o
FULL OUTER JOIN new_counts n
  ON n.month_bucket = o.month_bucket
ORDER BY month_bucket;
```

## 23.3 Missing rows sample

```sql id="t8j1fw"
SELECT o.event_id, o.event_time
FROM app.audit_event_old o
WHERE o.event_time >= :fromTime
  AND o.event_time <  :toTime
  AND NOT EXISTS (
      SELECT 1
      FROM app.audit_event_new n
      WHERE n.event_id = o.event_id
        AND n.event_time = o.event_time
  )
LIMIT 100;
```

# 24. Production Cutover Checklist

| Checklist | Status |
|---|---|
| Target partitioned schema created |
| Historical partitions created |
| Future partitions created |
| Default partition policy clear |
| Indexes exist on all target partitions |
| Backfill completed |
| Dual-write or final delta completed |
| Row count validation passed |
| Checksum/sample validation passed |
| Anti-join missing row check passed |
| EXPLAIN pruning check passed |
| App mapper supports new PK/query contract |
| FK/dependency reviewed |
| Grants/permissions copied |
| Monitoring dashboards ready |
| Rollback plan rehearsed |
| Old table retained |
| Business/ops approval obtained |

---

# 25. Common Migration Failure Modes

| Failure mode | Gejala | Root cause | Mitigasi |
|---|---|---|---|
| Insert to new fails | No matching partition | Missing historical/future partition | Generate partitions first |
| Backfill too slow | Long runtime, high I/O | One huge insert | Chunk per window |
| Attach partition slow | DDL waiting/scanning | No matching CHECK constraint | Add CHECK before attach |
| Index build blocks | App latency | Non-concurrent index on big table | Build concurrently per partition |
| Data mismatch | Count/checksum diff | Missed delta writes | Dual-write/final delta |
| Duplicate conflict | Backfill retry inserts duplicate | No idempotent strategy | `ON CONFLICT`, registry, batch metadata |
| App query slow after cutover | All partitions scanned | Missing date predicate | Update API/mapper |
| FK breaks cutover | Rename/drop blocked | Dependencies not mapped | Dependency audit |
| Rollback impossible | Old table dropped too early | Over-aggressive cleanup | Keep old until confidence window |
| Default partition grows | Late/out-of-bound data | Partition gap | Monitor and move data |

---

# 26. Recommended Migration Pattern untuk Enterprise

Untuk tabel besar seperti `audit_event`, `transaction_history`, `workflow_task_history`:

```text id="1up4cn"
Recommended:
1. Create shadow partitioned table
2. Create partitions ahead
3. Backfill by partition window
4. Use staging + attach for large historical windows
5. Enable dual-write or final delta freeze
6. Validate per window
7. Switch reads first
8. Switch writes after confidence
9. Keep old table for rollback
10. Drop/archive old table only after approval
```

Untuk tabel kecil/sedang:

```text id="tdxvzc"
Offline rebuild boleh,
tapi tetap harus punya validation dan rollback.
```

---

# Kesimpulan Seri 11

Migration ke partitioned table adalah **data migration + application migration + operational migration** sekaligus.

Rule paling penting:

```text id="l4bimh"
1. Jangan migrasi tabel besar dalam satu transaksi besar.
2. Gunakan shadow table untuk downtime rendah.
3. Gunakan staging + CHECK + index + ANALYZE + ATTACH untuk historical partition besar.
4. `COPY FROM` bisa dipakai, tetapi staging sering lebih aman untuk backfill besar.
5. Dual-write atau final delta wajib jika old table masih menerima write.
6. Validasi harus per partition window: count, checksum, anti-join, boundary, EXPLAIN.
7. PK/unique/FK perubahan harus diselesaikan sebelum cutover.
8. Rollback plan harus realistis, bukan formalitas.
9. Old table jangan drop terlalu cepat.
10. Migration pipeline harus punya smoke test dan observability.
```

**Seri 11/14 selesai.**

Rekomendasi materi berikutnya: **Seri 12 - Maintenance & Observability: VACUUM, ANALYZE, bloat, partition size skew, default partition monitoring, catalog query, pg_stat views, alerting, dan runbook production.**
