# Seri 14/14 - Java/Jersey/MyBatis Integration Pattern for PostgreSQL Partitioning

## 1. Tujuan Seri Ini

Seri terakhir ini menyatukan semua materi PostgreSQL partitioning ke dalam pola implementasi aplikasi **Java 17+ / Jersey / MyBatis**.

Fokusnya:

```text id="u7bm4g"
1. API contract yang partition-friendly
2. DTO date boundary yang aman
3. MyBatis mapper yang tidak merusak pruning
4. Insert/update/delete pattern yang aman
5. Error handling production
6. Testcontainers/CI validation
7. Health endpoint untuk partition lifecycle
8. Operational usage pattern
```

Secara database, PostgreSQL partitioning efektif jika query membawa predicate partition key sehingga partition pruning bisa bekerja. PostgreSQL juga punya batasan penting: `PRIMARY KEY`/`UNIQUE` pada partitioned table harus mencakup semua partition key. Dari sisi aplikasi, berarti API, DTO, mapper, dan test harus ikut “sadar partitioning”, bukan hanya DDL. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/ddl-partitioning.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

MyBatis cocok untuk pola ini karena memang berfokus pada custom SQL, stored procedure, dan advanced mappings; Mapper XML adalah tempat utama mapped statements didefinisikan. ([MyBatis](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-3/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 2. Big Picture Integration

```text id="c2vc71"
Jersey API
│
├─ Request contract
│  ├─ tenantId
│  ├─ fromTime
│  ├─ toTime
│  ├─ limit
│  └─ cursor
│
├─ Validation layer
│  ├─ required partition key range
│  ├─ max date range
│  ├─ UTC normalization
│  ├─ limit cap
│  └─ no unsafe query mode
│
├─ Service layer
│  ├─ builds explicit access pattern
│  ├─ no generic unrestricted search
│  ├─ handles idempotency
│  ├─ maps SQLSTATE
│  └─ emits observability
│
├─ MyBatis mapper
│  ├─ SQL includes partition predicate
│  ├─ half-open interval
│  ├─ typed parameters
│  ├─ index-friendly ORDER BY
│  └─ no function wrapping partition key
│
├─ PostgreSQL
│  ├─ parent partitioned table
│  ├─ child partitions
│  ├─ local/partitioned indexes
│  ├─ pruning
│  └─ constraints
│
└─ Ops/Tests
   ├─ EXPLAIN tests
   ├─ insert routing tests
   ├─ partition health endpoint
   ├─ default partition alert
   └─ future horizon alert
```

---

# 3. Core Rule for Application Layer

```text id="vu0g5h"
Kalau table dipartisi by event_time,
maka endpoint, service, dan mapper harus menjadikan event_time range sebagai first-class contract.
```

Buruk:

```http id="bbzhk8"
GET /audit-events?caseId=11111111-1111-1111-1111-111111111111
```

Baik:

```http id="tx0piz"
GET /audit-events?caseId=11111111-1111-1111-1111-111111111111
  &from=2026-07-01T00:00:00Z
  &to=2026-08-01T00:00:00Z
  &limit=100
```

Jakarta REST mendukung binding query parameter menggunakan `@QueryParam`, sehingga boundary seperti `from`/`to` sebaiknya menjadi bagian eksplisit dari resource method atau request bean. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/learn/docs/jakartaee-tutorial/current/websvcs/rest/rest.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 4. API Contract Pattern

## 4.1 Audit Event Search

```http id="o1no56"
GET /v1/audit-events
    ?tenantId={uuid}
    &caseId={uuid optional}
    &from={ISO-8601 offset datetime}
    &to={ISO-8601 offset datetime}
    &limit=100
```

Rules:

| Parameter | Required | Reason |
|---|---:|---|
| `tenantId` | Ya | Multi-tenant isolation dan index predicate |
| `from` | Ya | Partition pruning lower bound |
| `to` | Ya | Partition pruning upper bound |
| `caseId` | Opsional | Secondary filter |
| `eventType` | Opsional | Secondary filter |
| `limit` | Ya/default | Latency guardrail |
| `cursor` | Opsional | Keyset pagination |

---

## 4.2 DTO Request

```java id="6pu1rm"
public record AuditEventSearchRequest(
    UUID tenantId,
    UUID caseId,
    String eventType,
    OffsetDateTime fromTime,
    OffsetDateTime toTime,
    Integer limit,
    OffsetDateTime cursorEventTime,
    UUID cursorEventId
) {}
```

Kenapa `OffsetDateTime`?

```text id="sd0iwp"
- cocok untuk input ISO-8601 dengan offset
- bisa dinormalisasi ke UTC
- menghindari ambiguitas timezone
```

---

# 5. Jersey Resource Pattern

```java id="la8ywl"
@Path("/v1/audit-events")
@Produces(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
@Consumes(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
public class AuditEventResource {

    private final AuditEventService auditEventService;

    public AuditEventResource(AuditEventService auditEventService) {
        this.auditEventService = auditEventService;
    }

    @GET
    public Response search(
            @QueryParam("tenantId") UUID tenantId,
            @QueryParam("caseId") UUID caseId,
            @QueryParam("eventType") String eventType,
            @QueryParam("from") String from,
            @QueryParam("to") String to,
            @DefaultValue("100") @QueryParam("limit") Integer limit,
            @QueryParam("cursorEventTime") String cursorEventTime,
            @QueryParam("cursorEventId") UUID cursorEventId
    ) {
        AuditEventSearchRequest request = new AuditEventSearchRequest(
                tenantId,
                caseId,
                eventType,
                parseOffsetDateTime(from),
                parseOffsetDateTime(to),
                limit,
                parseOffsetDateTime(cursorEventTime),
                cursorEventId
        );

        AuditEventSearchResponse response = auditEventService.search(request);

        return Response.ok(response).build();
    }

    private OffsetDateTime parseOffsetDateTime(String value) {
        if (value == null || value.isBlank()) {
            return null;
        }

        try {
            return OffsetDateTime.parse(value);
        } catch (DateTimeParseException ex) {
            throw new BadRequestException("Invalid datetime format: " + value);
        }
    }
}
```

Catatan praktis:

```text id="w2fmkf"
Boleh memakai ParamConverter custom untuk OffsetDateTime,
tetapi parsing eksplisit sering lebih mudah diaudit di service enterprise.
```

---

# 6. Validation Pattern

```java id="q8gxxd"
public final class AuditEventSearchValidator {

    private static final int DEFAULT_LIMIT = 100;
    private static final int MAX_LIMIT = 500;
    private static final Duration MAX_RANGE = Duration.ofDays(31);

    public ValidatedAuditEventSearch validate(AuditEventSearchRequest request) {
        if (request.tenantId() == null) {
            throw new BadRequestException("tenantId is required");
        }

        if (request.fromTime() == null || request.toTime() == null) {
            throw new BadRequestException("from and to are required");
        }

        if (!request.toTime().isAfter(request.fromTime())) {
            throw new BadRequestException("to must be after from");
        }

        Duration range = Duration.between(request.fromTime(), request.toTime());
        if (range.compareTo(MAX_RANGE) > 0) {
            throw new BadRequestException("Date range must not exceed 31 days");
        }

        int limit = request.limit() == null ? DEFAULT_LIMIT : request.limit();
        if (limit < 1 || limit > MAX_LIMIT) {
            throw new BadRequestException("limit must be between 1 and 500");
        }

        if (request.cursorEventTime() == null && request.cursorEventId() != null) {
            throw new BadRequestException("cursorEventTime is required when cursorEventId is provided");
        }

        OffsetDateTime fromUtc = request.fromTime().withOffsetSameInstant(ZoneOffset.UTC);
        OffsetDateTime toUtc = request.toTime().withOffsetSameInstant(ZoneOffset.UTC);

        return new ValidatedAuditEventSearch(
                request.tenantId(),
                request.caseId(),
                normalizeText(request.eventType()),
                fromUtc,
                toUtc,
                limit,
                request.cursorEventTime() == null
                        ? null
                        : request.cursorEventTime().withOffsetSameInstant(ZoneOffset.UTC),
                request.cursorEventId()
        );
    }

    private String normalizeText(String value) {
        if (value == null || value.isBlank()) {
            return null;
        }
        return value.trim();
    }
}
```

Validated DTO:

```java id="dijh2w"
public record ValidatedAuditEventSearch(
    UUID tenantId,
    UUID caseId,
    String eventType,
    OffsetDateTime fromTime,
    OffsetDateTime toTime,
    int limit,
    OffsetDateTime cursorEventTime,
    UUID cursorEventId
) {}
```

Rule penting:

```text id="nx1n8e"
Jangan biarkan MyBatis mapper menerima request mentah.
Mapper harus menerima request yang sudah divalidasi dan dinormalisasi.
```

---

# 7. MyBatis Mapper Design

## 7.1 Good Mapper: Tenant + Time

```xml id="0c4kxr"
<select id="searchByTenantAndTime" resultMap="AuditEventResultMap">
    SELECT
        event_id,
        tenant_id,
        case_id,
        event_time,
        event_type,
        actor_id,
        payload,
        created_at
    FROM app.audit_event
    WHERE tenant_id = #{tenantId, javaType=java.util.UUID, jdbcType=OTHER}
      AND event_time &gt;= #{fromTime}
      AND event_time &lt;  #{toTime}

    <if test="caseId != null">
      AND case_id = #{caseId, javaType=java.util.UUID, jdbcType=OTHER}
    </if>

    <if test="eventType != null">
      AND event_type = #{eventType}
    </if>

    <if test="cursorEventTime != null and cursorEventId != null">
      AND (
          event_time &lt; #{cursorEventTime}
          OR (
              event_time = #{cursorEventTime}
              AND event_id &lt; #{cursorEventId, javaType=java.util.UUID, jdbcType=OTHER}
          )
      )
    </if>

    ORDER BY event_time DESC, event_id DESC
    LIMIT #{limit}
</select>
```

Ini aman karena `event_time >=` dan `event_time <` selalu ada.

MyBatis Mapper XML memang didesain untuk menulis SQL eksplisit, termasuk dynamic SQL seperti `<if>`; kekuatannya ada pada mapped statements, sehingga kita bisa membuat query yang persis mengikuti access pattern production. ([MyBatis](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-3/sqlmap-xml.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 7.2 Bad Mapper: Dynamic Search Terlalu Fleksibel

```xml id="98rsdd"
<select id="searchFlexibleUnsafe" resultMap="AuditEventResultMap">
    SELECT *
    FROM app.audit_event
    WHERE 1 = 1

    <if test="tenantId != null">
      AND tenant_id = #{tenantId}
    </if>

    <if test="caseId != null">
      AND case_id = #{caseId}
    </if>

    <if test="fromTime != null">
      AND event_time &gt;= #{fromTime}
    </if>

    <if test="toTime != null">
      AND event_time &lt; #{toTime}
    </if>
</select>
```

Masalah:

```text id="9v4ols"
fromTime/toTime opsional.
Jika caller lupa, query bisa menyentuh semua partition.
```

Pattern yang lebih aman:

```text id="tlz9k7"
Buat mapper per access pattern:
- searchByTenantAndTime
- searchByCaseAndTime
- searchByEventTypeAndTime
- findByEventIdAndRange
```

---

# 8. ResultMap Pattern

```xml id="8ufjc2"
<resultMap id="AuditEventResultMap" type="com.company.audit.AuditEventRecord">
    <id property="eventId" column="event_id" javaType="java.util.UUID"/>
    <result property="tenantId" column="tenant_id" javaType="java.util.UUID"/>
    <result property="caseId" column="case_id" javaType="java.util.UUID"/>
    <result property="eventTime" column="event_time" javaType="java.time.OffsetDateTime"/>
    <result property="eventType" column="event_type"/>
    <result property="actorId" column="actor_id" javaType="java.util.UUID"/>
    <result property="payloadJson" column="payload"/>
    <result property="createdAt" column="created_at" javaType="java.time.OffsetDateTime"/>
</resultMap>
```

Java record:

```java id="snk874"
public record AuditEventRecord(
    UUID eventId,
    UUID tenantId,
    UUID caseId,
    OffsetDateTime eventTime,
    String eventType,
    UUID actorId,
    String payloadJson,
    OffsetDateTime createdAt
) {}
```

---

# 9. Insert Pattern

## 9.1 Application inserts to parent table

```xml id="77fw9q"
<insert id="insertAuditEvent">
    INSERT INTO app.audit_event (
        event_id,
        tenant_id,
        case_id,
        event_time,
        event_type,
        actor_id,
        payload
    )
    VALUES (
        #{eventId, javaType=java.util.UUID, jdbcType=OTHER},
        #{tenantId, javaType=java.util.UUID, jdbcType=OTHER},
        #{caseId, javaType=java.util.UUID, jdbcType=OTHER},
        #{eventTime},
        #{eventType},
        #{actorId, javaType=java.util.UUID, jdbcType=OTHER},
        CAST(#{payloadJson} AS jsonb)
    )
</insert>
```

Aplikasi tidak insert ke `audit_event_p2026_07`. PostgreSQL yang melakukan insert routing ke partition yang cocok.

---

## 9.2 Service Append

```java id="njl7dk"
public AuditEventRecord append(AppendAuditEventCommand command) {
    OffsetDateTime eventTime = command.eventTime() == null
            ? OffsetDateTime.now(ZoneOffset.UTC)
            : command.eventTime().withOffsetSameInstant(ZoneOffset.UTC);

    AuditEventRecord event = new AuditEventRecord(
            UUID.randomUUID(),
            command.tenantId(),
            command.caseId(),
            eventTime,
            command.eventType(),
            command.actorId(),
            command.payloadJson(),
            OffsetDateTime.now(ZoneOffset.UTC)
    );

    try {
        auditEventMapper.insertAuditEvent(event);
        return event;
    } catch (PersistenceException ex) {
        throw persistenceExceptionMapper.map(ex);
    }
}
```

---

# 10. Composite Primary Key Pattern

Jika table partitioned by `event_time`, PK biasanya:

```sql id="2txgnc"
PRIMARY KEY (event_id, event_time)
```

Java key:

```java id="fdvst4"
public record AuditEventKey(
    UUID eventId,
    OffsetDateTime eventTime
) {}
```

Lookup by exact key:

```xml id="0vs1x0"
<select id="findByKey" resultMap="AuditEventResultMap">
    SELECT
        event_id,
        tenant_id,
        case_id,
        event_time,
        event_type,
        actor_id,
        payload,
        created_at
    FROM app.audit_event
    WHERE event_id = #{eventId, javaType=java.util.UUID, jdbcType=OTHER}
      AND event_time = #{eventTime}
</select>
```

Namun untuk API, lebih partition-friendly:

```xml id="m1fxu3"
<select id="findByEventIdAndRange" resultMap="AuditEventResultMap">
    SELECT
        event_id,
        tenant_id,
        case_id,
        event_time,
        event_type,
        actor_id,
        payload,
        created_at
    FROM app.audit_event
    WHERE event_id = #{eventId, javaType=java.util.UUID, jdbcType=OTHER}
      AND event_time &gt;= #{fromTime}
      AND event_time &lt;  #{toTime}
</select>
```

---

# 11. Registry Table Pattern for Global Lookup

Jika public API hanya punya `eventId`, tapi table partitioned by `event_time`, gunakan registry table.

```sql id="8b9oaa"
CREATE TABLE app.audit_event_registry (
    event_id    uuid        NOT NULL,
    event_time  timestamptz NOT NULL,
    tenant_id   uuid        NOT NULL,
    created_at  timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),

    CONSTRAINT audit_event_registry_pk
        PRIMARY KEY (event_id)
);
```

Insert transaction:

```xml id="thc6fe"
<insert id="insertAuditEventRegistry">
    INSERT INTO app.audit_event_registry (
        event_id,
        event_time,
        tenant_id
    )
    VALUES (
        #{eventId, javaType=java.util.UUID, jdbcType=OTHER},
        #{eventTime},
        #{tenantId, javaType=java.util.UUID, jdbcType=OTHER}
    )
</insert>
```

Find by event id:

```xml id="wixdsc"
<select id="findByEventIdViaRegistry" resultMap="AuditEventResultMap">
    SELECT
        e.event_id,
        e.tenant_id,
        e.case_id,
        e.event_time,
        e.event_type,
        e.actor_id,
        e.payload,
        e.created_at
    FROM app.audit_event_registry r
    JOIN app.audit_event e
      ON e.event_id = r.event_id
     AND e.event_time = r.event_time
    WHERE r.event_id = #{eventId, javaType=java.util.UUID, jdbcType=OTHER}
</select>
```

Kelebihan:

```text id="v1gdsf"
- event_id tetap globally unique di DB
- lookup by eventId tetap cepat
- audit_event tetap partition by event_time
```

---

# 12. Keyset Pagination Pattern

Offset pagination buruk untuk history besar.

Buruk:

```sql id="6vhqkr"
OFFSET 100000 LIMIT 100
```

Baik:

```xml id="1sc6ju"
<select id="searchNextPage" resultMap="AuditEventResultMap">
    SELECT
        event_id,
        tenant_id,
        case_id,
        event_time,
        event_type,
        actor_id,
        payload,
        created_at
    FROM app.audit_event
    WHERE tenant_id = #{tenantId, javaType=java.util.UUID, jdbcType=OTHER}
      AND event_time &gt;= #{fromTime}
      AND event_time &lt;  #{toTime}
      AND (
          event_time &lt; #{cursorEventTime}
          OR (
              event_time = #{cursorEventTime}
              AND event_id &lt; #{cursorEventId, javaType=java.util.UUID, jdbcType=OTHER}
          )
      )
    ORDER BY event_time DESC, event_id DESC
    LIMIT #{limit}
</select>
```

Index per partition:

```sql id="0bi325"
CREATE INDEX audit_event_p2026_07_tenant_time_id_idx
ON app.audit_event_p2026_07 (tenant_id, event_time DESC, event_id DESC);
```

---

# 13. Avoid Function on Partition Key

Buruk:

```xml id="rpl0am"
<select id="searchByLocalDateUnsafe" resultMap="AuditEventResultMap">
    SELECT *
    FROM app.audit_event
    WHERE tenant_id = #{tenantId}
      AND date(event_time) = #{localDate}
</select>
```

Baik: hitung boundary di Java.

```java id="4exn60"
public TimeRange toUtcDayRange(LocalDate localDate, ZoneId userZone) {
    ZonedDateTime start = localDate.atStartOfDay(userZone);
    ZonedDateTime end = start.plusDays(1);

    return new TimeRange(
            start.toOffsetDateTime().withOffsetSameInstant(ZoneOffset.UTC),
            end.toOffsetDateTime().withOffsetSameInstant(ZoneOffset.UTC)
    );
}
```

Mapper tetap:

```xml id="n0nnsv"
AND event_time &gt;= #{fromTime}
AND event_time &lt;  #{toTime}
```

---

# 14. Outbox Worker Pattern

## 14.1 Table

```sql id="6e1fgy"
CREATE TABLE app.outbox_event (
    outbox_id     uuid        NOT NULL,
    aggregate_id  uuid        NOT NULL,
    event_type    text        NOT NULL,
    status        text        NOT NULL,
    created_at    timestamptz NOT NULL,
    processed_at  timestamptz,
    retry_count   integer     NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    payload       jsonb       NOT NULL,

    CONSTRAINT outbox_event_pk
        PRIMARY KEY (outbox_id, created_at)
) PARTITION BY RANGE (created_at);
```

Index current partition:

```sql id="6vm3ae"
CREATE INDEX outbox_event_p2026_07_pending_idx
ON app.outbox_event_p2026_07 (created_at, outbox_id)
WHERE status = 'PENDING';
```

---

## 14.2 Claim Batch Mapper

```xml id="agw11s"
<select id="claimPendingOutboxEvents" resultMap="OutboxEventResultMap">
    WITH candidate AS (
        SELECT
            outbox_id,
            created_at
        FROM app.outbox_event
        WHERE status = 'PENDING'
          AND created_at &gt;= #{fromTime}
          AND created_at &lt;  #{toTime}
        ORDER BY created_at, outbox_id
        LIMIT #{batchSize}
        FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED
    )
    UPDATE app.outbox_event e
    SET
        status = 'PROCESSING',
        retry_count = retry_count + 1,
        processing_started_at = now()
    FROM candidate c
    WHERE e.outbox_id = c.outbox_id
      AND e.created_at = c.created_at
    RETURNING
        e.outbox_id,
        e.aggregate_id,
        e.event_type,
        e.status,
        e.created_at,
        e.processed_at,
        e.retry_count,
        e.payload
</select>
```

`FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED` berguna untuk queue-like workload karena worker bisa melewati row yang terkunci worker lain, tetapi tidak cocok untuk query bisnis umum yang butuh snapshot konsisten. PostgreSQL docs juga menyebut `SKIP LOCKED` memberikan view data yang tidak konsisten namun dapat dipakai untuk menghindari lock contention pada consumer queue. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/ddl-partitioning.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 14.3 Worker Flow

```text id="rtjfdg"
1. TX1: claim PENDING → PROCESSING, commit
2. Outside DB transaction: publish/process
3. TX2: mark PROCESSED or FAILED
4. Recovery job: PROCESSING too long → PENDING/FAILED
```

Jangan tahan DB transaction selama external HTTP/Kafka call.

---

# 15. Error Handling Pattern

## 15.1 SQLSTATE Mapping

| SQLSTATE | Meaning | HTTP / App behavior |
|---|---|---|
| `23505` | unique violation | `409 Conflict` atau idempotent success |
| `23503` | foreign key violation | `400 Bad Request` / `409 Conflict` |
| `23514` | check violation | `400 Bad Request` |
| `23502` | not null violation | `400 Bad Request` |
| `40P01` | deadlock detected | retry with backoff |
| `40001` | serialization failure | retry transaction |
| lock timeout | operational/retryable | retry if idempotent |
| missing partition | operational failure | alert, likely `503`/internal error |

## 15.2 Exception Mapper

```java id="x1v3h5"
public final class PersistenceExceptionMapper {

    public RuntimeException map(PersistenceException exception) {
        String sqlState = findSqlState(exception);

        if ("23505".equals(sqlState)) {
            return new ConflictException("Duplicate resource");
        }

        if ("23503".equals(sqlState)) {
            return new BadRequestException("Referenced resource does not exist");
        }

        if ("23514".equals(sqlState) || "23502".equals(sqlState)) {
            return new BadRequestException("Invalid data");
        }

        if ("40P01".equals(sqlState) || "40001".equals(sqlState)) {
            return new ServiceUnavailableException("Temporary database concurrency failure");
        }

        return new InternalServerErrorException("Database error");
    }

    private String findSqlState(Throwable throwable) {
        Throwable current = throwable;

        while (current != null) {
            if (current instanceof SQLException sqlException) {
                return sqlException.getSQLState();
            }
            current = current.getCause();
        }

        return null;
    }
}
```

Missing partition biasanya harus masuk alert operasional karena akar masalahnya adalah future partition automation/horizon, bukan request user biasa.

# 16. Health Endpoint Pattern

## 16.1 Endpoint

```http id="7h0vzw"
GET /internal/db/partition-health/audit-event
```

## 16.2 Response

```json id="s2jmv7"
{
  "table": "app.audit_event",
  "partitionKey": "RANGE(event_time)",
  "partitionCount": 48,
  "currentPartitionExists": true,
  "futurePartitionHorizonDays": 168,
  "defaultPartitionRows": 0,
  "largestPartition": {
    "name": "audit_event_p2026_07",
    "totalSizeBytes": 91234567890,
    "estimatedRows": 41234567
  },
  "missingIndexes": [],
  "alerts": []
}
```

## 16.3 Mapper: Partition Stats

```xml id="x62qey"
<select id="listPartitionStats" resultMap="PartitionStatResultMap">
    SELECT
        child.relname AS partition_name,
        pg_total_relation_size(child.oid) AS total_size_bytes,
        pg_relation_size(child.oid) AS table_size_bytes,
        pg_indexes_size(child.oid) AS index_size_bytes,
        child.reltuples::bigint AS estimated_rows,
        stats.n_live_tup,
        stats.n_dead_tup,
        stats.last_autovacuum,
        stats.last_autoanalyze
    FROM pg_inherits i
    JOIN pg_class parent ON parent.oid = i.inhparent
    JOIN pg_class child  ON child.oid = i.inhrelid
    LEFT JOIN pg_stat_user_tables stats
      ON stats.relid = child.oid
    WHERE parent.oid = #{parentTableName}::regclass
    ORDER BY child.relname
</select>
```

## 16.4 Mapper: Default Rows

```xml id="1kz90z"
<select id="countAuditEventDefaultRows" resultType="long">
    SELECT COUNT(*)
    FROM app.audit_event_default
</select>
```

## 16.5 Service Evaluation

```java id="to7mpu"
public PartitionHealth evaluateAuditEventHealth(
        List<PartitionStat> partitions,
        long defaultRows
) {
    List<String> alerts = new ArrayList<>();

    if (defaultRows > 0) {
        alerts.add("Default partition contains rows: " + defaultRows);
    }

    boolean currentExists = hasCurrentMonthPartition(partitions, Clock.systemUTC());
    if (!currentExists) {
        alerts.add("Current month partition is missing");
    }

    long horizonDays = calculateFutureHorizonDays(partitions);
    if (horizonDays < 90) {
        alerts.add("Future partition horizon is below 90 days");
    }

    List<String> missingIndexes = findMissingRequiredIndexes(partitions);
    alerts.addAll(missingIndexes);

    return new PartitionHealth(
            "app.audit_event",
            "RANGE(event_time)",
            partitions.size(),
            currentExists,
            horizonDays,
            defaultRows,
            alerts
    );
}
```

# 17. Testcontainers Integration Tests

## 17.1 Test Goals

```text id="i5h5v6"
1. Migration creates parent + partitions
2. Insert routes to expected partition
3. Query prunes irrelevant partitions
4. Mapper always includes partition key range
5. Default partition behavior is expected
6. Required indexes exist
7. Error handling maps constraint violations
```

## 17.2 Insert Routing Test

```java id="k45zzr"
@Test
void insertAuditEventRoutesToExpectedPartition() {
    UUID eventId = UUID.randomUUID();

    auditEventMapper.insertAuditEvent(new AuditEventRecord(
            eventId,
            tenantId,
            caseId,
            OffsetDateTime.parse("2026-07-16T10:00:00Z"),
            "SMOKE_TEST",
            actorId,
            "{}",
            OffsetDateTime.now(ZoneOffset.UTC)
    ));

    String actualPartition = jdbcTemplate.queryForObject("""
        SELECT tableoid::regclass::text
        FROM app.audit_event
        WHERE event_id = ?
          AND event_time >= TIMESTAMPTZ '2026-07-01 00:00:00+00'
          AND event_time <  TIMESTAMPTZ '2026-08-01 00:00:00+00'
        """, String.class, eventId);

    assertEquals("app.audit_event_p2026_07", actualPartition);
}
```

## 17.3 EXPLAIN Pruning Test

```java id="24hddq"
@Test
void searchByTenantAndTimePrunesPartitions() {
    List<String> lines = jdbcTemplate.queryForList("""
        EXPLAIN
        SELECT *
        FROM app.audit_event
        WHERE tenant_id = ?
          AND event_time >= TIMESTAMPTZ '2026-07-01 00:00:00+00'
          AND event_time <  TIMESTAMPTZ '2026-08-01 00:00:00+00'
        """, String.class, tenantId);

    String plan = String.join("\n", lines);

    assertTrue(plan.contains("audit_event_p2026_07"));
    assertFalse(plan.contains("audit_event_p2026_06"));
    assertFalse(plan.contains("audit_event_p2026_08"));
}
```

Lebih robust: gunakan `EXPLAIN (FORMAT JSON)` dan parse relation names.

# 18. CI/CD Checks

## 18.1 Migration Pipeline

```text id="ag29hw"
Maven/GitHub CI:
1. Start PostgreSQL container
2. Apply database migrations
3. Insert test rows across partition windows
4. Verify tableoid routing
5. Verify required indexes
6. Run EXPLAIN pruning test
7. Run MyBatis mapper integration test
8. Run health endpoint service test
```

## 18.2 Required SQL Checks

```sql id="rj4586"
-- current/future partition exists
SELECT
    child.relname AS partition_name
FROM pg_inherits i
JOIN pg_class parent ON parent.oid = i.inhparent
JOIN pg_class child  ON child.oid = i.inhrelid
WHERE parent.oid = 'app.audit_event'::regclass
ORDER BY child.relname;
```

```sql id="wbp9y6"
-- required index exists
SELECT
    tablename,
    indexname
FROM pg_indexes
WHERE schemaname = 'app'
  AND tablename = 'audit_event_p2026_07';
```

# 20. Security and Safety

## 20.1 Don’t Expose Partition Names Publicly

Public API should not expose physical table names:

Buruk:

```json id="ugbwze"
{
  "partition": "audit_event_p2026_07"
}
```

Baik:

```json id="r6anx4"
{
  "from": "2026-07-01T00:00:00Z",
  "to": "2026-08-01T00:00:00Z"
}
```

Partition names are operational details.

## 20.2 Internal Endpoint Protection

`/internal/db/partition-health/*` should be protected:

```text id="zwser8"
- internal network only
- authenticated service account
- not exposed publicly
- no raw SQL input
- no arbitrary table name unless allowlisted
```

## 20.3 Allowlist Parent Tables

```java id="an61kn"
private static final Set<String> ALLOWED_PARENT_TABLES = Set.of(
        "app.audit_event",
        "app.outbox_event",
        "app.transaction_history"
);
```

Do not accept arbitrary `parentTableName` from public request and concatenate into SQL.

---

# 21. MyBatis Safety for Catalog Queries

Because PostgreSQL `regclass` cannot be bound for object identifiers in all contexts safely, use allowlist and map logical names to fixed SQL.

Good:

```java id="26zcxf"
public enum PartitionedTable {
    AUDIT_EVENT("app.audit_event", "app.audit_event_default"),
    OUTBOX_EVENT("app.outbox_event", "app.outbox_event_default");

    private final String parentTable;
    private final String defaultPartition;
}
```

Then mapper receives known string value from enum only.

Avoid:

```xml id="q1s6nf"
WHERE parent.oid = '${parentTableName}'::regclass
```

unless `parentTableName` is strictly allowlisted before mapper call. Prefer parameter binding where possible, but identifiers often require careful handling.

---

# 22. Handling Date Range Limits by Endpoint Type

| Endpoint | Max range | Reason |
|---|---:|---|
| OLTP search | 7-31 days | Low latency |
| Case timeline | 31-90 days | User investigation |
| Audit export | Async job only | Potentially large |
| Compliance report | Async/reporting path | Avoid API timeout |
| Outbox worker | current + retry windows | Queue efficiency |
| Health endpoint | no data scan | Catalog/stat views only |

Pattern:

```text id="61sk04"
Synchronous REST endpoint:
small bounded range

Large export/report:
async job + pagination/export storage
```

---

# 23. Async Export Pattern

Do not run huge audit export synchronously.

API:

```http id="kbu05c"
POST /v1/audit-event-exports
```

Request:

```json id="aa8dam"
{
  "tenantId": "00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000001",
  "from": "2026-01-01T00:00:00Z",
  "to": "2026-07-01T00:00:00Z",
  "format": "CSV"
}
```

Response:

```json id="neyy0c"
{
  "exportJobId": "5d1fcf8b-3f03-45a4-bf5e-466037a13a3a",
  "status": "ACCEPTED"
}
```

Worker breaks range by partition/month:

```text id="r6v06u"
2026-01
2026-02
2026-03
2026-04
2026-05
2026-06
```

Each chunk query still uses partition key range.

# 24. Service Patterns per Table Type

## 24.1 Audit/Event History

```text id="v2snjn"
API:
- require tenantId + from/to
- optional caseId/eventType
- keyset pagination

Mapper:
- half-open event_time range
- no date(event_time)
- ORDER BY event_time DESC, event_id DESC

Ops:
- health endpoint
- future partition alert
- default rows alert
```

## 24.2 Outbox/Inbox

```text id="62o4xe"
API:
- usually internal worker, not public search

Mapper:
- claim with status + created_at range
- FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED
- partial index WHERE status='PENDING'

Ops:
- processing lag metric
- stuck PROCESSING recovery
- partition by created_at/received_at
```

## 24.3 Transaction History

```text id="pt9vo8"
API:
- accountId + businessDate range
- async export for large range

Mapper:
- business_date >= and <
- transaction_id lookup via registry if needed

Ops:
- archive after statutory retention
- no unsafe global scan
```

## 24.4 Workflow History / Camunda-adjacent Data

```text id="ne97ky"
API:
- processInstanceId + time range
- caseId + time range
- SLA reports async

Mapper:
- started_at/completed_at range
- avoid querying all partitions by process id only unless hash partitioned

Ops:
- separate runtime vs history access pattern
```

# 25. Final Production Checklist

| Area | Checklist |
|---|---|
| API | Partition key range required |
| API | Max range enforced |
| API | Large export async |
| DTO | Uses `OffsetDateTime`/`Instant` consistently |
| Service | Normalizes time to UTC |
| Service | Uses validated request only |
| Mapper | Includes partition key predicate |
| Mapper | Uses `>= from` and `< to` |
| Mapper | Does not wrap partition key in function |
| Mapper | Avoids unsafe dynamic SQL |
| Insert | Inserts to parent table |
| Update | Does not update partition key |
| PK | Composite key handled in Java model |
| Registry | Used if global lookup/uniqueness needed |
| Error | SQLSTATE mapped to proper app error |
| Test | Insert routing tested |
| Test | EXPLAIN pruning tested |
| Test | Required indexes checked |
| Ops | Health endpoint exists |
| Ops | Future horizon alert exists |
| Ops | Default partition alert exists |
| Ops | Missing index detection exists |
| Ops | Runbook exists |

---

# 26. Common Integration Failure Modes

| Failure mode | Gejala | Root cause | Fix |
|---|---|---|---|
| Query scans all partitions | Latency spike | API allowed missing from/to | Make range required |
| Mapper ignores index | Seq scan/filter high | Function on partition key | Use raw range predicate |
| New month slow | Query suddenly Seq Scan | Partition created without index | Fix automation/index template |
| Insert fails | No partition found | Future partition missing | Create partition + alert |
| Default partition grows | Silent fallback | Missing partition/late data | Monitor and remediate |
| PK lookup awkward | Need eventTime with eventId | Composite PK | Registry table or API range |
| Duplicate not handled | 500 error | Unique violation unmapped | Map SQLSTATE 23505 |
| Worker contention | Lock waits | Bad queue claim pattern | `SKIP LOCKED` + claim transaction |
| Export timeout | REST request too large | Synchronous large range | Async export |
| Test passes but prod slow | No EXPLAIN assertion | CI only tests result correctness | Add plan tests |

---

```text id="e4elvx"
Java/Jersey API
│
├─ AuditEventResource
│  └─ requires tenantId/from/to
│
├─ AuditEventService
│  ├─ validates request
│  ├─ normalizes UTC

│
├─ AuditEventMapper

│
├─ PostgreSQL
│  ├─ audit_event parent
│  ├─ audit_event_pYYYY_MM partitions
│  ├─ audit_event_default
│  ├─ tenant_time/case_time indexes
│  └─ audit_event_registry if needed
│
├─ Test
│  ├─ Testcontainers migration test
│  ├─ insert routing test
│  ├─ EXPLAIN pruning test
│  └─ mapper SQL test
│
└─ Ops
   ├─ partition maintenance job
   ├─ health endpoint
   ├─ future horizon alert
   ├─ default partition alert
   └─ retention/archive runbook
```

# 28. Ringkasan Akhir Seri 14

PostgreSQL partitioning tidak boleh berhenti di DDL. Supaya benar-benar production-grade, partitioning harus masuk ke:

```text id="fv73tz"
1. API contract
2. DTO validation
3. MyBatis SQL shape
4. Java time handling
5. Composite key modeling
6. Error handling
7. Integration testing
8. Observability
9. Maintenance automation
10. Operational runbook
```

Rule terpenting untuk Java/Jersey/MyBatis:

```text id="94my49"
1. Require partition key filter di API.
2. Normalize date/time di service, bukan di SQL.
3. Gunakan half-open interval: >= from dan < to.
4. Jangan jadikan date range opsional di mapper.
5. Insert selalu ke parent table.
6. Jangan update partition key.
7. Hindari generic flexible search untuk table besar.
8. Gunakan registry table jika perlu global lookup/uniqueness.
9. Test bukan hanya result, tapi juga tableoid routing dan EXPLAIN pruning.
10. Expose internal health untuk future partition, default rows, missing index, dan skew.
```

**Seri 14/14 selesai. Seluruh seri PostgreSQL Partitioning selesai.**

Rekomendasi lanjutan setelah seri ini:

```text id="rbi9j2"
1. Cheatsheet PostgreSQL Partitioning - commands/config/catalog views/runbooks lengkap.
2. Cheatsheet PostgreSQL Partitioning + MyBatis Mapper Pattern - XML snippets siap pakai.
3. Production Readiness Checklist PostgreSQL Partitioning untuk GitHub PR / deployment review.
4. Simulasi end-to-end: audit_event partitioning dari DDL → Java API → MyBatis → Testcontainers → health endpoint.
```

---

**Sources:**

- [Documentation: 18: 5.12. Table Partitioning](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/ddl-partitioning.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com)
- [MyBatis 3 | Introduction](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-3/?utm_source=chatgpt.com)
- [Building RESTful Web Services with Jakarta REST](https://jakarta.ee/learn/docs/jakartaee-tutorial/current/websvcs/rest/rest.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com)

## Response:
7/16/2026, 8:29:53 AM

Berikut **Cheatsheet PostgreSQL Partitioning - commands/config/catalog views/runbooks lengkap** untuk konteks production Java/Jersey/MyBatis, PostgreSQL, Linux/Kubernetes/on-prem/AWS/Azure.

Basis utama: PostgreSQL declarative partitioning mendukung `RANGE`, `LIST`, dan `HASH`; parent partitioned table bersifat logical, data fisik ada di child partitions, dan manfaat performa utama muncul saat partition pruning bisa mengeliminasi partition yang tidak relevan. PostgreSQL juga membatasi `PRIMARY KEY`/`UNIQUE` pada partitioned table: semua kolom partition key harus ikut dalam constraint tersebut. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/ddl-partitioning.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 0. Golden Rules

```text
1. Aplikasi insert ke parent table, bukan child partition.
2. Query wajib membawa predicate partition key.
3. Untuk time partition, pakai half-open interval:
   event_time >= :fromTime AND event_time < :toTime
4. Jangan pakai date(event_time), to_char(event_time), timezone(...) di WHERE.
5. Future partition harus dibuat sebelum dibutuhkan.
6. Default partition boleh sebagai safety net, tapi harus dimonitor.
7. PK/UNIQUE pada parent partitioned table harus include partition key.
8. Index mempercepat scan di dalam partition; partitioning hanya memilih partition.
9. Retention pakai DETACH/DROP partition, bukan massive DELETE.
10. Semua DDL production harus lock-aware, idempotent, dan punya rollback/runbook.
```

---

# 1. DDL Commands

## 1.1 RANGE Partition - Time-based

```sql
CREATE TABLE app.audit_event (
    event_id    uuid        NOT NULL,
    tenant_id   uuid        NOT NULL,
    case_id     uuid        NOT NULL,
    event_time  timestamptz NOT NULL,
    event_type  text        NOT NULL,
    payload     jsonb       NOT NULL,
    created_at  timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),

    CONSTRAINT audit_event_pk
        PRIMARY KEY (event_id, event_time)
) PARTITION BY RANGE (event_time);
```

```sql
CREATE TABLE app.audit_event_p2026_07
PARTITION OF app.audit_event
FOR VALUES FROM ('2026-07-01 00:00:00+00')
             TO ('2026-08-01 00:00:00+00');

CREATE TABLE app.audit_event_p2026_08
PARTITION OF app.audit_event
FOR VALUES FROM ('2026-08-01 00:00:00+00')
             TO ('2026-09-01 00:00:00+00');
```

---

## 1.2 LIST Partition

```sql
CREATE TABLE app.regulatory_event (
    event_id           uuid        NOT NULL,
    jurisdiction_code  text        NOT NULL,
    case_id            uuid        NOT NULL,
    event_time         timestamptz NOT NULL,
    payload            jsonb       NOT NULL,

    CONSTRAINT regulatory_event_pk
        PRIMARY KEY (event_id, jurisdiction_code)
) PARTITION BY LIST (jurisdiction_code);
```

```sql
CREATE TABLE app.regulatory_event_id
PARTITION OF app.regulatory_event
FOR VALUES IN ('ID');

CREATE TABLE app.regulatory_event_sg
PARTITION OF app.regulatory_event
FOR VALUES IN ('SG');

CREATE TABLE app.regulatory_event_default
PARTITION OF app.regulatory_event
DEFAULT;
```

---

## 1.3 HASH Partition

```sql
CREATE TABLE app.case_activity (
    activity_id    uuid        NOT NULL,
    case_id        uuid        NOT NULL,
    tenant_id      uuid        NOT NULL,
    activity_type  text        NOT NULL,
    created_at     timestamptz NOT NULL,
    payload        jsonb       NOT NULL,

    CONSTRAINT case_activity_pk
        PRIMARY KEY (activity_id, case_id)
) PARTITION BY HASH (case_id);
```

```sql
CREATE TABLE app.case_activity_h00
PARTITION OF app.case_activity
FOR VALUES WITH (MODULUS 8, REMAINDER 0);

CREATE TABLE app.case_activity_h01
PARTITION OF app.case_activity
FOR VALUES WITH (MODULUS 8, REMAINDER 1);
```

---

## 1.4 DEFAULT Partition

```sql
CREATE TABLE app.audit_event_default
PARTITION OF app.audit_event
DEFAULT;
```

Gunakan untuk fallback, bukan storage permanen.

---

## 1.5 Composite Partition: RANGE + HASH

```sql
CREATE TABLE app.audit_event (
    event_id    uuid        NOT NULL,
    tenant_id   uuid        NOT NULL,
    case_id     uuid        NOT NULL,
    event_time  timestamptz NOT NULL,
    payload     jsonb       NOT NULL,

    CONSTRAINT audit_event_pk
        PRIMARY KEY (event_id, event_time, tenant_id)
) PARTITION BY RANGE (event_time);
```

```sql
CREATE TABLE app.audit_event_p2026_07
PARTITION OF app.audit_event
FOR VALUES FROM ('2026-07-01 00:00:00+00')
             TO ('2026-08-01 00:00:00+00')
PARTITION BY HASH (tenant_id);

CREATE TABLE app.audit_event_p2026_07_h00
PARTITION OF app.audit_event_p2026_07
FOR VALUES WITH (MODULUS 4, REMAINDER 0);

CREATE TABLE app.audit_event_p2026_07_h01
PARTITION OF app.audit_event_p2026_07
FOR VALUES WITH (MODULUS 4, REMAINDER 1);
```

---

# 2. Index Commands

Membuat index pada parent partitioned table akan membuat matching index pada partitions, termasuk partition baru/attached kemudian; tetapi concurrent index build langsung pada parent partitioned table belum didukung, sehingga untuk existing large table strategi aman adalah build index `CONCURRENTLY` per partition lalu create parent index metadata-only. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/ddl-partitioning.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

## 2.1 Parent-level Partitioned Index

```sql
CREATE INDEX audit_event_tenant_time_idx
ON app.audit_event (tenant_id, event_time DESC);

CREATE INDEX audit_event_case_time_idx
ON app.audit_event (case_id, event_time DESC);
```

Cocok untuk fresh table atau table yang belum besar.

---

## 2.2 Local Index per Partition

```sql
CREATE INDEX audit_event_p2026_07_tenant_time_idx
ON app.audit_event_p2026_07 (tenant_id, event_time DESC);

CREATE INDEX audit_event_p2026_07_case_time_idx
ON app.audit_event_p2026_07 (case_id, event_time DESC);
```

---

## 2.3 Concurrent Index untuk Existing Large Partition

```sql
CREATE INDEX CONCURRENTLY audit_event_p2026_07_tenant_time_idx
ON app.audit_event_p2026_07 (tenant_id, event_time DESC);
```

Catatan:

```text
CREATE INDEX CONCURRENTLY tidak boleh dijalankan di dalam transaction block.
Migration tool harus support non-transactional migration.
```

---

## 2.4 BRIN untuk Append-only Time Range

```sql
CREATE INDEX audit_event_p2026_07_event_time_brin_idx
ON app.audit_event_p2026_07
USING BRIN (event_time);
```

Dengan option:

```sql
CREATE INDEX audit_event_p2026_07_event_time_brin_idx
ON app.audit_event_p2026_07
USING BRIN (event_time)
WITH (pages_per_range = 64);
```

---

## 2.5 GIN untuk JSONB

```sql
CREATE INDEX audit_event_p2026_07_payload_gin_idx
ON app.audit_event_p2026_07
USING GIN (payload);
```

Untuk field spesifik JSONB:

```sql
CREATE INDEX audit_event_p2026_07_payload_source_idx
ON app.audit_event_p2026_07 ((payload ->> 'source'));
```

---

## 2.6 Covering Index

```sql
CREATE INDEX audit_event_p2026_07_tenant_time_cover_idx
ON app.audit_event_p2026_07 (tenant_id, event_time DESC)
INCLUDE (event_id, event_type);
```

---

## 2.7 Partial Index untuk Outbox

```sql
CREATE INDEX outbox_event_p2026_07_pending_idx
ON app.outbox_event_p2026_07 (created_at, outbox_id)
WHERE status = 'PENDING';
```

---

## 2.8 Reindex / Drop Index

```sql
REINDEX INDEX CONCURRENTLY app.audit_event_p2026_07_tenant_time_idx;
```

```sql
DROP INDEX CONCURRENTLY IF EXISTS app.audit_event_p2026_07_payload_gin_idx;
```

---

# 3. Constraint Commands

## 3.1 Valid Primary Key pada RANGE Time

```sql
CONSTRAINT audit_event_pk
PRIMARY KEY (event_id, event_time)
```

Tidak valid jika partition key `event_time` tidak ikut:

```sql
PRIMARY KEY (event_id)
```

---

## 3.2 Unique Constraint

Valid:

```sql
CONSTRAINT audit_event_external_ref_uk
UNIQUE (tenant_id, external_reference, event_time)
```

Namun semantics-nya hanya unique untuk kombinasi yang menyertakan `event_time`. Jika `external_reference` harus unique lintas semua waktu, gunakan registry table.

---

## 3.3 Registry Table untuk Global Uniqueness

```sql
CREATE TABLE app.audit_event_registry (
    event_id    uuid        NOT NULL,
    event_time  timestamptz NOT NULL,
    tenant_id   uuid        NOT NULL,
    created_at  timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),

    CONSTRAINT audit_event_registry_pk
        PRIMARY KEY (event_id)
);
```

Insert:

```sql
BEGIN;

INSERT INTO app.audit_event_registry (
    event_id,
    event_time,
    tenant_id
)
VALUES (
    :event_id,
    :event_time,
    :tenant_id
);

INSERT INTO app.audit_event (
    event_id,
    tenant_id,
    case_id,
    event_time,
    event_type,
    payload
)
VALUES (
    :event_id,
    :tenant_id,
    :case_id,
    :event_time,
    :event_type,
    CAST(:payload AS jsonb)
);

COMMIT;
```

---

## 3.4 Foreign Key ke Master Table

```sql
CREATE TABLE app.case_event (
    event_id    uuid        NOT NULL,
    case_id     uuid        NOT NULL,
    event_time  timestamptz NOT NULL,
    event_type  text        NOT NULL,
    payload     jsonb       NOT NULL,

    CONSTRAINT case_event_pk
        PRIMARY KEY (event_id, event_time),

    CONSTRAINT case_event_case_fk
        FOREIGN KEY (case_id)
        REFERENCES app.case_file (case_id)
        ON DELETE RESTRICT
) PARTITION BY RANGE (event_time);
```

Tambahkan index di referencing side:

```sql
CREATE INDEX case_event_p2026_07_case_time_idx
ON app.case_event_p2026_07 (case_id, event_time DESC);
```

PostgreSQL tidak otomatis membuat index pada kolom referencing FK; index sering dibutuhkan untuk performa FK check/delete/update. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-altertable.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 3.5 Add Constraint Production-safe

```sql
ALTER TABLE app.case_event
ADD CONSTRAINT case_event_case_fk
FOREIGN KEY (case_id)
REFERENCES app.case_file (case_id)
NOT VALID;
```

```sql
ALTER TABLE app.case_event
VALIDATE CONSTRAINT case_event_case_fk;
```

---

# 4. Attach / Detach / Drop / Retention

`DETACH PARTITION` membuat partition menjadi standalone table; dokumentasi PostgreSQL juga menyebut index yang attached ke parent index akan dilepas, trigger clone dihapus, dan FK reference bisa memengaruhi lock. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-altertable.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

## 4.1 Attach Existing Table as Partition

```sql
CREATE TABLE app.audit_event_stage_2026_06 (
    LIKE app.audit_event INCLUDING DEFAULTS INCLUDING CONSTRAINTS
);
```

```sql
INSERT INTO app.audit_event_stage_2026_06
SELECT *
FROM app.audit_event_old
WHERE event_time >= '2026-06-01 00:00:00+00'
  AND event_time <  '2026-07-01 00:00:00+00';
```

```sql
ALTER TABLE app.audit_event_stage_2026_06
ADD CONSTRAINT audit_event_stage_2026_06_bound_chk
CHECK (
    event_time >= TIMESTAMPTZ '2026-06-01 00:00:00+00'
    AND event_time < TIMESTAMPTZ '2026-07-01 00:00:00+00'
);
```

```sql
CREATE INDEX audit_event_stage_2026_06_tenant_time_idx
ON app.audit_event_stage_2026_06 (tenant_id, event_time DESC);

CREATE INDEX audit_event_stage_2026_06_case_time_idx
ON app.audit_event_stage_2026_06 (case_id, event_time DESC);

ANALYZE app.audit_event_stage_2026_06;
```

```sql
ALTER TABLE app.audit_event
ATTACH PARTITION app.audit_event_stage_2026_06
FOR VALUES FROM ('2026-06-01 00:00:00+00')
             TO ('2026-07-01 00:00:00+00');
```

`CHECK` bound yang sesuai membantu PostgreSQL menghindari/mengurangi scan validasi saat attach partition besar. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/ddl-partitioning.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 4.2 Detach Partition

```sql
ALTER TABLE app.audit_event
DETACH PARTITION app.audit_event_p2023_06;
```

## 4.3 Detach Concurrently

```sql
ALTER TABLE app.audit_event
DETACH PARTITION app.audit_event_p2023_06 CONCURRENTLY;
```

Catatan:

```text
DETACH PARTITION CONCURRENTLY tidak boleh di transaction block.
Ada restriction jika parent punya default partition.
```

---

## 4.4 Drop Old Partition

```sql
DROP TABLE app.audit_event_p2023_06;
```

Lebih baik untuk retention daripada:

```sql
DELETE FROM app.audit_event
WHERE event_time < '2023-07-01 00:00:00+00';
```

Karena bulk `DELETE` menghasilkan dead tuples dan membutuhkan vacuum; partition drop/detach jauh lebih cocok untuk lifecycle retention. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/ddl-partitioning.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 5. COPY / Bulk Load

`COPY FROM` dapat digunakan pada partitioned table; `COPY TO` untuk mengambil semua row dari partitioned relation sebaiknya memakai `COPY (SELECT * FROM table) TO ...` pada dokumentasi current. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-copy.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

## 5.1 COPY ke Parent Partitioned Table

```sql
COPY app.audit_event (
    event_id,
    tenant_id,
    case_id,
    event_time,
    event_type,
    payload
)
FROM '/data/audit_event_2026_07.csv'
WITH (FORMAT csv, HEADER true);
```

## 5.2 COPY ke Staging Table

```sql
COPY app.audit_event_stage_2026_07 (
    event_id,
    tenant_id,
    case_id,
    event_time,
    event_type,
    payload
)
FROM '/data/audit_event_2026_07.csv'
WITH (FORMAT csv, HEADER true);
```

Production recommendation:

```text
Large historical migration:
COPY → staging table → validate → CHECK bound → index → ANALYZE → ATTACH
```

---

# 6. Query / Partition Pruning Commands

## 6.1 Good Query

```sql
SELECT
    event_id,
    tenant_id,
    case_id,
    event_time,
    event_type,
    payload
FROM app.audit_event
WHERE tenant_id = :tenant_id
  AND event_time >= :from_time
  AND event_time <  :to_time
ORDER BY event_time DESC
LIMIT :limit;
```

## 6.2 Bad Query

```sql
SELECT *
FROM app.audit_event
WHERE date(event_time) = DATE '2026-07-16';
```

## 6.3 EXPLAIN

```sql
EXPLAIN
SELECT *
FROM app.audit_event
WHERE event_time >= TIMESTAMPTZ '2026-07-01 00:00:00+00'
  AND event_time <  TIMESTAMPTZ '2026-08-01 00:00:00+00';
```

## 6.4 EXPLAIN ANALYZE BUFFERS

```sql
EXPLAIN (ANALYZE, BUFFERS, VERBOSE, SETTINGS)
SELECT *
FROM app.audit_event
WHERE tenant_id = '00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000001'
  AND event_time >= TIMESTAMPTZ '2026-07-01 00:00:00+00'
  AND event_time <  TIMESTAMPTZ '2026-08-01 00:00:00+00'
ORDER BY event_time DESC
LIMIT 100;
```

`EXPLAIN ANALYZE` benar-benar menjalankan statement, sedangkan `FORMAT JSON` cocok untuk test otomatis/CI. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-explain.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

## 6.5 EXPLAIN JSON

```sql
EXPLAIN (FORMAT JSON)
SELECT *
FROM app.audit_event
WHERE event_time >= TIMESTAMPTZ '2026-07-01 00:00:00+00'
  AND event_time <  TIMESTAMPTZ '2026-08-01 00:00:00+00';
```

## 6.6 Toggle Partition Pruning

```sql
SET enable_partition_pruning = on;

EXPLAIN
SELECT COUNT(*)
FROM app.audit_event
WHERE event_time >= TIMESTAMPTZ '2026-07-01 00:00:00+00'
  AND event_time <  TIMESTAMPTZ '2026-08-01 00:00:00+00';

RESET enable_partition_pruning;
```

---

# 7. Config / Settings Cheat Sheet

## 7.1 Session Safety untuk Migration

```sql
SET lock_timeout = '5s';
SET statement_timeout = '10min';
```

## 7.2 Planner / Prepared Statement Investigation

```sql
SET plan_cache_mode = force_custom_plan;
SET plan_cache_mode = force_generic_plan;
RESET plan_cache_mode;
```

```sql
SELECT
    name,
    statement,
    parameter_types,
    generic_plans,
    custom_plans
FROM pg_prepared_statements;
```

## 7.3 Autovacuum Per Partition

Autovacuum otomatis menjalankan `VACUUM` dan `ANALYZE` saat table mengalami cukup banyak insert/update/delete; autovacuum bergantung pada statistics collection (`track_counts`). ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/routine-vacuuming.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

```sql
ALTER TABLE app.outbox_event_p2026_07
SET (
    autovacuum_vacuum_scale_factor = 0.02,
    autovacuum_analyze_scale_factor = 0.01,
    autovacuum_vacuum_cost_limit = 2000
);
```

Reset:

```sql
ALTER TABLE app.outbox_event_p2026_07
RESET (
    autovacuum_vacuum_scale_factor,
    autovacuum_analyze_scale_factor,
    autovacuum_vacuum_cost_limit
);
```

## 7.4 pg_stat_statements

`pg_stat_statements` melacak planning/execution statistics SQL yang dijalankan server. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/pgstatstatements.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

```sql
CREATE EXTENSION IF NOT EXISTS pg_stat_statements;
```

Biasanya perlu konfigurasi server:

```conf
shared_preload_libraries = 'pg_stat_statements'
```

## 7.5 auto_explain

`auto_explain` dapat log execution plan query lambat otomatis. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/auto-explain.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

```sql
LOAD 'auto_explain';

SET auto_explain.log_min_duration = '1000ms';
SET auto_explain.log_analyze = on;
SET auto_explain.log_buffers = on;
SET auto_explain.log_verbose = on;
```

Gunakan hati-hati di production karena overhead/log volume.

---

# 8. Catalog & Monitoring Views

System catalogs menyimpan metadata schema, table, column, dan internal bookkeeping; jangan update catalog manual, gunakan SQL commands. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/catalogs.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

## 8.1 List Partitions

```sql
SELECT
    parent.oid::regclass AS parent_table,
    child.oid::regclass  AS child_partition
FROM pg_inherits i
JOIN pg_class parent ON parent.oid = i.inhparent
JOIN pg_class child  ON child.oid = i.inhrelid
WHERE parent.oid = 'app.audit_event'::regclass
ORDER BY child.oid::regclass::text;
```

## 8.2 Partition Strategy & Key

```sql
SELECT
    c.oid::regclass AS table_name,
    pt.partstrat,
    pg_get_partkeydef(c.oid) AS partition_key
FROM pg_class c
JOIN pg_partitioned_table pt ON pt.partrelid = c.oid
WHERE c.oid = 'app.audit_event'::regclass;
```

```text
partstrat:
r = range
l = list
h = hash
```

## 8.3 Partition Bounds

```sql
SELECT
    c.oid::regclass AS partition_name,
    pg_get_expr(c.relpartbound, c.oid) AS partition_bound
FROM pg_class c
JOIN pg_inherits i ON i.inhrelid = c.oid
WHERE i.inhparent = 'app.audit_event'::regclass
ORDER BY c.oid::regclass::text;
```

## 8.4 Table / Index Size per Partition

```sql
SELECT
    child.oid::regclass AS partition_name,
    pg_size_pretty(pg_relation_size(child.oid)) AS table_size,
    pg_size_pretty(pg_indexes_size(child.oid)) AS index_size,
    pg_size_pretty(pg_total_relation_size(child.oid)) AS total_size,
    pg_total_relation_size(child.oid) AS total_size_bytes
FROM pg_inherits i
JOIN pg_class parent ON parent.oid = i.inhparent
JOIN pg_class child  ON child.oid = i.inhrelid
WHERE parent.oid = 'app.audit_event'::regclass
ORDER BY pg_total_relation_size(child.oid) DESC;
```

## 8.5 Stats per Partition

PostgreSQL cumulative statistics system menghitung akses table/index, row count, vacuum/analyze activity, dan aktivitas server lainnya. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/monitoring-stats.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

```sql
SELECT
    schemaname,
    relname AS partition_name,
    n_live_tup,
    n_dead_tup,
    n_tup_ins,
    n_tup_upd,
    n_tup_del,
    last_vacuum,
    last_autovacuum,
    last_analyze,
    last_autoanalyze,
    vacuum_count,
    autovacuum_count,
    analyze_count,
    autoanalyze_count
FROM pg_stat_user_tables
WHERE schemaname = 'app'
  AND relname LIKE 'audit_event_p%'
ORDER BY relname;
```

## 8.6 Dead Tuple Ratio

```sql
SELECT
    relname AS partition_name,
    n_live_tup,
    n_dead_tup,
    ROUND(
        n_dead_tup::numeric
        / NULLIF(n_live_tup + n_dead_tup, 0),
        4
    ) AS dead_tuple_ratio,
    last_autovacuum,
    last_autoanalyze
FROM pg_stat_user_tables
WHERE schemaname = 'app'
  AND relname LIKE 'audit_event_p%'
ORDER BY dead_tuple_ratio DESC NULLS LAST;
```

## 8.7 Index Usage

```sql
SELECT
    schemaname,
    relname AS partition_name,
    indexrelname AS index_name,
    idx_scan,
    idx_tup_read,
    idx_tup_fetch
FROM pg_stat_user_indexes
WHERE schemaname = 'app'
  AND relname LIKE 'audit_event_p%'
ORDER BY idx_scan ASC, relname, indexrelname;
```

## 8.8 Index Definitions

```sql
SELECT
    tablename,
    indexname,
    indexdef
FROM pg_indexes
WHERE schemaname = 'app'
  AND tablename LIKE 'audit_event_p%'
ORDER BY tablename, indexname;
```

## 8.9 Missing Required Index

```sql
WITH partitions AS (
    SELECT child.relname AS partition_name
    FROM pg_inherits i
    JOIN pg_class parent ON parent.oid = i.inhparent
    JOIN pg_class child  ON child.oid = i.inhrelid
    WHERE parent.oid = 'app.audit_event'::regclass
      AND child.relname <> 'audit_event_default'
),
indexes AS (
    SELECT tablename, indexname
    FROM pg_indexes
    WHERE schemaname = 'app'
      AND indexname LIKE '%tenant_time_idx'
)
SELECT p.partition_name
FROM partitions p
LEFT JOIN indexes i ON i.tablename = p.partition_name
WHERE i.indexname IS NULL
ORDER BY p.partition_name;
```

---

# 9. Progress / Lock Monitoring

PostgreSQL menyediakan progress views untuk operasi seperti `CREATE INDEX`, `REINDEX`, dan `VACUUM`; `pg_stat_progress_create_index` berisi satu row per backend yang sedang membangun index. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/progress-reporting.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

## 9.1 Create Index Progress

```sql
SELECT
    pid,
    datname,
    relid::regclass AS table_name,
    index_relid::regclass AS index_name,
    command,
    phase,
    lockers_total,
    lockers_done,
    blocks_total,
    blocks_done,
    tuples_total,
    tuples_done
FROM pg_stat_progress_create_index;
```

## 9.2 Vacuum Progress

```sql
SELECT
    pid,
    datname,
    relid::regclass AS table_name,
    phase,
    heap_blks_total,
    heap_blks_scanned,
    heap_blks_vacuumed,
    index_vacuum_count
FROM pg_stat_progress_vacuum;
```

## 9.3 Long Transactions

```sql
SELECT
    pid,
    usename,
    application_name,
    state,
    now() - xact_start AS xact_age,
    wait_event_type,
    wait_event,
    left(query, 500) AS query
FROM pg_stat_activity
WHERE xact_start IS NOT NULL
ORDER BY xact_age DESC;
```

## 9.4 Blocking Sessions

```sql
SELECT
    blocked.pid AS blocked_pid,
    blocked_activity.usename AS blocked_user,
    blocked_activity.application_name AS blocked_app,
    blocked_activity.query AS blocked_query,
    blocking.pid AS blocking_pid,
    blocking_activity.usename AS blocking_user,
    blocking_activity.application_name AS blocking_app,
    blocking_activity.query AS blocking_query
FROM pg_locks blocked
JOIN pg_stat_activity blocked_activity
  ON blocked_activity.pid = blocked.pid
JOIN pg_locks blocking
  ON blocking.locktype = blocked.locktype
 AND blocking.database IS NOT DISTINCT FROM blocked.database
 AND blocking.relation IS NOT DISTINCT FROM blocked.relation
 AND blocking.page IS NOT DISTINCT FROM blocked.page
 AND blocking.tuple IS NOT DISTINCT FROM blocked.tuple
 AND blocking.virtualxid IS NOT DISTINCT FROM blocked.virtualxid
 AND blocking.transactionid IS NOT DISTINCT FROM blocked.transactionid
 AND blocking.classid IS NOT DISTINCT FROM blocked.classid
 AND blocking.objid IS NOT DISTINCT FROM blocked.objid
 AND blocking.objsubid IS NOT DISTINCT FROM blocked.objsubid
 AND blocking.pid <> blocked.pid
JOIN pg_stat_activity blocking_activity
  ON blocking_activity.pid = blocking.pid
WHERE NOT blocked.granted
  AND blocking.granted;
```

---

# 10. pg_stat_statements Queries

## 10.1 Top Query by Total Time

```sql
SELECT
    calls,
    ROUND(total_exec_time::numeric, 2) AS total_exec_time_ms,
    ROUND(mean_exec_time::numeric, 2) AS mean_exec_time_ms,
    ROUND(max_exec_time::numeric, 2) AS max_exec_time_ms,
    rows,
    query
FROM pg_stat_statements
WHERE query ILIKE '%audit_event%'
ORDER BY total_exec_time DESC
LIMIT 30;
```

## 10.2 Heuristic Query Tanpa Partition Key

```sql
SELECT
    calls,
    ROUND(total_exec_time::numeric, 2) AS total_exec_time_ms,
    ROUND(mean_exec_time::numeric, 2) AS mean_exec_time_ms,
    query
FROM pg_stat_statements
WHERE query ILIKE '%audit_event%'
  AND query NOT ILIKE '%event_time%'
ORDER BY total_exec_time DESC
LIMIT 30;
```

---

# 11. Future Horizon / Default Partition Checks

## 11.1 Future Horizon by Naming Convention

```sql
WITH partitions AS (
    SELECT
        child.relname AS partition_name,
        to_date(
            substring(child.relname FROM 'audit_event_p([0-9]{4}_[0-9]{2})'),
            'YYYY_MM'
        ) AS month_start
    FROM pg_inherits i
    JOIN pg_class parent ON parent.oid = i.inhparent
    JOIN pg_class child  ON child.oid = i.inhrelid
    WHERE parent.oid = 'app.audit_event'::regclass
      AND child.relname ~ '^audit_event_p[0-9]{4}_[0-9]{2}$'
)
SELECT
    max(month_start) AS latest_partition_month,
    date_trunc('month', current_date)::date AS current_month,
    max(month_start) - date_trunc('month', current_date)::date AS horizon
FROM partitions;
```

## 11.2 Default Rows

```sql
SELECT COUNT(*) AS default_rows
FROM app.audit_event_default;
```

## 11.3 Default Min/Max

```sql
SELECT
    min(event_time) AS min_event_time,
    max(event_time) AS max_event_time,
    count(*) AS row_count
FROM app.audit_event_default;
```

## 11.4 Default Rows by Month

```sql
SELECT
    date_trunc('month', event_time) AS month_bucket,
    COUNT(*) AS row_count
FROM app.audit_event_default
GROUP BY date_trunc('month', event_time)
ORDER BY month_bucket;
```

---

# 12. Automation: pg_partman / pg_cron

`pg_partman` membantu mengelola partition set time/id-based; pg_partman memiliki background worker yang dapat menjalankan maintenance otomatis, dan dokumentasi AWS juga menunjukkan pola `run_maintenance_proc` yang dijadwalkan secara periodik. ([GitHub](https://github.com/pgpartman/pg_partman?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

## 12.1 pg_partman Setup

```sql
CREATE SCHEMA IF NOT EXISTS partman;

CREATE EXTENSION IF NOT EXISTS pg_partman
WITH SCHEMA partman;
```

Register parent, sesuaikan dengan versi pg_partman yang terpasang:

```sql
SELECT partman.create_parent(
    p_parent_table => 'app.audit_event',
    p_control      => 'event_time',
    p_type         => 'range',
    p_interval     => 'monthly',
    p_premake      => 6
);
```

# 13. Custom Automation Functions

## 13.1 Ensure Monthly Partition

```sql
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION app.ensure_audit_event_month_partition(
    p_month_start timestamptz
)
RETURNS void
LANGUAGE plpgsql
AS $$
DECLARE
    v_month_start timestamptz;
    v_month_end   timestamptz;
    v_table_name  text;
BEGIN
    v_month_start := date_trunc('month', p_month_start);
    v_month_end   := v_month_start + interval '1 month';
    v_table_name  := format('audit_event_p%s', to_char(v_month_start, 'YYYY_MM'));

    EXECUTE format(
        'CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS app.%I
         PARTITION OF app.audit_event
         FOR VALUES FROM (%L) TO (%L)',
        v_table_name,
        v_month_start,
        v_month_end
    );

    EXECUTE format(
        'CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS %I
         ON app.%I (tenant_id, event_time DESC)',
        v_table_name || '_tenant_time_idx',
        v_table_name
    );

    EXECUTE format(
        'CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS %I
         ON app.%I (case_id, event_time DESC)',
        v_table_name || '_case_time_idx',
        v_table_name
    );

    EXECUTE format('ANALYZE app.%I', v_table_name);
END;
$$;
```

## 13.2 Ensure Future Horizon

```sql
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION app.ensure_audit_event_future_partitions(
    p_months_ahead integer DEFAULT 6
)
RETURNS void
LANGUAGE plpgsql
AS $$
DECLARE
    v_i integer;
    v_month_start timestamptz;
BEGIN
    FOR v_i IN 0..p_months_ahead LOOP
        v_month_start := date_trunc('month', now()) + (v_i || ' months')::interval;
        PERFORM app.ensure_audit_event_month_partition(v_month_start);
    END LOOP;
END;
$$;
```

```sql
SELECT app.ensure_audit_event_future_partitions(6);
```

## 13.3 Advisory Lock untuk Job

```sql
SELECT pg_try_advisory_lock(hashtext('audit_event_partition_maintenance')) AS acquired;
```

# 15. Legal Hold Metadata

```sql
CREATE TABLE app.partition_retention_hold (
    table_name      text        NOT NULL,
    partition_name  text        NOT NULL,
    hold_reason     text        NOT NULL,
    hold_by         text        NOT NULL,
    hold_until      timestamptz,
    created_at      timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),

    CONSTRAINT partition_retention_hold_pk
        PRIMARY KEY (table_name, partition_name)
);
```

```sql
SELECT EXISTS (
    SELECT 1
    FROM app.partition_retention_hold
    WHERE table_name = 'audit_event'
      AND partition_name = 'audit_event_p2023_06'
      AND (hold_until IS NULL OR hold_until > now())
) AS has_active_hold;
```

# 16. Runbooks

## Runbook A - Future Partition Missing

**Gejala**

```text
ERROR: no partition of relation "audit_event" found for row
```

**Langkah**

```sql
SELECT app.ensure_audit_event_month_partition('2026-10-01 00:00:00+00');
```

```sql
SELECT
    child.oid::regclass AS child_partition
FROM pg_inherits i
JOIN pg_class parent ON parent.oid = i.inhparent
JOIN pg_class child  ON child.oid = i.inhrelid
WHERE parent.oid = 'app.audit_event'::regclass
ORDER BY child.oid::regclass::text;
```

```sql
INSERT INTO app.audit_event (
    event_id, tenant_id, case_id, event_time, event_type, payload
)
VALUES (
    gen_random_uuid(),
    gen_random_uuid(),
    gen_random_uuid(),
    '2026-10-15 10:00:00+00',
    'SMOKE_TEST',
    '{"smoke":true}'::jsonb
);

SELECT tableoid::regclass AS actual_partition
FROM app.audit_event
WHERE event_type = 'SMOKE_TEST'
ORDER BY event_time DESC
LIMIT 1;
```

**Follow-up**

```text
1. Fix scheduler/pg_partman/CronJob.
2. Add/repair future horizon alert.
3. Check default partition rows.
4. Record lifecycle event.
```

---

## Runbook B - Default Partition Contains Rows

**Diagnosis**

```sql
SELECT
    date_trunc('month', event_time) AS month_bucket,
    COUNT(*) AS row_count
FROM app.audit_event_default
GROUP BY date_trunc('month', event_time)
ORDER BY month_bucket;
```

**Remediation pattern**

```text
1. Identify missing partition.
2. Create target partition.
3. Move default rows to staging/target.
4. Validate count.
5. Delete moved rows from default.
6. ANALYZE target/default.
7. Fix automation.
```

**Move via staging**

```sql
CREATE TABLE app.audit_event_stage_2026_10 (
    LIKE app.audit_event INCLUDING DEFAULTS INCLUDING CONSTRAINTS
);

INSERT INTO app.audit_event_stage_2026_10
SELECT *
FROM app.audit_event_default
WHERE event_time >= '2026-10-01 00:00:00+00'
  AND event_time <  '2026-11-01 00:00:00+00';

DELETE FROM app.audit_event_default
WHERE event_time >= '2026-10-01 00:00:00+00'
  AND event_time <  '2026-11-01 00:00:00+00';
```

---

## Runbook C - Query Scans All Partitions

**Diagnosis**

```sql
EXPLAIN (ANALYZE, BUFFERS)
SELECT *
FROM app.audit_event
WHERE case_id = :case_id;
```

**Checklist**

```text
1. Apakah WHERE punya event_time >= dan event_time < ?
2. Apakah partition key dibungkus function?
3. Apakah MyBatis dynamic SQL membuat from/to optional?
4. Apakah parameter time dikirim sebagai string?
5. Apakah query pakai OR yang melemahkan partition predicate?
```

**Fix**

```sql
SELECT *
FROM app.audit_event
WHERE case_id = :case_id
  AND event_time >= :from_time
  AND event_time <  :to_time
ORDER BY event_time DESC
LIMIT :limit;
```

---

## Runbook D - New Partition Slow

**Gejala**

```text
Bulan baru dimulai, endpoint lambat.
EXPLAIN menunjukkan Seq Scan di partition baru.
```

**Check missing indexes**

```sql
SELECT
    tablename,
    indexname,
    indexdef
FROM pg_indexes
WHERE schemaname = 'app'
  AND tablename = 'audit_event_p2026_08';
```

**Fix**

```sql
CREATE INDEX CONCURRENTLY IF NOT EXISTS audit_event_p2026_08_tenant_time_idx
ON app.audit_event_p2026_08 (tenant_id, event_time DESC);

CREATE INDEX CONCURRENTLY IF NOT EXISTS audit_event_p2026_08_case_time_idx
ON app.audit_event_p2026_08 (case_id, event_time DESC);

ANALYZE app.audit_event_p2026_08;
```

---

## Runbook E - High Dead Tuples / Bloat

**Diagnosis**

```sql
SELECT
    relname,
    n_live_tup,
    n_dead_tup,
    ROUND(
        n_dead_tup::numeric / NULLIF(n_live_tup + n_dead_tup, 0),
        4
    ) AS dead_tuple_ratio,
    last_autovacuum,
    last_autoanalyze
FROM pg_stat_user_tables
WHERE schemaname = 'app'
  AND relname LIKE 'outbox_event_p%'
ORDER BY dead_tuple_ratio DESC NULLS LAST;
```

**Actions**

```text
1. Check long transactions.
2. Check update/delete pattern.
3. Run VACUUM ANALYZE on affected partition.
4. Tune autovacuum only if recurring.
5. Review hot partition indexes/write amplification.
```

```sql
VACUUM (VERBOSE, ANALYZE) app.outbox_event_p2026_07;
```

---

## Runbook F - Retention / Archive Old Partition

**Pre-check**

```sql
SELECT EXISTS (
    SELECT 1
    FROM app.partition_retention_hold
    WHERE table_name = 'audit_event'
      AND partition_name = 'audit_event_p2023_06'
      AND (hold_until IS NULL OR hold_until > now())
) AS has_active_hold;
```

**Detach**

```sql
ALTER TABLE app.audit_event
DETACH PARTITION app.audit_event_p2023_06;
```

**Validate**

```sql
SELECT
    COUNT(*) AS row_count,
    MIN(event_time) AS min_event_time,
    MAX(event_time) AS max_event_time,
    SUM(hashtext(event_id::text)) AS event_hash_sum
FROM app.audit_event_p2023_06;
```

**Export**

```sql
COPY (
    SELECT *
    FROM app.audit_event_p2023_06
    ORDER BY event_time, event_id
) TO '/archive/audit_event_p2023_06.csv'
WITH (FORMAT csv, HEADER true);
```

**Drop after verification**

```sql
DROP TABLE app.audit_event_p2023_06;
```

---

## Runbook G - Backfill / Migration Validation

**Old vs new count**

```sql
SELECT COUNT(*)
FROM app.audit_event_old
WHERE event_time >= '2026-07-01 00:00:00+00'
  AND event_time <  '2026-08-01 00:00:00+00';

SELECT COUNT(*)
FROM app.audit_event_new
WHERE event_time >= '2026-07-01 00:00:00+00'
  AND event_time <  '2026-08-01 00:00:00+00';
```

**Missing rows**

```sql
SELECT o.event_id, o.event_time
FROM app.audit_event_old o
LEFT JOIN app.audit_event_new n
  ON n.event_id = o.event_id
 AND n.event_time = o.event_time
WHERE o.event_time >= '2026-07-01 00:00:00+00'
  AND o.event_time <  '2026-08-01 00:00:00+00'
  AND n.event_id IS NULL
LIMIT 100;
```

**Unexpected new rows**

```sql
SELECT n.event_id, n.event_time
FROM app.audit_event_new n
LEFT JOIN app.audit_event_old o
  ON o.event_id = n.event_id
 AND o.event_time = n.event_time
WHERE n.event_time >= '2026-07-01 00:00:00+00'
  AND n.event_time <  '2026-08-01 00:00:00+00'
  AND o.event_id IS NULL
LIMIT 100;
```

---

## Runbook H - Blocking DDL / Lock Wait

**Find blockers**

```sql
SELECT
    blocked.pid AS blocked_pid,
    blocked_activity.query AS blocked_query,
    blocking.pid AS blocking_pid,
    blocking_activity.query AS blocking_query
FROM pg_locks blocked
JOIN pg_stat_activity blocked_activity
  ON blocked_activity.pid = blocked.pid
JOIN pg_locks blocking
  ON blocking.locktype = blocked.locktype
 AND blocking.database IS NOT DISTINCT FROM blocked.database
 AND blocking.relation IS NOT DISTINCT FROM blocked.relation
 AND blocking.pid <> blocked.pid
JOIN pg_stat_activity blocking_activity
  ON blocking_activity.pid = blocking.pid
WHERE NOT blocked.granted
  AND blocking.granted;
```

**Safe migration defaults**

```sql
SET lock_timeout = '5s';
SET statement_timeout = '10min';
```

**Actions**

```text
1. Jangan kill session sembarangan.
2. Identify app/DDL session.
3. Jika migration menunggu lock, biarkan fail fast via lock_timeout.
4. Reschedule heavy DDL di maintenance window.
5. Untuk index besar, gunakan CREATE INDEX CONCURRENTLY per partition.
```

---

## Runbook I - Prepared Statement / Generic Plan Regression

**Check prepared statements**

```sql
SELECT
    name,
    statement,
    parameter_types,
    generic_plans,
    custom_plans
FROM pg_prepared_statements
WHERE statement ILIKE '%audit_event%';
```

**Compare plans**

```sql
SET plan_cache_mode = force_custom_plan;

EXPLAIN (ANALYZE, BUFFERS)
EXECUTE search_audit_event(
    TIMESTAMPTZ '2026-07-01 00:00:00+00',
    TIMESTAMPTZ '2026-08-01 00:00:00+00',
    '00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000001'
);
```

```sql
SET plan_cache_mode = force_generic_plan;

EXPLAIN (ANALYZE, BUFFERS)
EXECUTE search_audit_event(
    TIMESTAMPTZ '2026-07-01 00:00:00+00',
    TIMESTAMPTZ '2026-08-01 00:00:00+00',
    '00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000001'
);
```

# 17. Alerts Checklist

| Alert | Condition |
|---|---|
| Missing current partition | current date tidak masuk partition manapun |
| Low future horizon | daily < 7 hari, monthly < 3 bulan |
| Default partition rows | `COUNT(*) > 0` |
| Missing required index | partition aktif tanpa required index |
| High dead tuple ratio | `dead_tuple_ratio > 0.20` |
| Stale analyze | partition besar belum analyze setelah load |
| Partition size skew | partition > 3x average |
| Long transaction | `xact_age` melebihi threshold |
| Blocking DDL | lock wait pada parent/partition |
| Failed lifecycle job | latest job status failed |
| Slow query without partition key | pg_stat_statements heuristic hit |

---

# 18. Production PR Review Checklist

```text
DDL
[ ] Parent table uses correct partition key.
[ ] PK/UNIQUE includes partition key.
[ ] Partition boundaries are gap-free and non-overlapping.
[ ] Default partition policy is explicit.
[ ] Future partitions exist.

Index
[ ] Required indexes exist on all active/future partitions.
[ ] Existing large partition indexes use CONCURRENTLY.
[ ] No unnecessary GIN/large covering index on hot partition.

Query
[ ] API requires partition key filter.
[ ] MyBatis SQL uses >= from and < to.
[ ] No function wrapping partition key.
[ ] EXPLAIN shows pruning.

Ops
[ ] Future horizon alert exists.
[ ] Default partition alert exists.
[ ] Retention/legal hold considered.
[ ] Rollback/runbook included.
[ ] Migration has lock_timeout.
```

# 20. One-page Command Summary

```sql
-- create parent
CREATE TABLE app.audit_event (...) PARTITION BY RANGE (event_time);

-- create partition
CREATE TABLE app.audit_event_p2026_07
PARTITION OF app.audit_event
FOR VALUES FROM ('2026-07-01') TO ('2026-08-01');

-- create default
CREATE TABLE app.audit_event_default
PARTITION OF app.audit_event DEFAULT;

-- index parent
CREATE INDEX audit_event_tenant_time_idx
ON app.audit_event (tenant_id, event_time DESC);

-- index child concurrently
CREATE INDEX CONCURRENTLY audit_event_p2026_07_tenant_time_idx
ON app.audit_event_p2026_07 (tenant_id, event_time DESC);

-- attach existing table
ALTER TABLE app.audit_event
ATTACH PARTITION app.audit_event_stage_2026_07
FOR VALUES FROM ('2026-07-01') TO ('2026-08-01');

-- detach
ALTER TABLE app.audit_event
DETACH PARTITION app.audit_event_p2023_06;

-- detach concurrently
ALTER TABLE app.audit_event
DETACH PARTITION app.audit_event_p2023_06 CONCURRENTLY;

-- drop old partition
DROP TABLE app.audit_event_p2023_06;

-- analyze
ANALYZE app.audit_event_p2026_07;

-- vacuum
VACUUM (VERBOSE, ANALYZE) app.audit_event_p2026_07;

-- explain
EXPLAIN (ANALYZE, BUFFERS)
SELECT * FROM app.audit_event
WHERE event_time >= :from_time
  AND event_time < :to_time;

-- list partitions
SELECT parent.oid::regclass, child.oid::regclass
FROM pg_inherits i
JOIN pg_class parent ON parent.oid = i.inhparent
JOIN pg_class child ON child.oid = i.inhrelid
WHERE parent.oid = 'app.audit_event'::regclass;

-- default rows
SELECT COUNT(*) FROM app.audit_event_default;
```
