## Response:
7/16/2026, 8:32:06 AM

Saya buat versi **siap pakai untuk MyBatis XML**: pattern query yang partition-friendly, insert ke parent, keyset pagination, outbox `SKIP LOCKED`, registry table, health/catalog mapper, dan anti-pattern yang harus dihindari.

# Cheatsheet PostgreSQL Partitioning + MyBatis Mapper Pattern  
## XML snippets siap pakai untuk Java/Jersey/MyBatis

Cheatsheet ini diasumsikan untuk tabel PostgreSQL partitioned by time, misalnya `app.audit_event PARTITION BY RANGE(event_time)`. Di PostgreSQL, partition pruning bekerja jika query punya predicate yang cocok dengan partition key; untuk `PRIMARY KEY`/`UNIQUE` pada partitioned table, kolom constraint harus mencakup semua partition key. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/ddl-partitioning.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

MyBatis cocok untuk pattern ini karena memang mendukung custom SQL, stored procedure, advanced mapping, dan Mapper XML. Dynamic SQL MyBatis menyediakan elemen seperti `<if>`, `<where>`, `<trim>`, dan `<foreach>` yang berguna, tetapi harus dipakai hati-hati agar predicate partition key tidak menjadi opsional. ([MyBatis](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-3/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 0. Assumsi Table

```sql
CREATE TABLE app.audit_event (
    event_id    uuid        NOT NULL,
    tenant_id   uuid        NOT NULL,
    case_id     uuid        NOT NULL,
    event_time  timestamptz NOT NULL,
    event_type  text        NOT NULL,
    actor_id    uuid,
    payload     jsonb       NOT NULL,
    created_at  timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),

    CONSTRAINT audit_event_pk
        PRIMARY KEY (event_id, event_time)
) PARTITION BY RANGE (event_time);
```

Index per partition atau parent partitioned index:

```sql
CREATE INDEX audit_event_tenant_time_idx
ON app.audit_event (tenant_id, event_time DESC, event_id DESC);

CREATE INDEX audit_event_case_time_idx
ON app.audit_event (case_id, event_time DESC, event_id DESC);

CREATE INDEX audit_event_event_type_time_idx
ON app.audit_event (event_type, event_time DESC);
```

---

# 1. Mapper XML Skeleton

```xml
<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8" ?>
<!DOCTYPE mapper
  PUBLIC "-//mybatis.org//DTD Mapper 3.0//EN"
  "https://mybatis.org/dtd/mybatis-3-mapper.dtd">

<mapper namespace="com.company.audit.AuditEventMapper">

    <!-- resultMap, sql fragments, selects/inserts/updates go here -->

</mapper>
```

---

# 2. ResultMap

```xml
<resultMap id="AuditEventResultMap" type="com.company.audit.AuditEventRecord">
    <id     property="eventId"     column="event_id"   javaType="java.util.UUID"/>
    <result property="tenantId"    column="tenant_id"  javaType="java.util.UUID"/>
    <result property="caseId"      column="case_id"    javaType="java.util.UUID"/>
    <result property="eventTime"   column="event_time" javaType="java.time.OffsetDateTime"/>
    <result property="eventType"   column="event_type"/>
    <result property="actorId"     column="actor_id"   javaType="java.util.UUID"/>
    <result property="payloadJson" column="payload"/>
    <result property="createdAt"   column="created_at" javaType="java.time.OffsetDateTime"/>
</resultMap>
```

Java record contoh:

```java
public record AuditEventRecord(
    UUID eventId,
    UUID tenantId,
    UUID caseId,
    OffsetDateTime eventTime,
    String eventType,
    UUID actorId,
    String payloadJson,
    OffsetDateTime createdAt
) {}
```

---

# 3. Reusable SQL Fragments

## 3.1 Column List

```xml
<sql id="AuditEventColumns">
    event_id,
    tenant_id,
    case_id,
    event_time,
    event_type,
    actor_id,
    payload,
    created_at
</sql>
```

## 3.2 Partition-friendly Time Predicate

```xml
<sql id="RequiredEventTimeRangePredicate">
    event_time &gt;= #{fromTime}
    AND event_time &lt;  #{toTime}
</sql>
```

## 3.3 Tenant + Time Predicate

```xml
<sql id="RequiredTenantAndTimePredicate">
    tenant_id = #{tenantId, javaType=java.util.UUID, jdbcType=OTHER}
    AND event_time &gt;= #{fromTime}
    AND event_time &lt;  #{toTime}
</sql>
```

MyBatis configuration docs mencatat bahwa beberapa driver membutuhkan JDBC type eksplisit, dan `OTHER` adalah salah satu enum umum; pada PostgreSQL, ini sering dipakai untuk UUID melalui driver JDBC. ([MyBatis](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-3/configuration.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 4. Insert ke Parent Table

Aplikasi selalu insert ke parent logical table, bukan child partition.

```xml
<insert id="insertAuditEvent" parameterType="com.company.audit.AuditEventRecord">
    INSERT INTO app.audit_event (
        event_id,
        tenant_id,
        case_id,
        event_time,
        event_type,
        actor_id,
        payload
    )
    VALUES (
        #{eventId, javaType=java.util.UUID, jdbcType=OTHER},
        #{tenantId, javaType=java.util.UUID, jdbcType=OTHER},
        #{caseId, javaType=java.util.UUID, jdbcType=OTHER},
        #{eventTime},
        #{eventType},
        #{actorId, javaType=java.util.UUID, jdbcType=OTHER},
        CAST(#{payloadJson} AS jsonb)
    )
</insert>
```

Pattern ini menjaga aplikasi tidak bergantung pada nama fisik seperti `audit_event_p2026_07`.

---

# 5. Search by Tenant + Time Range

Ini pattern utama untuk table `RANGE(event_time)`.

```xml
<select id="searchByTenantAndTime"
        parameterType="com.company.audit.AuditEventSearchParam"
        resultMap="AuditEventResultMap">
    SELECT
        <include refid="AuditEventColumns"/>
    FROM app.audit_event
    WHERE
        <include refid="RequiredTenantAndTimePredicate"/>

    <if test="eventType != null and eventType != ''">
        AND event_type = #{eventType}
    </if>

    ORDER BY event_time DESC, event_id DESC
    LIMIT #{limit}
</select>
```

Index target:

```sql
CREATE INDEX audit_event_tenant_time_id_idx
ON app.audit_event (tenant_id, event_time DESC, event_id DESC);
```

# 6. Search by Case + Time Range

```xml
<select id="searchByCaseAndTime"
        parameterType="com.company.audit.AuditEventSearchParam"
        resultMap="AuditEventResultMap">
    SELECT
        <include refid="AuditEventColumns"/>
    FROM app.audit_event
    WHERE case_id = #{caseId, javaType=java.util.UUID, jdbcType=OTHER}
      AND event_time &gt;= #{fromTime}
      AND event_time &lt;  #{toTime}

    <if test="eventType != null and eventType != ''">
        AND event_type = #{eventType}
    </if>

    ORDER BY event_time DESC, event_id DESC
    LIMIT #{limit}
</select>
```

Index target:

```sql
CREATE INDEX audit_event_case_time_id_idx
ON app.audit_event (case_id, event_time DESC, event_id DESC);
```

    FROM app.audit_event
    WHERE event_type = #{eventType}

CREATE INDEX audit_event_event_type_time_id_idx
ON app.audit_event (event_type, event_time DESC, event_id DESC);

# 8. Search with Optional Filters, but Required Partition Key

Gunakan dynamic SQL hanya untuk secondary filters. `fromTime` dan `toTime` tetap wajib dari service layer.

```xml
<select id="searchAuditEvents"
        parameterType="com.company.audit.AuditEventSearchParam"
        resultMap="AuditEventResultMap">
    SELECT
        <include refid="AuditEventColumns"/>
    FROM app.audit_event
    WHERE tenant_id = #{tenantId, javaType=java.util.UUID, jdbcType=OTHER}
      AND event_time &gt;= #{fromTime}
      AND event_time &lt;  #{toTime}

    <if test="caseId != null">
        AND case_id = #{caseId, javaType=java.util.UUID, jdbcType=OTHER}
    </if>

    <if test="eventType != null and eventType != ''">
        AND event_type = #{eventType}
    </if>

    <if test="actorId != null">
        AND actor_id = #{actorId, javaType=java.util.UUID, jdbcType=OTHER}
    </if>

    ORDER BY event_time DESC, event_id DESC
    LIMIT #{limit}
</select>
```

Anti-pattern yang harus dihindari:

```xml
<!-- Jangan jadikan event_time range opsional untuk partitioned table besar -->
<if test="fromTime != null">
    AND event_time &gt;= #{fromTime}
</if>
<if test="toTime != null">
    AND event_time &lt; #{toTime}
</if>
```

# 9. Keyset Pagination

## 9.1 First Page

```xml
<select id="searchFirstPage"
        parameterType="com.company.audit.AuditEventSearchParam"
        resultMap="AuditEventResultMap">
    SELECT
        <include refid="AuditEventColumns"/>
    FROM app.audit_event
    WHERE tenant_id = #{tenantId, javaType=java.util.UUID, jdbcType=OTHER}
      AND event_time &gt;= #{fromTime}
      AND event_time &lt;  #{toTime}
    ORDER BY event_time DESC, event_id DESC
    LIMIT #{limit}
</select>
```

## 9.2 Next Page

```xml
<select id="searchNextPage"
        parameterType="com.company.audit.AuditEventSearchParam"
        resultMap="AuditEventResultMap">
    SELECT
        <include refid="AuditEventColumns"/>
    FROM app.audit_event
    WHERE tenant_id = #{tenantId, javaType=java.util.UUID, jdbcType=OTHER}
      AND event_time &gt;= #{fromTime}
      AND event_time &lt;  #{toTime}
      AND (
          event_time &lt; #{cursorEventTime}
          OR (
              event_time = #{cursorEventTime}
              AND event_id &lt; #{cursorEventId, javaType=java.util.UUID, jdbcType=OTHER}
          )
      )
    ORDER BY event_time DESC, event_id DESC
    LIMIT #{limit}
</select>
```

Index target:

```sql
CREATE INDEX audit_event_tenant_time_id_idx
ON app.audit_event (tenant_id, event_time DESC, event_id DESC);
```

public record AuditEventKey(

# 12. Registry Table Pattern untuk Global Lookup

## 12.1 Insert Registry

```xml
<insert id="insertAuditEventRegistry"
        parameterType="com.company.audit.AuditEventRecord">
    INSERT INTO app.audit_event_registry (
        event_id,
        event_time,
        tenant_id
    )
    VALUES (
        #{eventId, javaType=java.util.UUID, jdbcType=OTHER},
        #{eventTime},
        #{tenantId, javaType=java.util.UUID, jdbcType=OTHER}
    )
</insert>
```

## 12.2 Insert Event + Registry harus satu transaksi di service

Mapper XML hanya statement; transaksi dilakukan oleh service / transaction manager.

```xml
<insert id="insertAuditEventOnly"
        parameterType="com.company.audit.AuditEventRecord">
    INSERT INTO app.audit_event (
        event_id,
        tenant_id,
        case_id,
        event_time,
        event_type,
        actor_id,
        payload
    )
    VALUES (
        #{eventId, javaType=java.util.UUID, jdbcType=OTHER},
        #{tenantId, javaType=java.util.UUID, jdbcType=OTHER},
        #{caseId, javaType=java.util.UUID, jdbcType=OTHER},
        #{eventTime},
        #{eventType},
        #{actorId, javaType=java.util.UUID, jdbcType=OTHER},
        CAST(#{payloadJson} AS jsonb)
    )
</insert>
```

## 12.3 Lookup via Registry

```xml
<select id="findByEventIdViaRegistry"
        parameterType="java.util.UUID"
        resultMap="AuditEventResultMap">
    SELECT
        e.event_id,
        e.tenant_id,
        e.case_id,
        e.event_time,
        e.event_type,
        e.actor_id,
        e.payload,
        e.created_at
    FROM app.audit_event_registry r
    JOIN app.audit_event e
      ON e.event_id = r.event_id
     AND e.event_time = r.event_time
    WHERE r.event_id = #{eventId, javaType=java.util.UUID, jdbcType=OTHER}
</select>
```

---

# 13. Batch Insert dengan `<foreach>`

MyBatis dynamic SQL menyediakan `<foreach>` untuk membangun SQL dari collection. ([MyBatis](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-3/dynamic-sql.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

```xml
<insert id="insertAuditEventsBatch">
    INSERT INTO app.audit_event (
        event_id,
        tenant_id,
        case_id,
        event_time,
        event_type,
        actor_id,
        payload
    )
    VALUES
    <foreach collection="events" item="event" separator=",">
        (
            #{event.eventId, javaType=java.util.UUID, jdbcType=OTHER},
            #{event.tenantId, javaType=java.util.UUID, jdbcType=OTHER},
            #{event.caseId, javaType=java.util.UUID, jdbcType=OTHER},
            #{event.eventTime},
            #{event.eventType},
            #{event.actorId, javaType=java.util.UUID, jdbcType=OTHER},
            CAST(#{event.payloadJson} AS jsonb)
        )
    </foreach>
</insert>
```

Practical rule:

```text
- Cocok untuk batch kecil/sedang.
- Untuk batch besar, gunakan ExecutorType.BATCH atau COPY/staging.
- Jangan membuat single SQL terlalu besar.
```

---

# 14. Update Payload, Tidak Update Partition Key

```xml
<update id="updatePayloadByKey"
        parameterType="com.company.audit.AuditEventUpdatePayloadParam">
    UPDATE app.audit_event
    SET payload = CAST(#{payloadJson} AS jsonb)
    WHERE event_id = #{eventId, javaType=java.util.UUID, jdbcType=OTHER}
      AND event_time = #{eventTime}
</update>
```

Hindari mapper seperti ini:

```xml
<!-- Anti-pattern: update partition key bisa memindahkan row antar partition -->
<update id="updateEventTimeUnsafe">
    UPDATE app.audit_event
    SET event_time = #{newEventTime}
    WHERE event_id = #{eventId}
</update>
```

PostgreSQL dapat memindahkan row ke partition lain ketika partition key di-update dan row tidak lagi cocok dengan partition asal. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/ddl-partitioning.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 15. Delete Kecil untuk Cleanup/Test

```xml
<delete id="deleteSmokeEvents">
    DELETE FROM app.audit_event
    WHERE event_type = 'SMOKE_TEST'
      AND event_time &gt;= #{fromTime}
      AND event_time &lt;  #{toTime}
</delete>
```

Untuk retention data lama, jangan mapper `DELETE` massal. Gunakan `DETACH PARTITION` / `DROP TABLE partition`.

---

# 16. Outbox Pattern: Claim with `FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED`

PostgreSQL `SKIP LOCKED` melewati row yang tidak bisa segera di-lock; dokumentasi menekankan hasilnya adalah inconsistent view sehingga tidak cocok untuk query umum, tetapi cocok untuk queue-like table dengan banyak consumer. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-select.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

## 16.1 ResultMap

```xml
<resultMap id="OutboxEventResultMap" type="com.company.outbox.OutboxEventRecord">
    <id     property="outboxId"    column="outbox_id" javaType="java.util.UUID"/>
    <result property="aggregateId" column="aggregate_id" javaType="java.util.UUID"/>
    <result property="eventType"   column="event_type"/>
    <result property="status"      column="status"/>
    <result property="createdAt"   column="created_at" javaType="java.time.OffsetDateTime"/>
    <result property="retryCount"  column="retry_count"/>
    <result property="payloadJson" column="payload"/>
</resultMap>
```

## 16.2 Claim Pending Batch

```xml
<select id="claimPendingOutboxEvents"
        parameterType="com.company.outbox.OutboxClaimParam"
        resultMap="OutboxEventResultMap">
    WITH candidate AS (
        SELECT
            outbox_id,
            created_at
        FROM app.outbox_event
        WHERE status = 'PENDING'
          AND created_at &gt;= #{fromTime}
          AND created_at &lt;  #{toTime}
        ORDER BY created_at, outbox_id
        LIMIT #{batchSize}
        FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED
    )
    UPDATE app.outbox_event e
    SET
        status = 'PROCESSING',
        retry_count = retry_count + 1,
        processing_started_at = now()
    FROM candidate c
    WHERE e.outbox_id = c.outbox_id
      AND e.created_at = c.created_at
    RETURNING
        e.outbox_id,
        e.aggregate_id,
        e.event_type,
        e.status,
        e.created_at,
        e.retry_count,
        e.payload
</select>
```

PostgreSQL `UPDATE` docs juga menunjukkan pola batch update dengan CTE dan menyebut `SKIP LOCKED` bisa ditambahkan untuk menghindari beberapa command meng-update row yang sama. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-update.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

## 16.3 Mark Processed

```xml
<update id="markOutboxProcessed"
        parameterType="com.company.outbox.OutboxCompleteParam">
    UPDATE app.outbox_event
    SET
        status = 'PROCESSED',
        processed_at = now()
    WHERE outbox_id = #{outboxId, javaType=java.util.UUID, jdbcType=OTHER}
      AND created_at = #{createdAt}
      AND status = 'PROCESSING'
</update>
```

## 16.4 Mark Failed

```xml
<update id="markOutboxFailed"
        parameterType="com.company.outbox.OutboxFailureParam">
    UPDATE app.outbox_event
    SET
        status = 'FAILED',
        last_error = #{errorMessage},
        failed_at = now()
    WHERE outbox_id = #{outboxId, javaType=java.util.UUID, jdbcType=OTHER}
      AND created_at = #{createdAt}
      AND status = 'PROCESSING'
</update>
```

## 16.5 Recover Stuck Processing

```xml
<update id="resetStuckProcessingEvents"
        parameterType="com.company.outbox.OutboxRecoveryParam">
    UPDATE app.outbox_event
    SET
        status = 'PENDING',
        processing_started_at = NULL
    WHERE status = 'PROCESSING'
      AND processing_started_at &lt; now() - CAST(#{stuckInterval} AS interval)
      AND retry_count &lt; #{maxRetry}
      AND created_at &gt;= #{fromTime}
      AND created_at &lt;  #{toTime}
</update>
```

# 17. JSONB Filter Pattern

## 17.1 JSONB containment

```xml
<select id="searchByPayloadContainment"
        parameterType="com.company.audit.AuditEventPayloadSearchParam"
        resultMap="AuditEventResultMap">
    SELECT
        <include refid="AuditEventColumns"/>
    FROM app.audit_event
    WHERE tenant_id = #{tenantId, javaType=java.util.UUID, jdbcType=OTHER}
      AND event_time &gt;= #{fromTime}
      AND event_time &lt;  #{toTime}
      AND payload @&gt; CAST(#{payloadFilterJson} AS jsonb)
    ORDER BY event_time DESC, event_id DESC
    LIMIT #{limit}
</select>
```

Index target:

```sql
CREATE INDEX audit_event_payload_gin_idx
ON app.audit_event USING GIN (payload);
```

PostgreSQL JSON functions/operators support JSON/JSONB query operations; for production, JSONB filtering should still keep partition key range in `WHERE`. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/functions-json.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

## 17.2 JSONB field equality

```xml
<select id="searchByPayloadSource"
        parameterType="com.company.audit.AuditEventPayloadSourceParam"
        resultMap="AuditEventResultMap">
    SELECT
        <include refid="AuditEventColumns"/>
    FROM app.audit_event
    WHERE tenant_id = #{tenantId, javaType=java.util.UUID, jdbcType=OTHER}
      AND event_time &gt;= #{fromTime}
      AND event_time &lt;  #{toTime}
      AND payload -&gt;&gt; 'source' = #{source}
    ORDER BY event_time DESC, event_id DESC
    LIMIT #{limit}
</select>
```

Index target:

```sql
CREATE INDEX audit_event_payload_source_idx
ON app.audit_event ((payload ->> 'source'));
```

# 18. IN List Pattern dengan `<foreach>`

```xml
<select id="searchByEventTypes"
        parameterType="com.company.audit.AuditEventTypesSearchParam"
        resultMap="AuditEventResultMap">
    SELECT
        <include refid="AuditEventColumns"/>
    FROM app.audit_event
    WHERE tenant_id = #{tenantId, javaType=java.util.UUID, jdbcType=OTHER}
      AND event_time &gt;= #{fromTime}
      AND event_time &lt;  #{toTime}
      AND event_type IN
      <foreach collection="eventTypes"
               item="eventType"
               open="("
               separator=","
               close=")">
          #{eventType}
      </foreach>
    ORDER BY event_time DESC, event_id DESC
    LIMIT #{limit}
</select>
```

Guardrail di service layer:

```text
eventTypes tidak boleh null/kosong.
Jumlah item harus dibatasi, misalnya <= 50.
```

# 19. EXPLAIN Mapper untuk CI / Diagnostics

## 19.1 Text EXPLAIN

```xml
<select id="explainSearchByTenantAndTime"
        parameterType="com.company.audit.AuditEventSearchParam"
        resultType="string">
    EXPLAIN
    SELECT
        <include refid="AuditEventColumns"/>
    FROM app.audit_event
    WHERE tenant_id = #{tenantId, javaType=java.util.UUID, jdbcType=OTHER}
      AND event_time &gt;= #{fromTime}
      AND event_time &lt;  #{toTime}
    ORDER BY event_time DESC, event_id DESC
    LIMIT #{limit}
</select>
```

## 19.2 JSON EXPLAIN

```xml
<select id="explainSearchByTenantAndTimeJson"
        parameterType="com.company.audit.AuditEventSearchParam"
        resultType="string">
    EXPLAIN (FORMAT JSON)
    SELECT
        <include refid="AuditEventColumns"/>
    FROM app.audit_event
    WHERE tenant_id = #{tenantId, javaType=java.util.UUID, jdbcType=OTHER}
      AND event_time &gt;= #{fromTime}
      AND event_time &lt;  #{toTime}
    ORDER BY event_time DESC, event_id DESC
    LIMIT #{limit}
</select>
```

PostgreSQL `EXPLAIN` dapat memakai `FORMAT JSON`; `EXPLAIN ANALYZE` menjalankan query, jadi untuk CI read-only biasanya `EXPLAIN` atau `EXPLAIN (FORMAT JSON)` lebih aman. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/ddl-partitioning.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

<select id="findActualPartitionByEvent"

        resultType="string">
    SELECT tableoid::regclass::text AS actual_partition
    FROM app.audit_event

</select>

# 21. Partition Health Mapper

Gunakan hanya untuk internal endpoint. Jangan expose `parentTableName` dari public request. Allowlist di Java.

## 21.1 List Partitions

```xml
<select id="listPartitions"
        parameterType="string"
        resultType="com.company.db.PartitionInfo">
    SELECT
        parent.oid::regclass::text AS parent_table,
        child.oid::regclass::text AS partition_table
    FROM pg_inherits i
    JOIN pg_class parent ON parent.oid = i.inhparent
    JOIN pg_class child  ON child.oid = i.inhrelid
    WHERE parent.oid = #{parentTableName}::regclass
    ORDER BY child.oid::regclass::text
</select>
```

## 21.2 Partition Strategy and Key

```xml
<select id="getPartitionKey"
        parameterType="string"
        resultType="com.company.db.PartitionKeyInfo">
    SELECT
        c.oid::regclass::text AS table_name,
        pt.partstrat AS partition_strategy,
        pg_get_partkeydef(c.oid) AS partition_key
    FROM pg_class c
    JOIN pg_partitioned_table pt ON pt.partrelid = c.oid
    WHERE c.oid = #{parentTableName}::regclass
</select>
```

## 21.3 Partition Bounds

```xml
<select id="listPartitionBounds"
        parameterType="string"
        resultType="com.company.db.PartitionBoundInfo">
    SELECT
        c.oid::regclass::text AS partition_name,
        pg_get_expr(c.relpartbound, c.oid) AS partition_bound
    FROM pg_class c
    JOIN pg_inherits i ON i.inhrelid = c.oid
    WHERE i.inhparent = #{parentTableName}::regclass
    ORDER BY c.oid::regclass::text
</select>
```

---

# 22. Partition Size / Stats Mapper

```xml
<select id="listPartitionStats"
        parameterType="string"
        resultType="com.company.db.PartitionStat">
    SELECT
        child.oid::regclass::text AS partition_name,
        pg_total_relation_size(child.oid) AS total_size_bytes,
        pg_relation_size(child.oid) AS table_size_bytes,
        pg_indexes_size(child.oid) AS index_size_bytes,
        child.reltuples::bigint AS estimated_rows,
        stats.n_live_tup,
        stats.n_dead_tup,
        stats.last_vacuum,
        stats.last_autovacuum,
        stats.last_analyze,
        stats.last_autoanalyze
    FROM pg_inherits i
    JOIN pg_class parent ON parent.oid = i.inhparent
    JOIN pg_class child  ON child.oid = i.inhrelid
    LEFT JOIN pg_stat_user_tables stats
      ON stats.relid = child.oid
    WHERE parent.oid = #{parentTableName}::regclass
    ORDER BY child.oid::regclass::text
</select>
```

PostgreSQL statistics views seperti `pg_stat_user_tables` dan `pg_stat_user_indexes` menyediakan cumulative runtime statistics untuk table/index usage dan vacuum/analyze activity. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/ddl-partitioning.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 23. Default Partition Health Mapper

Karena nama table tidak boleh sembarang disubstitusi dari user input, buat mapper spesifik per table atau gunakan allowlist.

```xml
<select id="countAuditEventDefaultRows" resultType="long">
    SELECT COUNT(*)
    FROM app.audit_event_default
</select>
```

```xml
<select id="getAuditEventDefaultRange"
        resultType="com.company.db.DefaultPartitionRange">
    SELECT
        min(event_time) AS min_event_time,
        max(event_time) AS max_event_time,
        count(*) AS row_count
    FROM app.audit_event_default
</select>
```

```xml
<select id="countAuditEventDefaultRowsByMonth"
        resultType="com.company.db.DefaultPartitionBucket">
    SELECT
        date_trunc('month', event_time) AS bucket_start,
        COUNT(*) AS row_count
    FROM app.audit_event_default
    GROUP BY date_trunc('month', event_time)
    ORDER BY bucket_start
</select>
```

---

# 24. Required Index Check Mapper

```xml
<select id="findAuditEventPartitionsMissingTenantTimeIndex"
        resultType="string">
    WITH partitions AS (
        SELECT child.relname AS partition_name
        FROM pg_inherits i
        JOIN pg_class parent ON parent.oid = i.inhparent
        JOIN pg_class child  ON child.oid = i.inhrelid
        WHERE parent.oid = 'app.audit_event'::regclass
          AND child.relname &lt;&gt; 'audit_event_default'
    ),
    indexes AS (
        SELECT tablename, indexname
        FROM pg_indexes
        WHERE schemaname = 'app'
          AND indexname LIKE '%tenant_time%'
    )
    SELECT p.partition_name
    FROM partitions p
    LEFT JOIN indexes i ON i.tablename = p.partition_name
    WHERE i.indexname IS NULL
    ORDER BY p.partition_name
</select>
```

---

# 25. pg_stat_statements Mapper

```xml
<select id="findTopAuditEventQueries"
        resultType="com.company.db.QueryStat">
    SELECT
        calls,
        total_exec_time,
        mean_exec_time,
        max_exec_time,
        rows,
        query
    FROM pg_stat_statements
    WHERE query ILIKE '%audit_event%'
    ORDER BY total_exec_time DESC
    LIMIT #{limit}
</select>
```

```xml
<select id="findAuditEventQueriesWithoutEventTimeHeuristic"
        resultType="com.company.db.QueryStat">
    SELECT
        calls,
        total_exec_time,
        mean_exec_time,
        max_exec_time,
        rows,
        query
    FROM pg_stat_statements
    WHERE query ILIKE '%audit_event%'
      AND query NOT ILIKE '%event_time%'
    ORDER BY total_exec_time DESC
    LIMIT #{limit}
</select>
```

`pg_stat_statements` perlu diaktifkan di PostgreSQL dan dibuat sebagai extension sebelum view tersedia. ([MyBatis](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-3/configuration.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 26. Migration / Maintenance Mapper

## 26.1 Ensure Future Partitions via Function

```xml
<select id="ensureAuditEventFuturePartitions" resultType="void">
    SELECT app.ensure_audit_event_future_partitions(#{monthsAhead})
</select>
```

## 26.2 Lifecycle Event Insert

```xml
<insert id="insertPartitionLifecycleEvent"
        parameterType="com.company.db.PartitionLifecycleEvent">
    INSERT INTO app.partition_lifecycle_event (
        table_name,
        partition_name,
        action,
        action_status,
        lower_bound,
        upper_bound,
        row_count,
        total_size_bytes,
        archive_location,
        executed_by,
        details
    )
    VALUES (
        #{tableName},
        #{partitionName},
        #{action},
        #{actionStatus},
        #{lowerBound},
        #{upperBound},
        #{rowCount},
        #{totalSizeBytes},
        #{archiveLocation},
        #{executedBy},
        CAST(#{detailsJson} AS jsonb)
    )
</insert>
```

---

# 27. Backfill Mapper

## 27.1 Backfill Window

```xml
<insert id="backfillAuditEventWindow"
        parameterType="com.company.audit.AuditEventBackfillParam">
    INSERT INTO app.audit_event_new (
        event_id,
        tenant_id,
        case_id,
        event_time,
        event_type,
        actor_id,
        payload,
        created_at
    )
    SELECT
        event_id,
        tenant_id,
        case_id,
        event_time,
        event_type,
        actor_id,
        payload,
        created_at
    FROM app.audit_event_old
    WHERE event_time &gt;= #{fromTime}
      AND event_time &lt;  #{toTime}
    ON CONFLICT (event_id, event_time) DO NOTHING
</insert>
```

## 27.2 Validate Old/New Count

```xml
<select id="countOldAuditEventsInWindow"
        parameterType="com.company.audit.TimeWindow"
        resultType="long">
    SELECT COUNT(*)
    FROM app.audit_event_old
    WHERE event_time &gt;= #{fromTime}
      AND event_time &lt;  #{toTime}
</select>
```

```xml
<select id="countNewAuditEventsInWindow"
        parameterType="com.company.audit.TimeWindow"
        resultType="long">
    SELECT COUNT(*)
    FROM app.audit_event_new
    WHERE event_time &gt;= #{fromTime}
      AND event_time &lt;  #{toTime}
</select>
```

## 27.3 Missing Rows

```xml
<select id="findMissingRowsAfterBackfill"
        parameterType="com.company.audit.TimeWindow"
        resultMap="AuditEventKeyResultMap">
    SELECT o.event_id, o.event_time
    FROM app.audit_event_old o
    LEFT JOIN app.audit_event_new n
      ON n.event_id = o.event_id
     AND n.event_time = o.event_time
    WHERE o.event_time &gt;= #{fromTime}
      AND o.event_time &lt;  #{toTime}
      AND n.event_id IS NULL
    LIMIT #{limit}
</select>
```

---

# 28. Mapper Anti-patterns

## 28.1 Optional Partition Key

```xml
<!-- BAD -->
<if test="fromTime != null">
    AND event_time &gt;= #{fromTime}
</if>
```

Fix: service harus mewajibkan `fromTime`/`toTime`.

---

## 28.2 Function on Partition Key

```xml
<!-- BAD -->
AND date(event_time) = #{localDate}
```

Fix:

```xml
AND event_time &gt;= #{fromTime}
AND event_time &lt;  #{toTime}
```

---

## 28.3 OR yang Melemahkan Pruning

```xml
<!-- BAD -->
WHERE event_time &gt;= #{fromTime}
   OR tenant_id = #{tenantId}
```

Fix: pecah endpoint/mapper berdasarkan access pattern.

---

## 28.4 Direct Child Partition Insert

```xml
<!-- BAD -->
INSERT INTO app.audit_event_p2026_07 (...)
VALUES (...)
```

Fix:

```xml
INSERT INTO app.audit_event (...)
VALUES (...)
```

---

## 28.5 Massive Delete via Mapper

```xml
<!-- BAD -->
<delete id="deleteOldAuditEvents">
    DELETE FROM app.audit_event
    WHERE event_time &lt; #{cutoff}
</delete>
```

Fix: retention via `DETACH PARTITION`, archive, verify, then `DROP TABLE partition`.

---

# 29. Java Parameter DTO Cheat Sheet

```java
public record AuditEventSearchParam(
    UUID tenantId,
    UUID caseId,
    String eventType,
    UUID actorId,
    OffsetDateTime fromTime,
    OffsetDateTime toTime,
    OffsetDateTime cursorEventTime,
    UUID cursorEventId,
    int limit
) {}
```

Validation rule:

```text
- tenantId required
- fromTime required
- toTime required
- toTime > fromTime
- max range, e.g. 31 days
- limit between 1 and 500
- cursorEventTime and cursorEventId must appear together
- normalize to UTC before mapper call
```

---

# 30. Production Checklist untuk Mapper PR

| Area | Checklist |
|---|---|
| Partition pruning | Mapper selalu punya predicate partition key |
| Time range | Memakai `>= from` dan `< to` |
| Dynamic SQL | Secondary filters saja yang optional |
| Pagination | Keyset, bukan large offset |
| Insert | Insert ke parent table |
| Update | Tidak update partition key |
| JSONB | JSONB filter tetap punya time range |
| Outbox | `SKIP LOCKED` hanya untuk queue-like workload |
| Index | Mapper punya matching index |
| Test | Ada EXPLAIN test |
| Test | Ada tableoid routing test |
| Ops | Health mapper tidak menerima raw public table name |

---

# 31. One-file Mapper Example Ringkas

```xml
<mapper namespace="com.company.audit.AuditEventMapper">

    <resultMap id="AuditEventResultMap" type="com.company.audit.AuditEventRecord">
        <id     property="eventId"     column="event_id"   javaType="java.util.UUID"/>
        <result property="tenantId"    column="tenant_id"  javaType="java.util.UUID"/>
        <result property="caseId"      column="case_id"    javaType="java.util.UUID"/>
        <result property="eventTime"   column="event_time" javaType="java.time.OffsetDateTime"/>
        <result property="eventType"   column="event_type"/>
        <result property="actorId"     column="actor_id"   javaType="java.util.UUID"/>
        <result property="payloadJson" column="payload"/>
        <result property="createdAt"   column="created_at" javaType="java.time.OffsetDateTime"/>
    </resultMap>

    <sql id="AuditEventColumns">
        event_id,
        tenant_id,
        case_id,
        event_time,
        event_type,
        actor_id,
        payload,
        created_at
    </sql>

    <insert id="insertAuditEvent" parameterType="com.company.audit.AuditEventRecord">
        INSERT INTO app.audit_event (
            event_id,
            tenant_id,
            case_id,
            event_time,
            event_type,
            actor_id,
            payload
        )
        VALUES (
            #{eventId, javaType=java.util.UUID, jdbcType=OTHER},
            #{tenantId, javaType=java.util.UUID, jdbcType=OTHER},
            #{caseId, javaType=java.util.UUID, jdbcType=OTHER},
            #{eventTime},
            #{eventType},
            #{actorId, javaType=java.util.UUID, jdbcType=OTHER},
            CAST(#{payloadJson} AS jsonb)
        )
    </insert>

    <select id="searchByTenantAndTime"
            parameterType="com.company.audit.AuditEventSearchParam"
            resultMap="AuditEventResultMap">
        SELECT
            <include refid="AuditEventColumns"/>
        FROM app.audit_event
        WHERE tenant_id = #{tenantId, javaType=java.util.UUID, jdbcType=OTHER}
          AND event_time &gt;= #{fromTime}
          AND event_time &lt;  #{toTime}

        <if test="caseId != null">
            AND case_id = #{caseId, javaType=java.util.UUID, jdbcType=OTHER}
        </if>

        <if test="eventType != null and eventType != ''">
            AND event_type = #{eventType}
        </if>

        <if test="cursorEventTime != null and cursorEventId != null">
            AND (
                event_time &lt; #{cursorEventTime}
                OR (
                    event_time = #{cursorEventTime}
                    AND event_id &lt; #{cursorEventId, javaType=java.util.UUID, jdbcType=OTHER}
                )
            )
        </if>

        ORDER BY event_time DESC, event_id DESC
        LIMIT #{limit}
    </select>

    <select id="findActualPartitionByEvent"
            parameterType="com.company.audit.AuditEventKey"
            resultType="string">
        SELECT tableoid::regclass::text
        FROM app.audit_event
        WHERE event_id = #{eventId, javaType=java.util.UUID, jdbcType=OTHER}
          AND event_time = #{eventTime}
    </select>

    <select id="explainSearchByTenantAndTime"
            parameterType="com.company.audit.AuditEventSearchParam"
            resultType="string">
        EXPLAIN
        SELECT
            <include refid="AuditEventColumns"/>
        FROM app.audit_event
        WHERE tenant_id = #{tenantId, javaType=java.util.UUID, jdbcType=OTHER}
          AND event_time &gt;= #{fromTime}
          AND event_time &lt;  #{toTime}
        ORDER BY event_time DESC, event_id DESC
        LIMIT #{limit}
    </select>

</mapper>
```

---

# Ringkasan

Pattern MyBatis paling aman untuk PostgreSQL partitioning:

```text
1. Buat mapper per access pattern, bukan satu mapper fleksibel untuk semua.
2. Predicate partition key tidak boleh optional.
3. Gunakan half-open time range: >= from dan < to.
4. Hindari function pada partition key.
5. Insert selalu ke parent table.
6. Pakai keyset pagination untuk history besar.
7. Gunakan registry table jika butuh global lookup by ID.
8. Untuk outbox, gunakan SKIP LOCKED hanya untuk queue-like worker.
9. Tambahkan mapper diagnostics: tableoid routing dan EXPLAIN JSON.
10. Health/catalog mapper harus internal dan allowlisted.
```

Rekomendasi lanjutan: **Production Readiness Checklist PostgreSQL Partitioning untuk GitHub PR / deployment review**.
